# Blockfall Isle: a 2.5D voxel survival sandbox

A browser game demo that mixes a fully 3D, destructible voxel world with hand-feel
2D characters drawn as smooth inked vector art, in the spirit of Octopath Traveler's "HD-2D" look and
Don't Starve's billboarded sprites.

No image or audio assets: every sprite, texture, icon and sound is generated at runtime.

## Gallery

*Captured in-engine (headless Chromium, software rendering), HUD hidden.*

*Zoomed out over the 5120 × 512 × 5120 world. Every tree, terrace and the ruined fort is a live, destructible voxel; low-detail terrain carries the view to the horizon.*

## Run it

A Vite + TypeScript web app (three.js r169, Rapier 0.21).

```sh
npm install
npm run dev        # dev server with hot reload, then open the printed URL
npm run build      # typecheck (tsc) + production build into dist/
npm run preview    # serve the production build
```

The chunk mesher is Rust compiled to WebAssembly (`crates/voxel-core`). The built module is committed
in `src/wasm/`, so the commands above work without a Rust toolchain. To rebuild it after changing the Rust code:

```sh
rustup target add wasm32-unknown-unknown
# the shared-memory (threads) build rebuilds std with atomics, which needs a nightly
rustup toolchain install nightly-2026-10-01 --profile minimal --component rust-src --target wasm32-unknown-unknown
cargo install wasm-bindgen-cli --version 0.2.104
npm run build:wasm
```

Pushes to `main` are built by GitHub Actions and deployed to GitHub Pages
(enable it once under **Settings → Pages → Source: GitHub Actions**).

## Controls

| Input | Action |
| --- | --- |
| WASD | move (camera-relative) |
| Space / Shift | jump / dash |
| Left mouse | shoot, throw, or place the selected item |
| Right mouse | mine the block under the cursor |
| 1–0 | pick a hotbar slot: pistol, shotgun, SMG, rocket, grenade, planks, bricks, TNT, turret, lantern |
| Q / E, middle-drag | rotate the camera |
| Mouse wheel | zoom, from close-up to 900 blocks up (the voxel world streams wider as you zoom out) |
| Minimap: click / drag | explore: the camera flies there and the world streams in (game pauses); WASD pans, Esc or Space returns |
| B, or double-click the minimap | world map; click a spot to explore it |
| Tab or C | crafting (time slows while it's open) |
| H | use a medkit |
| Esc | pause menu (resume, save, load, new game) |
| F5 / F9 | quick-save / quick-load |
| N | toggle minimap (click it for the world map) |
| P / O / G / V / M | pixel mode / tilt-shift / ambient occlusion / light shafts / mute |

## What's in it

**2.5D rendering** (choices informed by write-ups on HD-2D, Don't Starve, Teardown and others)
- Each material has a procedural, seamlessly tiling 96×96 painterly detail texture applied in world space: brick courses, planks, cobbles, bark, shingles, ore crystals. The atlas has wrapped gutters and a capped mip chain, so trilinear filtering never bleeds between materials.
- Sprites cast shadows from an invisible caster that always faces the sun, so the shadow keeps the character's silhouette at any camera angle. They also get an ambient-tinted emissive pass so they sit in the scene's light instead of looking pasted on.
- Game feel:
  - Hitstop on kills and blasts.
  - Trauma-based camera shake with a little roll.
  - The camera leads toward where you aim.
  - Enemies stagger and squash when hit.
  - Guns eject shell casings, and dust motes drift in sunlight.
- Voxel chunks meshed with per-vertex ambient occlusion, painterly colour patches, and drifting cloud shadows.
- Characters are procedurally drawn vector sprite sheets (Canvas2D paths with flat colours, cel shading and a heavy outer ink line, baked at 256 px per frame into mipmapped textures with coverage-preserving alpha) on camera-facing cards that lean back toward the camera. They have front, back and side views plus walk cycles, are lit by the scene, and cast real shadows.
- Held weapons are small 3D models that rotate around the 2D sprite to aim in full 3D.
- Post-processing: tilt-shift miniature blur, bloom, split-tone colour grading, vignette and grain.
- Day/night cycle with sunsets, moonlight, fireflies, lantern blocks that get real point lights, and a lantern the player carries at night.

- N8AO screen-space ambient occlusion grounds every voxel and sprite.
- Volumetric light shafts: each pixel marches its view ray through the sun's shadow map, so trees and battlements cut beams through the low haze.

**A 5120 × 512 × 5120 world**
- The world is 26 million columns and 13 billion blocks, stored as sparse 32³ chunks. A chunk that is all air (open sky) or all rock stores a single fill value instead of 32 KB, so only chunks near the surface cost memory.
- Terrain is generated in Web Workers, one 32-wide column of chunks at a time:
  - continents and a ragged coastline in an endless sea
  - plains, hills and lakes
  - ridged mountain ranges up to ~490 blocks with snowy peaks and bare rocky slopes
- Structures are placed per 192-block region by hashing the seed: ruined forts, hut villages, watchtowers and dammed reservoirs. Every builder writes through a writer clipped to the column being generated, so any column can be generated on its own and a fort spanning four columns comes out identical whichever is generated first.
- Columns generate nearest-first around the player and chunks mesh nearest-first. Jobs for chunks the player has already left are dropped. Untouched columns far away are dropped from memory and regenerate from the seed if you come back. Columns you've changed stay loaded and go into saves.
- Water simulates within 80 blocks of the player. Pathfinding, the enemy grid and the ocean's shoreline texture all cover a window that follows the player, not the whole world.

**Voxel core in shared memory**
- The Rust crate is built with WebAssembly threads, so its memory is one shared `WebAssembly.Memory`. The main thread, the generation workers and the mesh workers all run the same module on it.
- Every chunk array (blocks, decor, water) is a 32 KB slab from the Rust allocator, used from TypeScript as a `Uint8Array` view. A chunk table in the same memory records each chunk's slabs.
- Generation workers write columns straight into slabs. Mesh workers gather a chunk's 26 neighbours from the table themselves. No voxel data is ever copied or transferred between threads; only finished geometry comes back.
- Shared memory needs a cross-origin isolated page. The dev and preview servers send COOP/COEP headers. On static hosts without them (GitHub Pages), a tiny service worker (`public/coi-sw.js`) adds the headers.

**Rust/WASM chunk mesher**
- Chunk meshing is Rust compiled to WebAssembly: face culling, per-vertex AO, damage and fire shading, and crossed-card foliage. Each 32³ chunk is meshed from a padded 34³ volume gathered from its 26 neighbours, so borders and AO are seamless in all three axes.
- Greedy meshing merges coplanar faces of the same block, face, state and per-vertex AO into larger quads. A quad only grows along an axis where its AO doesn't change, so the AO shading (and the AO-aware diagonal flip) looks exactly as it did with one quad per face. Damaged or burning blocks never merge. On real terrain this cuts solid vertices by about 35% around the castle, 30% on wooded plains and 16% in mountains.
- Per-block colour variation (hash tint, painterly grass and leaf patches, the ember tint of burning blocks) is computed in the world shader from the block cell, bit-identical to the old vertex-colour version, so it doesn't stop faces from merging. Vertex colours carry only face colour, AO and damage.
- Streamed chunks are meshed in a pool of Web Workers, completely off the main thread. Chunks you edit near the player are remeshed immediately on the main thread with the same WASM module.
- Vertices use compact formats: u8 positions, i8 normals, u16 colours and u8 flags, about 15 bytes per vertex.
- `cargo test` checks that the merged mesh covers exactly the faces of the per-face mesh, with no holes or overlaps and with matching colours.

**Far view**
- Far terrain: four nested clipmap rings (4 to 32 blocks per vertex, out to ~2 km) built from the analytic heightfield in workers, flat-shaded and coloured like the voxel surface, so distant mountain ranges show beyond the meshed voxels. A per-column mask hides them wherever voxel chunks are meshed.
- The minimap (1 block per pixel) and a whole-world map (B, or double-click the minimap) are rendered in workers from the analytic terrain, with landmarks, enemies and the boss marked.

**Water**
- Water is a volume-conserving voxel simulation: it falls, spreads and settles. The ocean is an infinite source, so craters dug below sea level flood.
- The castle has a moat, and a reservoir sits behind a cobblestone dam on the highest hill. Blow the dam and it floods downhill.
- Water puts out fires, and you can wade and swim in it.
- Debris floats or sinks by material: planks, logs, crates and barrels bob on the surface and right themselves, TNT floats low, and stone, brick and metal sink. Mixed wreckage tilts toward its heavy end. Floating pieces drift on a slow current, wash ashore or become waterlogged, and splash when they hit the water.
- Inland holes dug below sea level stay dry until water actually reaches them; the ocean plane only draws over the real sea.

**Saving**
- Games are saved to IndexedDB. Only the 32³ chunks you changed are stored, as gzip-compressed voxel, decor and water arrays; everything else regenerates from the seed.
- The game autosaves at dawn and every two minutes, and the title screen offers Continue.

**Things a flat top-down game can't do**
- Rotate the camera freely around the scene.
- Height matters. You can jump onto walls, climb the watchtowers, and shoot down from battlements, while grenades and spitter acid arc over cover.
- Roofs and walls between you and the camera are cut away along a plane, with solid caps drawn on the cut. Anything still hidden shows through as a silhouette.
- In the mountains the camera tilts down into valleys and pulls in rather than sink into a slope. When terrain still hides you, a see-through circle slices the hillside off at head height and draws the cut rock as a cross-section. If the camera is buried in a cliff, the whole camera side is sliced away, dollhouse style.
- Crawler bugs climb straight up walls, and explosions launch enemies through the air.

**Physics and simulation** (Rapier 0.21 via WASM, with a built-in fallback if it fails to load)
- The terrain near the player and around every falling body is mirrored into Rapier (SIMD build) as one voxel collider per 32³ chunk. Each collider holds only the exposed shell, is built within a per-frame budget, and is kept in sync on every block edit.
- Structures that lose support become dynamic voxel rigid bodies with real mass and inertia. They pivot over whatever still touches them and topple like Angry Birds towers. On a hard impact they shatter into individual rigid bricks, and bricks that come to rest are written back into the world as rubble you can mine.
- Explosions throw intact blocks around as rigid bricks, and fast bricks hurt enemies.
- Every block can be destroyed. Bullets chip blocks, and rockets, grenades and TNT carve craters.
- Structural integrity: each material has a maximum span. Stacking blocks is free, but every horizontal or hanging link costs 1, so anything that loses its load path breaks off as a rigid chunk. Chunks tip, fall, crush whatever is underneath, then shatter into tumbling debris and rubble piles. Knock out a tower's base and it comes down; cut a tree trunk and it topples.
- TNT is primed by bullets, fire or blasts, so it chain-reacts.
- Fire spreads through wood, leaves, grass and enemies. Burnt grass turns to scorched earth, and burnt-out supports cause collapses.
- Fast-moving debris hurts enemies (Angry Birds style).

**Survival**
- A three-night arc: night falls on a countdown, each wave is bigger, and night 3 brings the **Brood Mother** boss. She lobs acid volleys, births crawlers and bulldozes walls. Kill her to win, then keep going in endless mode.
- At each dawn you pick 1 of 3 upgrades (damage, speed, blast radius, mining, turrets, magnet, supplies).
- Horde pathfinding uses Dijkstra flow fields over a 192-block window around the player. Walls cost the time it takes to break them, so zombies go through gates and gaps and only dig when that's quicker. Climbers have their own field that lets them scale walls.
- Enemies:
  - **Brutes** smash walls three times as hard.
  - **Bats** fly over your defenses.
  - Oil **barrels** explode into fire.
- Zombies batter through any walls you build. Crawlers pounce and climb. Spitters lob acid that eats blocks.
- Waves get bigger every night.
- Mine materials and loot crates and enemies (wood, stone, metal, goo), then craft ammo, rockets, grenades, building blocks, TNT, lanterns, medkits and auto-turrets.

## Code tour

| File | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `src/main.ts` | Game loop, renderer and post-processing, lighting and day cycle, camera, input, combat, crafting |
| `src/world.ts` | Sparse 32³ chunk storage, streaming (generation and meshing queues, unloading), cutaway shader, voxel raycasting, structural integrity, fire |
| `src/core.ts` | The shared-memory voxel core: creates the shared WebAssembly memory and chunk table, hands out slabs, lets workers join |
| `src/blocks.ts` | Block table and deterministic noise (no renderer dependency, so workers can use it) |
| `src/physics.ts` | Instanced small debris; fallback chunk/TNT physics when Rapier isn't available |
| `src/rigid.ts` | Rapier world: voxel terrain collider, toppling voxel bodies, rigid bricks, TNT |
| `crates/voxel-core` | Rust chunk mesher (compiled to `src/wasm/`) |
| `src/meshing.ts`, `src/meshCore.ts`, `src/meshWorker.ts` | WASM mesher glue, worker pool, compact-geometry builders |
| `src/worldgen.ts`, `src/genWorker.ts` | Deterministic column generation in workers: analytic terrain, region-hashed structure sites, clipped builders; the worker also samples far terrain, maps and shoreline |
| `src/minimap.ts` | Minimap and world map rendered in a worker from the analytic terrain, with live markers |
| `src/farTerrain.ts`, `src/terrainSample.ts` | Far-terrain LOD rings from the analytic heightfield (sampled in workers), the terrain-sampling worker pool |
| `src/water.ts` | Voxel water cellular automaton, water meshing and shader, moat and reservoir |
| `src/save.ts` | IndexedDB save/load of modified chunks, gzip-compressed |
| `src/volumetric.ts` | Volumetric light-shaft post pass (shadow-map ray march) |
| `src/flowfield.ts` | Dijkstra flow fields for horde pathing (walkers and wall-climbers) |
| `src/entities.ts` | Player, enemies and their AI, turrets, pickups, ragdoll corpses, voxel collision |
| `src/projectiles.ts` | Bullets, rockets, bouncing grenades, lobbed acid |
| `src/particles.ts` | Point-sprite particle systems (glow, smoke, chunky bits that land) |
| `src/sprites.ts`, `src/art/` | Procedural vector art: character sheets, foliage atlas, UI icons, the block detail atlas, and the texture baker (colour bleed, coverage-preserving mips) |
| `src/audio.ts` | WebAudio synthesised sound effects |
| `src/ui.ts` | HUD, hotbar, crafting panel, floating damage numbers |

### vite.config.ts
```
import { defineConfig } from 'vite';

// Cross-origin isolation: unlocks SharedArrayBuffer and WebAssembly threads, so workers can share
// chunk memory instead of copying it. require-corp (not credentialless, which Safari ignores) matches
// what coi-sw.js serves on GitHub Pages; the Google Fonts stylesheet is loaded with CORS to pass it.
// (The desktop build serves the same headers from its own protocol handler.)
const isolation = { 'Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy': 'same-origin', 'Cross-Origin-Embedder-Policy': 'require-corp' };

export default defineConfig({
  // relative base so the build works from any sub-path
  base: './',
  build: { target: 'es2022', chunkSizeWarningLimit: 6000 },
  server: { headers: isolation },
  preview: { headers: isolation },
});
```

### public/coi-sw.js
```
// Cross-origin isolation for static hosts that can't set headers (GitHub Pages): this service
// worker re-serves every response with COOP/COEP, which the shared-memory voxel core needs.
// Dev/preview servers and the desktop build send the headers themselves and never register it.
// COEP is require-corp, not credentialless: Safari (so every iOS browser) ignores credentialless
// and the page would never isolate. Cross-origin loads must therefore be CORS (see the fonts link).
self.addEventListener('install', () => self.skipWaiting());
self.addEventListener('activate', (e) => e.waitUntil(self.clients.claim()));
self.addEventListener('fetch', (e) => {
  const r = e.request;
  if (r.cache === 'only-if-cached' && r.mode !== 'same-origin') return;
  e.respondWith(fetch(r).then((res) => {
    if (res.status === 0) return res; // opaque: pass through untouched
    const h = new Headers(res.headers);
    h.set('Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy', 'same-origin');
    h.set('Cross-Origin-Embedder-Policy', 'require-corp');
    h.set('Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy', 'cross-origin');
    return new Response(res.body, { status: res.status, statusText: res.statusText, headers: h });
  }));
});
```

### src/audio.ts
```
// Tiny WebAudio synth for all sound effects (no audio files).
interface NoiseOpts { freq?: number; to?: number | null; q?: number; type?: BiquadFilterType; vol?: number; attack?: number; }
interface ToneOpts { type?: OscillatorType; to?: number | null; vol?: number; attack?: number; delay?: number; lp?: number; }

export class Audio {
  ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  master: GainNode | null = null;
  noiseBuf: AudioBuffer | null = null;
  last: Record<string, number> = {};
  muted = false;
  constructor() { this.ctx = null; this.last = {}; this.muted = false; }
  init() {
    if (this.ctx) return;
    try {
      const Ctor = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext?: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext!;
      const ctx = this.ctx = new Ctor();
      const master = this.master = ctx.createGain(); master.gain.value = 0.45;
      const comp = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
      master.connect(comp); comp.connect(ctx.destination);
      const len = ctx.sampleRate * 2;
      const noiseBuf = this.noiseBuf = ctx.createBuffer(1, len, ctx.sampleRate);
      const d = noiseBuf.getChannelData(0);
      for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
    } catch (e) { this.ctx = null; }
  }
  toggleMute(): boolean { this.muted = !this.muted; if (this.master) this.master.gain.value = this.muted ? 0 : 0.45; return this.muted; }
  gate(name: string, ms: number): boolean {
    const now = performance.now();
    if (this.last[name] && now - this.last[name] < ms) return false;
    this.last[name] = now; return true;
  }
  noise(dur: number, { freq = 1000, to = null, q = 1, type = 'lowpass', vol = 0.5, attack = 0.002 }: NoiseOpts = {}): void {
    const c = this.ctx; if (!c || !this.master || this.muted) return;
    const t = c.currentTime;
    const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = this.noiseBuf;
    src.playbackRate.value = 0.8 + Math.random() * 0.4;
    src.loop = true; // long noises (a crash is up to 2 s) start at a random offset: wrap instead of cutting off with a click
    const f = c.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = type; f.Q.value = q;
    f.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
    if (to) f.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(to, t + dur);
    const g = c.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(vol, 0.0002), t + attack); // (an exponential ramp to 0 throws)
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    src.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(this.master);
    src.start(t, Math.random()); src.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
  }
  // Low body tones (under ~200 Hz) are sines: triangle/saw/square harmonics there read as a harsh buzz.
  // lp: optional lowpass cutoff, so buzzy waveforms (saw/square) don't sound harsh or flatulent at low pitch
  tone(freq: number, dur: number, { type = 'sine', to = null, vol = 0.3, attack = 0.005, delay = 0, lp = 0 }: ToneOpts = {}): void {
    const c = this.ctx; if (!c || !this.master || this.muted) return;
    const t = c.currentTime + delay;
    const o = c.createOscillator(); o.type = type;
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
    if (to) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(to, t + dur);
    const g = c.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(vol, 0.0002), t + attack);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    if (lp) { const f = c.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.value = lp; f.Q.value = 0.5; o.connect(f); f.connect(g); } else o.connect(g);
    g.connect(this.master);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
  }
  shot(kind: string, v = 1) {
    if (!this.gate('shot' + kind, 40)) return;
    if (kind === 'shotgun') { this.noise(0.35, { freq: 3000, to: 300, vol: 0.9 * v }); this.tone(120, 0.2, { to: 50, vol: 0.5 * v }); }
    else if (kind === 'smg') { this.noise(0.09, { freq: 4000, to: 900, vol: 0.4 * v }); this.tone(180, 0.06, { to: 90, vol: 0.18 * v }); }
    else if (kind === 'rocket') { this.noise(0.6, { freq: 800, to: 3000, vol: 0.5 * v, type: 'bandpass', q: 2, attack: 0.05 }); }
    else if (kind === 'turret') { this.noise(0.07, { freq: 5000, to: 1500, vol: 0.25 * v }); }
    else { this.noise(0.15, { freq: 3500, to: 600, vol: 0.55 * v }); this.tone(220, 0.08, { to: 100, vol: 0.2 * v }); }
  }
  explosion(size = 1, v = 1) {
    if (!this.gate('boom', 60)) return;
    this.noise(1.4 * size, { freq: 1800, to: 60, vol: 1.0 * v, attack: 0.005 });
    this.tone(75, 0.8 * size, { to: 35, vol: 0.8 * v });
  }
  hit() { if (this.gate('hit', 30)) this.tone(640, 0.06, { to: 260, vol: 0.16, type: 'triangle' }); }
  thunk(v = 1) { if (this.gate('thunk', 50)) { this.noise(0.12, { freq: 900, to: 200, vol: 0.4 * v }); this.tone(160, 0.08, { to: 80, vol: 0.25 * v }); } }
  break_(v = 1) { if (this.gate('break', 35)) this.noise(0.25, { freq: 1600, to: 200, vol: 0.45 * v, type: 'bandpass', q: 0.8 }); }
  crash(v = 1) { if (this.gate('crash', 150)) { this.noise(1.0 + v, { freq: 900, to: 60, vol: 0.9 * v + 0.2 }); this.tone(70, 0.9, { to: 38, vol: 0.5 * v }); } }
  // wood/stone groaning under load: a narrow resonant scrape plus a soft falling body tone
  creak(v = 1) { if (this.gate('creak', 200)) { this.noise(0.5, { freq: 700 + Math.random() * 300, to: 450, q: 9, type: 'bandpass', vol: 0.12 + 0.18 * v, attack: 0.08 }); this.tone(180 + Math.random() * 40, 0.45, { to: 140, vol: 0.05 + 0.06 * v, attack: 0.08 }); } }
  thud() { if (this.gate('thud', 60)) this.tone(110, 0.15, { to: 55, vol: 0.35 }); }
  fuse() { if (this.gate('fuse', 100)) this.noise(0.5, { freq: 5000, vol: 0.18, type: 'highpass', attack: 0.05 }); }
  pickup() { if (this.gate('pickup', 40)) this.tone(700 + Math.random() * 200, 0.08, { to: 1400, vol: 0.14, type: 'triangle' }); }
  place() { this.tone(200, 0.07, { to: 140, vol: 0.25 }); this.noise(0.06, { freq: 1200, vol: 0.15 }); }
  craft() { this.tone(520, 0.08, { vol: 0.17, type: 'triangle' }); this.tone(780, 0.12, { vol: 0.17, type: 'triangle', delay: 0.08 }); }
  click() { this.tone(420, 0.04, { vol: 0.12, type: 'triangle' }); }
  deny() { this.tone(240, 0.1, { to: 200, vol: 0.13, type: 'triangle' }); this.tone(180, 0.12, { vol: 0.12, delay: 0.08 }); }
  jump() { this.tone(260, 0.1, { to: 420, vol: 0.08, type: 'triangle' }); }
  dash() { this.noise(0.18, { freq: 600, to: 2400, vol: 0.2, type: 'bandpass', q: 1.5 }); }
  // a punchy hit: filtered noise smack + a short falling tone (no low buzz)
  hurt() { if (this.gate('hurt', 150)) { this.noise(0.12, { freq: 1600, to: 500, q: 1.2, type: 'bandpass', vol: 0.4 }); this.tone(560, 0.13, { to: 280, vol: 0.16, type: 'triangle' }); } }
  splat() { if (this.gate('splat', 40)) this.noise(0.2, { freq: 700, to: 150, vol: 0.4 }); }
  // breathy undead moan: two vowel-like ("oh") resonances over noise and a soft sine voice. (It used a
  // low-passed sawtooth voice, which with a horde around at night was a constant harsh low buzz.)
  groan(v = 1) {
    if (!this.gate('groan', 3500)) return;
    const f = 0.9 + Math.random() * 0.2;
    this.noise(0.9, { freq: 520 * f, to: 380 * f, q: 7, type: 'bandpass', vol: 0.09 * v, attack: 0.25 });
    this.noise(0.9, { freq: 900 * f, to: 700 * f, q: 9, type: 'bandpass', vol: 0.04 * v, attack: 0.3 });
    this.tone(210 * f, 0.9, { to: 165 * f, vol: 0.035 * v, attack: 0.25 });
  }
  // insect clicks (triangle, not square: a horde of crawlers chittered every couple of seconds)
  chitter(v = 1) { if (this.gate('chitter', 600)) for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) this.tone(1800 + Math.random() * 800, 0.03, { vol: 0.06 * v, type: 'triangle', delay: i * 0.045 }); }
  spit() { this.noise(0.25, { freq: 400, to: 1500, vol: 0.25, type: 'bandpass', q: 3 }); }
  sizzle() { if (this.gate('sizzle', 150)) this.noise(0.3, { freq: 6000, vol: 0.12, type: 'highpass' }); }
  night() { this.tone(110, 2.5, { vol: 0.2, type: 'sine', attack: 0.4 }); this.tone(164, 2.5, { vol: 0.12, type: 'sine', attack: 0.6, delay: 0.3 }); this.tone(98, 3, { vol: 0.15, attack: 0.8, delay: 0.6 }); }
}
```

### src/blocks.ts
```
// Block definitions and deterministic noise. Kept free of the renderer and the World class so
// world generation can run inside workers.
import * as THREE from 'three';

export const B = {
  AIR: 0, GRASS: 1, DIRT: 2, STONE: 3, SAND: 4, BEDROCK: 5, LOG: 6, LEAVES: 7,
  PLANKS: 8, BRICK: 9, ORE: 10, CRATE: 11, TNT: 12, LANTERN: 13, COBBLE: 14, SCORCH: 15,
  ROOF: 16, METAL: 17, BARREL: 18, SNOW: 19,
} as const;

export interface BlockDef {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  top: number;
  side: number | null;
  sideTop: number | null;
  bottom: number | null;
  hp: number;
  span: number;
  flammable: number;
  burnTime: number;
  anchored: boolean;
  drop: Record<string, number> | null;
  emit: number;
  solid: boolean;
  // cached colours (filled in below)
  cTop: THREE.Color;
  cSide: THREE.Color;
  cSideTop: THREE.Color | null;
  cBottom: THREE.Color;
}

type BlockInit = Partial<Omit<BlockDef, 'id' | 'cTop' | 'cSide' | 'cSideTop' | 'cBottom'>>;

// sparse: indexed by block id
export const BLOCKS: BlockDef[] = [];
function def(id: number, o: BlockInit): void {
  BLOCKS[id] = Object.assign({
    id, name: '', top: 0xffffff, side: null, sideTop: null, bottom: null,
    hp: 3, span: 4, flammable: 0, burnTime: 6, anchored: false, drop: null, emit: 0, solid: true,
  }, o) as BlockDef;
}
def(B.AIR, { name: 'air', solid: false, hp: 0 });
def(B.GRASS, { name: 'grass', top: 0x8fc45a, side: 0x8a6643, sideTop: 0x6fa44a, bottom: 0x7a5a3a, hp: 2, anchored: true, flammable: 0.3, burnTime: 2.5 });
def(B.DIRT, { name: 'dirt', top: 0x8f6a45, hp: 2, anchored: true });
def(B.STONE, { name: 'stone', top: 0x9c9a94, side: 0x8a8781, hp: 6, anchored: true, drop: { stone: 1 } });
def(B.SAND, { name: 'sand', top: 0xe8d39a, side: 0xdcc58a, hp: 1.5, anchored: true });
def(B.BEDROCK, { name: 'bedrock', top: 0x3a3a44, hp: Infinity, anchored: true });
def(B.LOG, { name: 'log', span: 4, top: 0xb88a55, side: 0x6e4a2c, hp: 4, flammable: 0.45, burnTime: 9, drop: { wood: 2 } });
def(B.LEAVES, { name: 'leaves', span: 4, top: 0x5fa845, side: 0x4f9440, hp: 0.6, flammable: 0.7, burnTime: 2.5 });
def(B.PLANKS, { name: 'planks', span: 3, top: 0xc9955a, side: 0xb8844c, hp: 3, flammable: 0.5, burnTime: 7, drop: { wood: 1 } });
def(B.BRICK, { name: 'brick', span: 3, top: 0xb9b2a4, side: 0xa8705a, hp: 8, drop: { stone: 1 } });
def(B.ORE, { name: 'ore', top: 0x8c8a86, side: 0x8c8a86, hp: 7, anchored: true, drop: { metal: 2, stone: 1 } });
def(B.CRATE, { name: 'crate', span: 1, top: 0xd8a860, side: 0xa8743c, hp: 2, flammable: 0.4, burnTime: 4, drop: { metal: 2, wood: 1 } });
def(B.TNT, { name: 'tnt', span: 1, top: 0xeee4d0, side: 0xd8443a, hp: 1, flammable: 1, burnTime: 0.6 });
def(B.LANTERN, { name: 'lantern', span: 1, top: 0xffd27a, side: 0xffc35a, hp: 1, emit: 1, drop: { metal: 1 } });
def(B.COBBLE, { name: 'cobble', span: 3, top: 0x8e8b84, side: 0x7d7a73, hp: 5, drop: { stone: 1 } });
def(B.SCORCH, { name: 'scorched', top: 0x4a3c30, side: 0x5a4433, hp: 2, anchored: true });
def(B.ROOF, { name: 'roof', span: 3, top: 0xb04a3a, side: 0x9a3e30, hp: 2.5, flammable: 0.35, burnTime: 6, drop: { wood: 1 } });
def(B.BARREL, { name: 'barrel', span: 1, top: 0x6a3a2a, side: 0xc23a2a, hp: 2, flammable: 1, burnTime: 0.8 });
def(B.SNOW, { name: 'snow', top: 0xf2f5fa, side: 0xdfe5ee, hp: 1.5, anchored: true });
def(B.METAL, { name: 'metal', span: 6, top: 0x7a8a96, side: 0x66747e, hp: 18, drop: { metal: 2 } });

// linear-space colours cached per block / face
const C = (hex: number) => new THREE.Color(hex);
for (const b of BLOCKS) {
  if (!b) continue;
  b.cTop = C(b.top);
  b.cSide = C(b.side ?? b.top);
  b.cSideTop = b.sideTop != null ? C(b.sideTop) : null;
  b.cBottom = C(b.bottom ?? b.side ?? b.top);
}

// sea level
export const WATER_LEVEL = 48;

// ---------- deterministic noise ----------
export function hash3(x: number, y: number, z: number): number {
  let h = Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) ^ Math.imul(y | 0, 668265263) ^ Math.imul(z | 0, 1274126177);
  h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 1274126177);
  h ^= h >>> 16;
  return (h >>> 0) / 4294967295;
}
function vnoise(x: number, y: number, seed: number): number {
  const xi = Math.floor(x), yi = Math.floor(y);
  const xf = x - xi, yf = y - yi;
  const u = xf * xf * (3 - 2 * xf), v = yf * yf * (3 - 2 * yf);
  const a = hash3(xi, yi, seed), b = hash3(xi + 1, yi, seed);
  const c = hash3(xi, yi + 1, seed), d = hash3(xi + 1, yi + 1, seed);
  return a + (b - a) * u + (c - a) * v + (a - b - c + d) * u * v;
}
export function fbm(x: number, y: number, seed: number, oct = 4): number {
  let s = 0, a = 0.5, f = 1, n = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < oct; i++) { s += vnoise(x * f, y * f, seed + i * 17) * a; n += a; a *= 0.5; f *= 2; }
  return s / n;
}
// ridged multifractal (0..1): sharp crests where the noise crosses its midpoint, for mountain ranges
export function ridged(x: number, y: number, seed: number, oct = 5): number {
  let s = 0, a = 0.5, f = 1, n = 0, wgt = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < oct; i++) {
    let v = 1 - Math.abs(vnoise(x * f, y * f, seed + i * 31) * 2 - 1);
    v *= v * wgt;
    wgt = Math.min(1, v * 1.6);
    s += v * a; n += a; a *= 0.5; f *= 2.03;
  }
  return s / n;
}
```

### src/core.ts
```
// The voxel core in shared memory. The Rust crate (crates/voxel-core) is compiled with threads, so
// its linear memory is a shared WebAssembly.Memory. The main thread creates the memory and the
// chunk table (Store); every worker instantiates the same module on the same memory. Chunk arrays
// (blocks, decor, water) are 32 KB slabs from the Rust allocator, used from JS as Uint8Array views,
// so workers generate columns straight into them and the mesher gathers neighbourhoods from them:
// no voxel data is copied or transferred between threads.
// Kept free of three.js / world imports so worker bundles stay small.
import init, { Store, slab_alloc, slab_free } from './wasm/voxel_core';

export const SLAB = 32 * 32 * 32;
/** chunk-table fill value of a chunk that isn't loaded */
export const ABSENT = 0xff;
const PAGES_INITIAL = 1024, PAGES_MAX = 32768; // 64 MB, growing on demand up to 2 GB

/** what a worker needs to join the core (structured-cloneable; the memory is shared, not copied) */
export interface CoreHandle {
  module: WebAssembly.Module;
  memory: WebAssembly.Memory;
  blk: number; dec: number; fill: number; // chunk table addresses
}

export class VoxelCore {
  readonly handle: CoreHandle;
  // the main thread's chunk table; held for the whole session, because wasm-bindgen frees a Rust
  // object once its JS wrapper is garbage-collected (and the table is written through raw views)
  store: Store | null = null;
  private constructor(handle: CoreHandle) { this.handle = handle; }

  /** main thread: create the shared memory, instantiate the module and allocate the chunk table */
  static async create(): Promise<VoxelCore> {
    const memory = new WebAssembly.Memory({ initial: PAGES_INITIAL, maximum: PAGES_MAX, shared: true });
    await init({ memory });
    const module = (init as unknown as { __wbindgen_wasm_module: WebAssembly.Module }).__wbindgen_wasm_module;
    const store = new Store();
    const core = new VoxelCore({ module, memory, blk: store.blk_ptr(), dec: store.dec_ptr(), fill: store.fill_ptr() });
    core.store = store;
    return core;
  }

  /** worker: instantiate the module on the main thread's memory */
  static async attach(handle: CoreHandle): Promise<VoxelCore> {
    await init({ module_or_path: handle.module, memory: handle.memory });
    return new VoxelCore(handle);
  }

  get memory(): WebAssembly.Memory { return this.handle.memory; }

  /** a zeroed slab as a view (its byteOffset is its address in the shared memory) */
  alloc(): Uint8Array {
    const ptr = slab_alloc();
    // out of memory (the 2 GB maximum is reached): a view at address 0 would silently scribble over
    // the module's own static data, so fail loudly instead
    if (!ptr) throw new Error('voxel core: out of slab memory');
    // memory.buffer is re-read every time: the allocation may just have grown the memory
    return new Uint8Array(this.handle.memory.buffer, ptr, SLAB);
  }
  free(a: Uint8Array | null): void { if (a) slab_free(a.byteOffset); }

  /** views of the chunk table (main thread writes them; workers and the mesher only read) */
  tables(n: number): { blk: Uint32Array; dec: Uint32Array; fill: Uint8Array } {
    const buf = this.handle.memory.buffer, h = this.handle;
    return { blk: new Uint32Array(buf, h.blk, n), dec: new Uint32Array(buf, h.dec, n), fill: new Uint8Array(buf, h.fill, n) };
  }
}
```

### src/entities.ts
```
// Billboarded pixel-art actors living in the 3D voxel world.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { B, BLOCKS, WATER_LEVEL } from './world';
import { makeBlobTexture } from './sprites';
import { FlowField } from './flowfield';
import type { Game } from './main';

const G = 25;
const FALL_SAFE_V = 17; // landing faster than this hurts (a ~6 block drop)
const TERMINAL_V = 55; // falling speed cap (a 400-block plunge shouldn't tunnel or take forever to resolve)
const GRID_CELL = 2, GRID_SLACK = 0.6; // enemy spatial hash (cell size, extra reach for movement within a frame)
const GRID_SPAN = 192; // grid cells per side, centred on the player (enemies further out share the border cells)
// by day the dead only notice the player this close (or once hurt), and lose interest past AGGRO_LOSE;
// at night the whole horde homes in (without this, crawlers spawned 50+ blocks out reached a new
// player in ~9 s and killed one standing at spawn within 16 s of pressing New game)
const AGGRO_DAY = 26, AGGRO_LOSE = 44, AGGRO_DY = 16;
const _v = new THREE.Vector3(), _w = new THREE.Vector3(), _a = new THREE.Vector3(), _b = new THREE.Vector3();
let blobTex: THREE.Texture | null = null;
const planeCache: Record<string, THREE.PlaneGeometry> = {};
function spritePlane(w: number, h: number): THREE.PlaneGeometry {
  const k = w + 'x' + h;
  if (!planeCache[k]) { const g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h); g.translate(0, h / 2, 0); planeCache[k] = g; }
  return planeCache[k];
}

export interface EntityOpts {
  r?: number; h?: number; hp?: number; kind: string;
  tex?: THREE.Texture; sw?: number; sh?: number; lean?: number;
}

type Drops = Record<string, [number, number]>;
type Vec3Tuple = [number, number, number];

export interface Sheets {
  player: THREE.Texture;
  zombies: THREE.Texture[];
  crawler: THREE.Texture;
  brute: THREE.Texture;
  bat: THREE.Texture;
  spitter: THREE.Texture;
}

export interface InputState {
  keys: Record<string, boolean | undefined>;
  pressed: Record<string, boolean | undefined>;
}

export interface Pickup { m: THREE.Mesh; res: string; v: THREE.Vector3; t: number; }
export interface Corpse { e: Entity; v: THREE.Vector3; t: number; spinV: number; }

export class Entity {
  game: Game;
  pos: THREE.Vector3;
  vel: THREE.Vector3;
  r: number; h: number;
  hp: number; maxHp: number;
  onGround: boolean; dead: boolean;
  facing: THREE.Vector2;
  animT: number; walking: boolean;
  flash: number; burn: number; inWater: boolean;
  blockedH: boolean;
  kind: string;
  hitSquash = 0;
  stagger = 0;
  burnTick?: number;
  sideOnly?: boolean;
  animSpeed?: number;
  spin?: number;
  squash?: number;
  lean = 0;
  tex!: THREE.Texture;
  mat!: THREE.MeshLambertMaterial;
  mesh!: THREE.Mesh<THREE.PlaneGeometry, THREE.MeshLambertMaterial>;
  caster!: THREE.Mesh<THREE.PlaneGeometry, THREE.MeshBasicMaterial>;
  xmat!: THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
  xray!: THREE.Mesh<THREE.PlaneGeometry, THREE.MeshBasicMaterial>;
  blob!: THREE.Mesh<THREE.PlaneGeometry, THREE.MeshBasicMaterial>;

  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number, o: EntityOpts) {
    this.game = game;
    this.pos = new THREE.Vector3(x, y, z);
    this.vel = new THREE.Vector3();
    this.r = o.r ?? 0.35; this.h = o.h ?? 1.8;
    this.maxHp = this.hp = o.hp ?? 100;
    this.onGround = false; this.dead = false;
    this.facing = new THREE.Vector2(0, 1);
    this.animT = Math.random() * 10; this.walking = false;
    this.flash = 0; this.burn = 0; this.inWater = false;
    this.blockedH = false;
    this.kind = o.kind;
    if (o.tex) this.makeSprite(o.tex, o.sw ?? 2.3, o.sh ?? 2.3, o.lean ?? 0.45);
  }

  makeSprite(tex: THREE.Texture, w: number, h: number, lean: number) {
    const g = this.game;
    // per-entity clone for its own frame offset; it shares the sheet's image (one GPU upload per sheet).
    // Bump only the clone's version: needsUpdate would also bump the shared source and re-upload the
    // whole high-res sheet and its mips on every spawn.
    this.tex = tex.clone(); this.tex.version++;
    this.tex.repeat.set(1 / 4, 1 / 3);
    this.lean = lean;
    const geo = spritePlane(w, h);
    // lit by the scene, plus an ambient-tinted emissive copy of itself so it never goes muddy
    this.mat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ map: this.tex, emissiveMap: this.tex, alphaTest: 0.5, side: THREE.DoubleSide, emissive: 0x000000 });
    this.mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, this.mat);
    this.mesh.castShadow = false;
    const depth = new THREE.MeshDepthMaterial({ depthPacking: THREE.RGBADepthPacking, map: this.tex, alphaTest: 0.5 });
    this.mesh.customDepthMaterial = depth;
    // invisible shadow caster that always faces the sun, so the shadow keeps the sprite's silhouette
    this.caster = new THREE.Mesh(geo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ colorWrite: false, depthWrite: false, map: this.tex, alphaTest: 0.5, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    this.caster.castShadow = true;
    this.caster.customDepthMaterial = depth;
    g.scene.add(this.caster);
    this.mesh.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
    g.scene.add(this.mesh);
    // silhouette visible only where the sprite is hidden behind geometry
    this.xmat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: this.tex, alphaTest: 0.5, color: this.kind === 'player' ? 0x7fe0ff : 0xff6a5a, transparent: true, opacity: 0.45, depthWrite: false, depthFunc: THREE.GreaterDepth });
    this.xray = new THREE.Mesh(geo, this.xmat);
    this.xray.renderOrder = 20;
    this.xray.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
    g.scene.add(this.xray);
    if (!blobTex) blobTex = makeBlobTexture();
    this.blob = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: blobTex, transparent: true, depthWrite: false }));
    this.blob.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    this.blob.scale.setScalar(this.r * 3);
    this.blob.renderOrder = 2;
    g.scene.add(this.blob);
  }

  removeSprite() {
    const s = this.game.scene;
    if (this.mesh) { s.remove(this.mesh); s.remove(this.xray); s.remove(this.blob); s.remove(this.caster); this.caster.material.dispose(); this.mat.dispose(); this.xmat.dispose(); this.blob.material.dispose(); this.blob.geometry.dispose(); this.mesh.customDepthMaterial!.dispose(); this.tex.dispose(); }
  }

  // ---------- voxel collision ----------
  overlaps(): boolean {
    const w = this.game.world, p = this.pos, r = this.r - 0.001;
    const x0 = Math.floor(p.x - r), x1 = Math.floor(p.x + r);
    const y0 = Math.floor(p.y + 0.001), y1 = Math.floor(p.y + this.h - 0.001);
    const z0 = Math.floor(p.z - r), z1 = Math.floor(p.z + r);
    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) for (let z = z0; z <= z1; z++) for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) if (w.get(x, y, z) !== B.AIR) return true;
    return false;
  }
  moveAxis(axis: 'x' | 'y' | 'z', amt: number): boolean {
    if (amt === 0) return false;
    const steps = Math.ceil(Math.abs(amt) / 0.3);
    const da = amt / steps;
    for (let s = 0; s < steps; s++) {
      const prev = this.pos[axis];
      this.pos[axis] += da;
      if (this.overlaps()) {
        if (axis === 'y') {
          if (da < 0) this.pos.y = Math.floor(this.pos.y) + 1;
          else this.pos.y = Math.floor(this.pos.y + this.h) - this.h - 0.001;
        } else {
          const ext = this.r;
          if (da > 0) this.pos[axis] = Math.floor(this.pos[axis] + ext) - ext - 0.001;
          else this.pos[axis] = Math.floor(this.pos[axis] - ext) + 1 + ext + 0.001;
        }
        if (this.overlaps()) this.pos[axis] = prev;
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  physics(dt: number, gravity = G) {
    const w = this.game.world;
    // columns stream in asynchronously: an entity over one that isn't generated yet would fall
    // straight through it, so it waits (frozen in place) until the terrain under it exists
    if (!w.isGenerated(Math.floor(this.pos.x), Math.floor(this.pos.z))) { this.vel.set(0, 0, 0); this.onGround = true; return; }
    const px = this.pos.x, pz = this.pos.z;
    // pop out if a block ended up inside us (placed, rubble, spawn)
    for (let i = 0; i < 24 && this.overlaps(); i++) this.pos.y = Math.floor(this.pos.y) + 1;
    this.inWater = (this.pos.y + 0.4 < WATER_LEVEL && !w.solidAt(this.pos.x, this.pos.y + 0.5, this.pos.z))
      || this.game.waterSim.levelAt(Math.floor(this.pos.x), Math.floor(this.pos.y + 0.4), Math.floor(this.pos.z)) >= 4;
    if (this.inWater) {
      this.vel.y = Math.max(this.vel.y - gravity * 0.25 * dt, -3);
      if (this.pos.y + 0.7 < WATER_LEVEL || this.game.waterSim.levelAt(Math.floor(this.pos.x), Math.floor(this.pos.y + 0.7), Math.floor(this.pos.z)) >= 4) this.vel.y += 12 * dt; // buoyancy
      if (this.burn > 0) this.burn = 0;
    } else this.vel.y = Math.max(this.vel.y - gravity * dt, -TERMINAL_V);
    this.blockedH = false;
    if (this.moveAxis('x', this.vel.x * dt)) { this.blockedH = true; this.vel.x *= this.onGround ? 0 : 0.2; }
    if (this.moveAxis('z', this.vel.z * dt)) { this.blockedH = true; this.vel.z *= this.onGround ? 0 : 0.2; }
    // never step into a column that hasn't been generated (it reads as empty air)
    if (!w.isGenerated(Math.floor(this.pos.x), Math.floor(this.pos.z))) { this.pos.x = px; this.pos.z = pz; this.vel.x = this.vel.z = 0; this.blockedH = true; }
    const vy = this.vel.y;
    this.onGround = false;
    if (this.moveAxis('y', vy * dt)) {
      // fall damage: free up to ~6 blocks, ~60 at 20 blocks, lethal from ~35 (water breaks any fall)
      if (vy < 0) { this.onGround = true; if (vy < -FALL_SAFE_V) this.damage((-vy - FALL_SAFE_V) * 4, null, 'fall'); }
      this.vel.y = 0;
    }
    const W = w.W, D = w.D;
    this.pos.x = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(this.pos.x, 1, W - 1);
    this.pos.z = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(this.pos.z, 1, D - 1);
    if (this.pos.y < -10) this.damage(9999, null, 'void');
  }

  damage(amt: number, knock?: THREE.Vector3 | null, cause?: string) {
    if (this.dead) return;
    this.hp -= amt;
    this.flash = 0.08;
    this.hitSquash = 1;
    if (this.kind !== 'boss') this.stagger = 0.14; // the boss can't be stun-locked
    if (knock) { this.vel.add(knock); if (knock.y > 1) this.onGround = false; }
    const g = this.game;
    if (amt >= 1 && this.mesh) g.ui.floatText(this.pos.x, this.pos.y + this.h + 0.2, this.pos.z, String(Math.round(amt)), this.kind === 'player' ? '#ff6a6a' : (cause === 'burn' ? '#ffa040' : '#fff4c0'));
    if (this.hp <= 0) this.die(cause, knock);
  }
  die(_cause?: string, _knock?: THREE.Vector3 | null) { this.dead = true; }

  updateSprite(dt: number) {
    if (!this.mesh) return;
    const g = this.game;
    this.animT += dt * (this.walking ? 1 : 0);
    this.flash = Math.max(0, this.flash - dt);
    const amb = g.spriteAmbient;
    if (this.flash > 0) this.mat.emissive.setRGB(2.2, 2.2, 2.2);
    else if (this.burn > 0) this.mat.emissive.setRGB(1.2 + Math.random() * 0.4, 0.5, 0.15);
    else this.mat.emissive.copy(amb);
    const yaw = g.camYaw;
    // choose view: front / back / side
    const cfx = -Math.sin(yaw), cfz = -Math.cos(yaw);
    const crx = Math.cos(yaw), crz = -Math.sin(yaw);
    const d1 = this.facing.x * cfx + this.facing.y * cfz;
    const d2 = this.facing.x * crx + this.facing.y * crz;
    let row = 2, flip = d2 < 0;
    if (this.sideOnly) row = 2;
    else if (d1 > 0.55) { row = 1; flip = false; }
    else if (d1 < -0.55) { row = 0; flip = false; }
    const frame = this.walking ? Math.floor(this.animT * (this.animSpeed || 8)) % 4 : 0;
    this.tex.repeat.x = flip ? -0.25 : 0.25;
    this.tex.offset.set(flip ? (frame + 1) / 4 : frame / 4, 1 - (row + 1) / 3);
    const hs = this.hitSquash || 0, sx = 1 + hs * 0.35, sy = (this.squash ?? 1) * (1 - hs * 0.2);
    const rx = -g.camPitch * this.lean, rz = this.spin || 0;
    this.mesh.position.copy(this.pos); this.mesh.rotation.set(rx, yaw, rz); this.mesh.scale.set(sx, sy, 1);
    this.xray.position.copy(this.pos); this.xray.rotation.set(rx, yaw, rz); this.xray.scale.set(sx, sy, 1);
    this.hitSquash = Math.max(0, (this.hitSquash || 0) - dt * 6);
    this.caster.position.copy(this.pos);
    this.caster.rotation.set(0, g.sunYaw || 0, 0);
    this.caster.visible = this.mesh.visible;
    // blob shadow on the ground beneath
    const w = g.world;
    let gy = Math.floor(this.pos.y + 0.1);
    for (let i = 0; i < 10 && gy > 0 && !w.solid(Math.floor(this.pos.x), gy - 1, Math.floor(this.pos.z)); i++) gy--;
    const hgt = this.pos.y - gy;
    this.blob.position.set(this.pos.x, gy + 0.03, this.pos.z);
    this.blob.scale.setScalar(this.r * 3 * Math.max(0.4, 1 - hgt * 0.12));
    this.blob.material.opacity = Math.max(0.2, 1 - hgt * 0.15);
  }

  updateBurn(dt: number) {
    if (this.burn <= 0) return;
    this.burn -= dt;
    const g = this.game;
    if (Math.random() < dt * 30) g.particles.fire(this.pos.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5, this.pos.y + Math.random() * this.h, this.pos.z + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5);
    this.burnTick = (this.burnTick || 0) + dt;
    if (this.burnTick > 0.5) {
      this.burnTick = 0;
      this.damage(this.kind === 'player' ? 3 : 7, null, 'burn');
      // burning things spread fire to what they walk over
      if (Math.random() < 0.4) g.world.ignite(Math.floor(this.pos.x), Math.floor(this.pos.y - 0.5), Math.floor(this.pos.z));
    }
  }

  center(out: THREE.Vector3 = new THREE.Vector3()) { return out.set(this.pos.x, this.pos.y + this.h * 0.5, this.pos.z); }
  hitRadius() { return Math.max(this.r + 0.15, this.h * 0.45); }
}

// ---------------- player ----------------
const OUTLINE = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x14101a, side: THREE.BackSide });
// a held-item part: lit box with a dark inverted-hull outline so it reads against any background
function box(w: number, h: number, d: number, color: number, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0, emissive = 0) {
  const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d);
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color, emissive: emissive || new THREE.Color(color).multiplyScalar(0.18).getHex() }));
  m.position.set(x, y, z); m.castShadow = true;
  const o = new THREE.Mesh(geo, OUTLINE);
  o.scale.set(1 + 0.05 / w, 1 + 0.05 / h, 1 + 0.05 / d);
  m.add(o);
  return m;
}
export function gunModel(kind: string) {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  const dark = 0x4a505c, mid = 0x7a828e, wood = 0xa86e3c;
  if (kind === 'pistol') { g.add(box(0.12, 0.12, 0.4, mid, 0, 0, 0.12)); g.add(box(0.1, 0.22, 0.12, wood, 0, -0.12, -0.02)); }
  else if (kind === 'shotgun') { g.add(box(0.12, 0.12, 0.9, dark, 0, 0.02, 0.3)); g.add(box(0.14, 0.16, 0.4, wood, 0, -0.04, -0.15)); g.add(box(0.1, 0.08, 0.3, wood, 0, -0.06, 0.35)); }
  else if (kind === 'smg') { g.add(box(0.14, 0.16, 0.6, mid, 0, 0, 0.15)); g.add(box(0.08, 0.28, 0.1, dark, 0, -0.18, 0.15)); g.add(box(0.06, 0.06, 0.25, dark, 0, 0.02, 0.55)); }
  else if (kind === 'rocket') { g.add(box(0.22, 0.22, 1.1, 0x5a7a4a, 0, 0.06, 0.1)); g.add(box(0.26, 0.26, 0.12, 0x3a4a30, 0, 0.06, 0.66)); g.add(box(0.08, 0.2, 0.1, dark, 0, -0.12, 0)); }
  else if (kind === 'grenade') { g.add(box(0.2, 0.24, 0.2, 0x5a7a3a, 0, 0, 0.1)); g.add(box(0.06, 0.08, 0.06, 0xaaaaaa, 0, 0.16, 0.1)); }
  else if (kind === 'pick') { g.add(box(0.07, 0.07, 0.8, wood, 0, 0, 0.3)); g.add(box(0.6, 0.1, 0.1, 0x9aa4ac, 0, 0, 0.66)); }
  else { const c = ({ planks: 0xc9955a, bricks: 0xa8705a, tnt: 0xd8443a, turret: 0x7a8a96, lantern: 0xffd27a } as Record<string, number>)[kind] ?? 0xffffff; g.add(box(0.3, 0.3, 0.3, c, 0, 0, 0.15, kind === 'lantern' ? 0x664400 : 0)); }
  g.scale.setScalar(1.5); // readable at the default camera distance
  return g;
}

export class Player extends Entity {
  dashT: number; dashCd: number; coyote: number; invuln: number;
  gunHolder: THREE.Group;
  models: Record<string, THREE.Group>;
  currentModel: THREE.Group | null;
  currentKind?: string;
  aimDir: THREE.Vector3;
  swing: number; recoil: number;
  light: THREE.PointLight;
  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number, tex: THREE.Texture) {
    super(game, x, y, z, { tex, hp: 100, kind: 'player', r: 0.32, h: 1.7 });
    this.dashT = 0; this.dashCd = 0; this.coyote = 0; this.invuln = 0;
    this.gunHolder = new THREE.Group();
    game.scene.add(this.gunHolder);
    this.models = {};
    this.currentModel = null;
    this.aimDir = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1);
    this.swing = 0;
    this.recoil = 0;
    this.light = new THREE.PointLight(0xffb060, 0, 14, 1.6);
    game.scene.add(this.light);
  }
  setModel(kind: string) {
    if (this.currentKind === kind) return;
    if (this.currentModel) this.gunHolder.remove(this.currentModel);
    if (!this.models[kind]) this.models[kind] = gunModel(kind);
    this.currentModel = this.models[kind]; this.currentKind = kind;
    this.gunHolder.add(this.currentModel);
  }
  update(dt: number, input: InputState) {
    const g = this.game;
    if (this.dead) return;
    const yaw = g.camYaw;
    const fx = -Math.sin(yaw), fz = -Math.cos(yaw), rx = Math.cos(yaw), rz = -Math.sin(yaw);
    let mx = 0, mz = 0;
    if (input.keys.KeyW) { mx += fx; mz += fz; }
    if (input.keys.KeyS) { mx -= fx; mz -= fz; }
    if (input.keys.KeyD) { mx += rx; mz += rz; }
    if (input.keys.KeyA) { mx -= rx; mz -= rz; }
    const len = Math.hypot(mx, mz);
    if (len > 0) { mx /= len; mz /= len; }
    this.walking = len > 0 && this.onGround;
    this.dashCd -= dt; this.invuln -= dt;
    if ((input.pressed.ShiftLeft || input.pressed.ShiftRight) && this.dashCd <= 0 && len > 0) {
      this.dashT = 0.18; this.dashCd = 0.8; this.invuln = 0.25; g.audio.dash();
      g.particles.dust(this.pos.x, this.pos.y + 0.1, this.pos.z, 8);
    }
    let speed = (this.inWater ? 3.5 : 6.2) * g.mods.speed;
    if (this.dashT > 0) { this.dashT -= dt; speed = 19; }
    const accel = this.onGround ? 14 : 5;
    const k = Math.min(1, accel * dt);
    this.vel.x += (mx * speed - this.vel.x) * k;
    this.vel.z += (mz * speed - this.vel.z) * k;
    if (this.onGround) this.coyote = 0.1; else this.coyote -= dt;
    if (input.pressed.Space && (this.coyote > 0 || this.inWater)) { this.vel.y = this.inWater ? 6 : 8.6; this.coyote = 0; g.audio.jump(); }
    this.physics(dt);
    // facing follows aim
    this.facing.set(this.aimDir.x, this.aimDir.z).normalize();
    if (this.facing.lengthSq() < 0.01) this.facing.set(mx || 0, mz || 1);
    this.updateBurn(dt);
    this.updateSprite(dt);
    // held item points at the cursor in full 3D
    this.recoil = Math.max(0, this.recoil - dt * 6);
    this.swing = Math.max(0, this.swing - dt * 4);
    const hx = this.aimDir.x, hz = this.aimDir.z, hl = Math.hypot(hx, hz) || 1;
    this.gunHolder.position.set(this.pos.x + hx / hl * 0.42, this.pos.y + 0.95, this.pos.z + hz / hl * 0.42);
    const pitch = Math.asin(THREE.MathUtils.clamp(this.aimDir.y, -1, 1));
    this.gunHolder.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
    this.gunHolder.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
    this.gunHolder.rotation.y = Math.atan2(hx, hz);
    this.gunHolder.rotation.x = -pitch - this.swing * 2.2 + this.recoil * 0.35;
    if (this.currentModel) this.currentModel.position.z = -this.recoil * 0.15;
    // carried lantern: held up and out toward the aim, not just over the head (2.2 above the feet at
    // full strength it blew the sprite out to white at night); it still pools light around the player
    this.light.position.set(this.pos.x + hx / hl * 1.3, this.pos.y + 3.4, this.pos.z + hz / hl * 1.3);
  }
  damage(amt: number, knock?: THREE.Vector3 | null, cause?: string) {
    if (this.dead || (this.invuln > 0 && cause !== 'void')) return;
    super.damage(amt, knock, cause);
    this.game.onPlayerHurt(amt);
  }
  die(cause?: string) {
    this.dead = true;
    this.game.onPlayerDeath(cause);
  }
}

// ---------------- enemies ----------------
export interface EnemyOpts extends EntityOpts {
  speed: number; atk: number; gore?: Vec3Tuple; drops?: Drops; sound?: 'groan' | 'chitter';
}

export abstract class Enemy extends Entity {
  speed: number; atk: number; atkCd: number; blockedT: number; strafe: number; strafeT: number;
  goreCol: Vec3Tuple;
  drops: Drops;
  thinkT: number;
  sound?: 'groan' | 'chitter';
  climbs?: boolean;
  climbing?: boolean;
  atkRate?: number;
  aggro = false; // noticed the player (always on at night)
  wanderT = 0; wanderX = 0; wanderZ = 0; // idle shuffle while unaware (day)
  abstract think(dt: number): void;
  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number, o: EnemyOpts) {
    super(game, x, y, z, o);
    this.speed = o.speed; this.atk = o.atk; this.atkCd = 0; this.blockedT = 0; this.strafe = 0; this.strafeT = 0;
    this.goreCol = o.gore || [0.55, 0.05, 0.05];
    this.drops = o.drops || {};
    this.thinkT = Math.random();
    this.sound = o.sound;
  }
  // aware of the player? always at night; by day only within AGGRO_DAY (kept up to AGGRO_LOSE) or once hurt
  awake(): boolean {
    const g = this.game;
    if (g.isNight()) return true;
    const p = g.entities.player.pos, dx = p.x - this.pos.x, dz = p.z - this.pos.z, d2 = dx * dx + dz * dz;
    if (this.aggro) { if (d2 > AGGRO_LOSE * AGGRO_LOSE) this.aggro = false; }
    else if (d2 < AGGRO_DAY * AGGRO_DAY && Math.abs(p.y - this.pos.y) < AGGRO_DY) this.aggro = true;
    return this.aggro;
  }
  damage(amt: number, knock?: THREE.Vector3 | null, cause?: string) { this.aggro = true; super.damage(amt, knock, cause); }
  // unaware: shuffle about slowly, pausing now and then
  wander(dt: number) {
    this.wanderT -= dt;
    if (this.wanderT <= 0) {
      this.wanderT = 2 + Math.random() * 4;
      const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, go = Math.random() < 0.55;
      this.wanderX = go ? Math.cos(a) : 0; this.wanderZ = go ? Math.sin(a) : 0;
    }
    if (this.blockedH) { this.wanderX = -this.wanderX; this.wanderZ = -this.wanderZ; }
    const sp = this.speed * 0.3, k = Math.min(1, (this.onGround ? 8 : 2) * dt);
    this.vel.x += (this.wanderX * sp - this.vel.x) * k;
    this.vel.z += (this.wanderZ * sp - this.vel.z) * k;
    this.walking = this.wanderX !== 0 || this.wanderZ !== 0;
    if (this.walking) this.facing.set(this.wanderX, this.wanderZ);
  }
  pickTarget(): Entity | null {
    const g = this.game, p = g.entities.player;
    if (!this.awake()) return null;
    let best: Entity | null = p.dead ? null : p, bd = best ? best.pos.distanceToSquared(this.pos) * 0.6 : Infinity;
    for (const t of g.entities.turrets) { const d = t.pos.distanceToSquared(this.pos); if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = t; } }
    return best;
  }
  // push apart from nearby enemies, looking only at the spatial-hash cells that can contain a neighbour
  separation(out: THREE.Vector3) {
    const ents = this.game.entities, head = ents.gridHead, next = ents.gridNext, list = ents.gridList, gw = ents.gridW, gd = ents.gridD;
    // grid positions are from the start of the frame, so leave slack for enemies that moved since
    const reach = Math.ceil(((this.r + ents.gridMaxR) * 1.3 + GRID_SLACK) / GRID_CELL);
    const cx = Math.floor(this.pos.x / GRID_CELL) - ents.gridOX, cz = Math.floor(this.pos.z / GRID_CELL) - ents.gridOZ;
    const x0 = Math.max(0, cx - reach), x1 = Math.min(gw - 1, cx + reach), z0 = Math.max(0, cz - reach), z1 = Math.min(gd - 1, cz + reach);
    for (let z = z0; z <= z1; z++) for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
      for (let i = head[x + z * gw]; i >= 0; i = next[i]) {
        const o = list[i];
        if (o === this || o.dead) continue;
        const dy = o.pos.y - this.pos.y;
        if (dy > this.h || -dy > o.h) continue; // different heights (e.g. a bat overhead) don't crowd each other
        let dx = this.pos.x - o.pos.x, dz = this.pos.z - o.pos.z;
        let d2 = dx * dx + dz * dz;
        const rr = (this.r + o.r) * 1.3;
        if (d2 <= 1e-4) { const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2; dx = Math.cos(a) * 0.01; dz = Math.sin(a) * 0.01; d2 = dx * dx + dz * dz; } // exactly stacked: pick a random way out
        if (d2 < rr * rr) { const d = Math.sqrt(d2); out.x += dx / d * (rr - d) * 4; out.z += dz / d * (rr - d) * 4; }
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
  steer(dt: number, tgt: Entity, speedMul = 1, away = false) {
    let dx = tgt.pos.x - this.pos.x, dz = tgt.pos.z - this.pos.z;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
    dx /= d; dz /= d;
    if (away) { dx = -dx; dz = -dz; }
    // follow the shared flow field when the target is the player and not close by
    else if (d > 3.5 && tgt === this.game.entities.player) {
      const f = (this.climbs ? this.game.entities.climbField : this.game.entities.walkField).sample(this.pos.x, this.pos.z);
      if (f) { dx = f[0]; dz = f[1]; }
    }
    if (this.strafeT > 0) { this.strafeT -= dt; const sx = -dz * this.strafe, sz = dx * this.strafe; dx = dx * 0.3 + sx; dz = dz * 0.3 + sz; }
    const sep = this.separation(_w.set(0, 0, 0));
    const sp = this.speed * speedMul * (this.inWater ? 0.5 : 1);
    const k = Math.min(1, (this.onGround ? 8 : 2) * dt);
    this.vel.x += (dx * sp + sep.x - this.vel.x) * k;
    this.vel.z += (dz * sp + sep.z - this.vel.z) * k;
    this.facing.set(dx, dz);
    this.walking = true;
    return d;
  }
  die(_cause?: string, knock?: THREE.Vector3 | null) {
    if (this.dead) return; // callers outside damage() can reach an already-dead enemy: no second corpse, kill or drops
    this.dead = true;
    const g = this.game;
    g.entities.addCorpse(this, knock);
    g.particles.blood(this.pos.x, this.pos.y + this.h * 0.5, this.pos.z, this.goreCol, 18, knock ? _v.copy(knock).normalize() : null);
    for (const [res, [n, chance]] of Object.entries(this.drops)) {
      if (Math.random() < chance) g.entities.addPickup(res, n, this.pos.x, this.pos.y + 0.6, this.pos.z);
    }
    g.kills++;
    g.audio.splat();
    g.hitstop(0.05);
  }
  // nothing solid between the two bodies (a diagonal wall's corner gap let zombies bite through it)
  reaches(tgt: Entity): boolean {
    const from = this.center(_a), dir = tgt.center(_b).sub(from), L = dir.length();
    return L < 1e-3 || !this.game.world.raycast(from, dir.divideScalar(L), L).hit;
  }
  contactAttack(tgt: Entity, dist: number): boolean {
    if (dist < this.r + tgt.r + 0.55 && Math.abs(tgt.pos.y - this.pos.y) < 1.4 && this.atkCd <= 0 && this.reaches(tgt)) {
      this.atkCd = this.atkRate || 1;
      const kb = _v.set(tgt.pos.x - this.pos.x, 0, tgt.pos.z - this.pos.z).normalize().multiplyScalar(5);
      kb.y = 3;
      tgt.damage(this.atk, kb.clone(), 'melee');
      this.squash = 0.8;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  update(dt: number) {
    this.atkCd -= dt;
    if (this.squash !== undefined) this.squash = Math.min(1, (this.squash ?? 1) + dt * 2);
    if (this.stagger > 0) { this.stagger -= dt; this.vel.x *= 0.9; this.vel.z *= 0.9; this.atkCd = Math.max(this.atkCd, 0.15); }
    else this.think(dt);
    this.physics(dt, this.climbing ? 4 : G);
    this.updateBurn(dt);
    this.updateSprite(dt);
    if (this.sound && Math.random() < dt * 0.08) {
      const d = this.pos.distanceTo(this.game.entities.player.pos);
      if (d < 22) this.game.audio[this.sound](1 - d / 22);
    }
  }
}

export class Zombie extends Enemy {
  wallDmg?: number;
  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number, tex: THREE.Texture) {
    super(game, x, y, z, { tex, kind: 'zombie', hp: 70, speed: 2.4 + Math.random() * 0.8, atk: 12, r: 0.34, h: 1.75, gore: [0.5, 0.08, 0.06], drops: { metal: [1, 0.55], goo: [1, 0.25] }, sound: 'groan' });
    this.animSpeed = 5;
  }
  think(dt: number) {
    const tgt = this.pickTarget();
    if (!tgt) { this.wander(dt); return; }
    const d = this.steer(dt, tgt);
    this.contactAttack(tgt, d);
    // stuck handling: hop, then break through player-built structures
    if (this.blockedH) {
      this.blockedT += dt;
      if (this.onGround && this.blockedT > 0.15) this.vel.y = 8.2;
      if (this.blockedT > 0.7 && this.atkCd <= 0) {
        const w = this.game.world;
        const fx = Math.floor(this.pos.x + this.facing.x * (this.r + 0.4)), fz = Math.floor(this.pos.z + this.facing.y * (this.r + 0.4));
        let broke = false;
        for (const yy of [Math.floor(this.pos.y + 0.5), Math.floor(this.pos.y + 1.4)]) {
          const t = w.get(fx, yy, fz);
          if (t && !BLOCKS[t].anchored) {
            w.damageBlock(fx, yy, fz, this.wallDmg || 1.4, 'zombie');
            this.game.particles.chips(fx + 0.5, yy + 0.5, fz + 0.5, BLOCKS[t].cSide, 3);
            this.game.audio.thunk(0.6);
            this.atkCd = 0.8; this.squash = 0.82; broke = true; break;
          }
        }
        if (!broke && this.blockedT > 1.6) { this.strafe = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1; this.strafeT = 1.4; this.blockedT = 0; }
      }
    } else this.blockedT = Math.max(0, this.blockedT - dt);
  }
}

export class Crawler extends Enemy {
  pounceCd: number;
  climbY?: number; // height when the current climb last made progress
  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number, tex: THREE.Texture) {
    super(game, x, y, z, { tex, kind: 'crawler', hp: 22, speed: 5.4, atk: 7, r: 0.38, h: 0.7, sw: 1.7, sh: 1.7, lean: 0.25, gore: [0.3, 0.8, 0.2], drops: { goo: [1, 0.7] }, sound: 'chitter' });
    this.sideOnly = true; this.animSpeed = 14; this.atkRate = 0.6; this.pounceCd = 1; this.climbs = true;
  }
  think(dt: number) {
    const tgt = this.pickTarget();
    this.climbing = false;
    if (!tgt) { this.wander(dt); return; }
    const d = this.steer(dt, tgt);
    this.pounceCd -= dt;
    if (d < 5 && d > 1.6 && this.onGround && this.pounceCd <= 0) {
      this.pounceCd = 2 + Math.random();
      this.vel.x = this.facing.x * 10; this.vel.z = this.facing.y * 10; this.vel.y = 6.5;
      this.game.audio.chitter(0.7);
    }
    this.contactAttack(tgt, d);
    // bugs crawl straight up walls; one wedged under an overhang (a tree canopy, battlements) stopped
    // rising and clung there forever, so after a moment without climbing it scuttles sideways instead
    if (this.blockedH) {
      this.climbing = true; this.vel.y = 4.2; this.spin = 0;
      const y0 = this.climbY ?? this.pos.y;
      if (this.pos.y > y0 + 0.3) { this.climbY = this.pos.y; this.blockedT = 0; }
      else if ((this.blockedT += dt) > 0.8) { this.strafe = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1; this.strafeT = 1.2; this.blockedT = 0; this.climbY = undefined; }
      else this.climbY = y0;
    } else { this.climbY = undefined; this.blockedT = 0; }
  }
}

export class Spitter extends Enemy {
  spitCd: number;
  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number, tex: THREE.Texture) {
    super(game, x, y, z, { tex, kind: 'spitter', hp: 38, speed: 2.8, atk: 6, r: 0.5, h: 1.1, sw: 2.3, sh: 2.3, lean: 0.3, gore: [0.35, 0.95, 0.25], drops: { goo: [2, 0.9], metal: [1, 0.3] }, sound: 'chitter' });
    this.sideOnly = true; this.animSpeed = 9; this.spitCd = 2 + Math.random() * 2;
  }
  think(dt: number) {
    const g = this.game;
    const tgt = this.pickTarget();
    this.climbing = false;
    if (!tgt) { this.wander(dt); return; }
    const d = Math.hypot(tgt.pos.x - this.pos.x, tgt.pos.z - this.pos.z);
    if (d > 12) this.steer(dt, tgt);
    else if (d < 7) this.steer(dt, tgt, 0.8, true);
    else { this.vel.x *= 0.9; this.vel.z *= 0.9; this.walking = false; this.facing.set((tgt.pos.x - this.pos.x) / d, (tgt.pos.z - this.pos.z) / d); }
    if (this.blockedH && this.onGround) this.vel.y = 8;
    this.spitCd -= dt;
    if (this.spitCd <= 0 && d < 16) {
      this.spitCd = 2.2 + Math.random() * 1.5;
      this.squash = 0.7;
      const from = this.center(new THREE.Vector3()); from.y += 0.3;
      const to = tgt.center(new THREE.Vector3());
      to.addScaledVector(tgt.vel, 0.5);
      g.projectiles.lob('acid', from, to, this);
      g.audio.spit();
    }
  }
}

// Brute: slow, tanky, smashes walls three times as hard and bowls the player over
export class Brute extends Zombie {
  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number, tex: THREE.Texture) {
    super(game, x, y, z, tex);
    Object.assign(this, { kind: 'brute', maxHp: 280, hp: 280, speed: 1.9, atk: 26, r: 0.6, h: 2.6, wallDmg: 4.5, atkRate: 1.4, animSpeed: 4 });
    this.drops = { metal: [3, 1], goo: [2, 0.8] };
    this.goreCol = [0.45, 0.05, 0.08];
    this.removeSprite(); this.makeSprite(tex, 3.4, 3.4, 0.45);
    this.mat.color.setRGB(1.0, 0.7, 0.7);
  }
  contactAttack(tgt: Entity, dist: number): boolean {
    const hp = tgt.hp, hit = super.contactAttack(tgt, dist);
    // (the extra launch only when the blow landed: not through dash or respawn invulnerability)
    if (hit && tgt.hp < hp) { tgt.vel.y += 6; this.game.shake(0.3); this.game.audio.thunk(1); }
    return hit;
  }
}

// Bat: flies over walls, swoops in to bite, then peels away
export class Bat extends Enemy {
  retreat: number;
  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number, tex: THREE.Texture) {
    super(game, x, y + 3, z, { tex, kind: 'bat', hp: 16, speed: 7, atk: 6, r: 0.35, h: 0.6, sw: 1.5, sh: 1.5, lean: 0.2, gore: [0.5, 0.1, 0.25], drops: { goo: [1, 0.5] }, sound: 'chitter' });
    this.sideOnly = true; this.animSpeed = 16; this.retreat = 0; this.walking = true;
  }
  think(dt: number) {
    const g = this.game, w = g.world;
    const tgt = this.pickTarget();
    if (!tgt) { this.vel.multiplyScalar(0.95); return; } // hover in place (it kept its last velocity and flew off)
    const c = tgt.center(_v);
    let dx = c.x - this.pos.x, dz = c.z - this.pos.z;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
    this.retreat -= dt;
    let sign = this.retreat > 0 ? -1 : 1;
    // clear the terrain under us and a few blocks ahead, so bats rise over hills instead of grinding into them
    let ground = w.surfaceY(Math.floor(this.pos.x), Math.floor(this.pos.z));
    for (const ahead of [0.5, 1.5, 2.5]) ground = Math.max(ground, w.surfaceY(Math.floor(this.pos.x + dx / d * ahead), Math.floor(this.pos.z + dz / d * ahead)));
    const cruise = Math.max(ground + 2.5, c.y + (d < 4 && this.retreat <= 0 ? -0.2 : 2.5));
    this.vel.x += (dx / d * this.speed * sign - this.vel.x) * Math.min(1, dt * 3);
    this.vel.z += (dz / d * this.speed * sign - this.vel.z) * Math.min(1, dt * 3);
    this.vel.y += ((cruise - this.pos.y) * 3 + Math.sin(this.animT * 9) * 2 - this.vel.y) * Math.min(1, dt * 4);
    this.facing.set(dx / d * sign, dz / d * sign);
    this.walking = true;
    if (this.contactAttack(tgt, d) || (d < 1 && this.retreat <= 0)) this.retreat = 1.2;
  }
  update(dt: number) {
    this.atkCd -= dt;
    if (this.stagger > 0) { this.stagger -= dt; this.vel.y -= 10 * dt; } else this.think(dt);
    this.physics(dt, 0);
    this.updateBurn(dt);
    this.walking = true;
    this.updateSprite(dt);
  }
}

// Brood Mother: the night-3 boss. Lobs acid volleys and births crawlers.
export class BroodMother extends Spitter {
  broodCd: number;
  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number, tex: THREE.Texture) {
    super(game, x, y, z, tex);
    Object.assign(this, { kind: 'boss', maxHp: 1600, hp: 1600, speed: 1.6, atk: 30, r: 1.4, h: 3.2 });
    this.drops = { metal: [8, 1], goo: [10, 1] };
    this.removeSprite(); this.makeSprite(tex, 7, 7, 0.35);
    this.mat.color.setRGB(1.0, 0.55, 0.75);
    this.broodCd = 4;
  }
  awake() { return true; } // she came for you: never idles, even by day
  think(dt: number) {
    const g = this.game, tgt = this.pickTarget();
    if (!tgt) return;
    const d = Math.hypot(tgt.pos.x - this.pos.x, tgt.pos.z - this.pos.z);
    if (d > 9) this.steer(dt, tgt); else { this.vel.x *= 0.92; this.vel.z *= 0.92; this.walking = false; }
    this.contactAttack(tgt, d);
    if (this.blockedH) {
      // bulldozes through anything in the way
      const w = g.world;
      for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) for (let s = -1; s <= 1; s++) w.damageBlock(Math.floor(this.pos.x + this.facing.x * 1.8 - this.facing.y * s), Math.floor(this.pos.y + dy), Math.floor(this.pos.z + this.facing.y * 1.8 + this.facing.x * s), dt * 12, 'zombie');
      if (this.onGround) this.vel.y = 7;
    }
    this.spitCd -= dt;
    if (this.spitCd <= 0 && d < 22) {
      this.spitCd = 2.4;
      this.squash = 0.75;
      const from = this.center(new THREE.Vector3()); from.y += 1;
      for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) {
        const to = tgt.center(new THREE.Vector3()).addScaledVector(tgt.vel, 0.6);
        to.x += i * 2.2; to.z += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
        g.projectiles.lob('acid', from, to, this);
      }
      g.audio.spit();
    }
    this.broodCd -= dt;
    if (this.broodCd <= 0) {
      this.broodCd = 6;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const c = g.entities.spawnEnemy('crawler', this.pos.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2, this.pos.y + 1, this.pos.z + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2);
        c.vel.set((Math.random() - 0.5) * 8, 6, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 8);
      }
      g.particles.blood(this.pos.x, this.pos.y + 1.5, this.pos.z, this.goreCol, 20);
    }
  }
  die(cause?: string, knock?: THREE.Vector3 | null) {
    if (this.dead) return;
    super.die(cause, knock);
    this.game.onBossDefeated(this);
  }
}

// ---------------- turret (3D model, not a sprite) ----------------
export class Turret extends Entity {
  head: THREE.Group;
  grp: THREE.Group;
  cd: number; ammo: number; yaw: number; side: number;
  target: Enemy | null = null; scanT = 0;
  constructor(game: Game, x: number, y: number, z: number) {
    super(game, x, y, z, { kind: 'turret', hp: 160, r: 0.45, h: 1.2 });
    const grp = new THREE.Group();
    grp.add(box(0.9, 0.35, 0.9, 0x5a6470, 0, 0.18, 0));
    grp.add(box(0.5, 0.4, 0.5, 0x48505a, 0, 0.55, 0));
    this.head = new THREE.Group();
    this.head.position.y = 0.95;
    this.head.add(box(0.55, 0.4, 0.6, 0x7a8a96));
    this.head.add(box(0.12, 0.12, 0.7, 0x2a2e36, 0.12, 0, 0.5));
    this.head.add(box(0.12, 0.12, 0.7, 0x2a2e36, -0.12, 0, 0.5));
    this.head.add(box(0.2, 0.08, 0.05, 0xff3a2a, 0, 0.1, 0.31, 0xff2a1a));
    grp.add(this.head);
    grp.position.copy(this.pos);
    grp.traverse(m => { if ((m as THREE.Mesh).isMesh) { m.castShadow = true; m.receiveShadow = true; } });
    this.head.children[3].userData.keep = true;
    this.grp = grp;
    game.scene.add(grp);
    this.cd = 0; this.ammo = 250; this.yaw = 0; this.side = 1;
  }
  update(dt: number) {
    const g = this.game;
    this.physics(dt);
    this.grp.position.copy(this.pos);
    this.cd -= dt;
    // full nearest-visible-enemy scan only a few times a second; in between just re-check the cached target
    this.scanT -= dt;
    if (this.scanT <= 0 || (this.target && (this.target.dead || !this.canSee(this.target, 18 * 18)))) {
      this.scanT = 0.12;
      let best: Enemy | null = null, bd = 18 * 18;
      for (const e of g.entities.enemies) {
        if (e.dead) continue;
        const d = e.pos.distanceToSquared(this.pos);
        if (d < bd && this.canSee(e, bd)) { bd = d; best = e; }
      }
      this.target = best;
    }
    const best = this.target;
    if (best) {
      const to = best.center(new THREE.Vector3());
      const want = Math.atan2(to.x - this.pos.x, to.z - this.pos.z);
      let diff = want - this.yaw; diff = Math.atan2(Math.sin(diff), Math.cos(diff));
      this.yaw += diff * Math.min(1, dt * 10);
      if (Math.abs(diff) < 0.2 && this.cd <= 0 && this.ammo > 0) {
        this.cd = 0.16 * g.mods.turret; this.ammo--; this.side = -this.side;
        const from = new THREE.Vector3(this.pos.x + Math.cos(this.yaw) * 0.12 * this.side, this.pos.y + 0.95, this.pos.z - Math.sin(this.yaw) * 0.12 * this.side);
        const dir = to.sub(from).normalize();
        from.addScaledVector(dir, 0.8);
        g.projectiles.bullet(from, dir, { dmg: 9, speed: 70, blockDmg: 0.2, owner: this, spread: 0.03 });
        g.particles.muzzle(from.x, from.y, from.z, dir);
        g.audio.shot('turret', Math.max(0.1, 1 - this.pos.distanceTo(g.entities.player.pos) / 30));
      }
    } else this.yaw += dt * 0.5;
    this.head.rotation.y = this.yaw;
    const fl = this.flash > 0 ? 0.8 : 0; this.flash = Math.max(0, this.flash - dt);
    this.grp.traverse(m => { const mat = (m as THREE.Mesh).material as THREE.MeshLambertMaterial | undefined; if ((m as THREE.Mesh).isMesh && mat && mat.emissive && !m.userData.keep) mat.emissive.setRGB(fl, fl, fl); });
    if (this.ammo <= 0 && !this.dead) { this.ammo = 0; g.ui.toast('A turret ran out of ammo'); this.die(); }
  }
  // within range (squared distance < maxD2) and not blocked by voxels
  canSee(e: Enemy, maxD2: number): boolean {
    if (e.pos.distanceToSquared(this.pos) >= maxD2) return false;
    const from = _v.set(this.pos.x, this.pos.y + 0.95, this.pos.z);
    const dir = e.center(_w).sub(from); const L = dir.length(); dir.divideScalar(L);
    return !this.game.world.raycast(from, dir, L).hit;
  }
  die() {
    if (this.dead) return;
    this.dead = true;
    this.game.scene.remove(this.grp);
    this.game.explode(this.pos.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.6, 0)), 2.2, 6, 'turret');
  }
}

// ---------------- manager ----------------
// drop dead entities in place (keeps array identity, no per-frame allocation)
const DESPAWN_RANGE = 90, DESPAWN_DY = 60;

function compactAlive<T extends Entity>(a: T[]): void {
  let n = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (!a[i].dead) a[n++] = a[i];
  a.length = n;
}

export class Entities {
  game: Game;
  sheets: Sheets;
  player: Player;
  enemies: Enemy[];
  turrets: Turret[];
  pickups: Pickup[];
  corpses: Corpse[];
  spawnT: number;
  walkField: FlowField;
  climbField: FlowField;
  fieldT: number; fieldFlip: boolean;
  fieldCells = [-1, -1]; // player cell each field (walk, climb) was last rooted at
  pickGeo: THREE.BoxGeometry;
  gridHead = new Int32Array(0); gridNext = new Int32Array(0); gridList: Enemy[] = [];
  gridW = 0; gridD = 0; gridMaxR = 0; gridOX = 0; gridOZ = 0; // grid size and corner (in cells); it covers a window around the player
  pickMats: Record<string, THREE.MeshLambertMaterial>;
  constructor(game: Game, sheets: Sheets) {
    this.game = game; this.sheets = sheets;
    this.player = null!; this.enemies = []; this.turrets = []; this.pickups = []; this.corpses = [];
    this.spawnT = 3;
    this.walkField = new FlowField(game.world, false);
    this.climbField = new FlowField(game.world, true);
    this.fieldT = 0; this.fieldFlip = false;
    this.pickGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.28, 0.28, 0.28);
    this.pickMats = {
      wood: new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: 0x9a6a3a }), stone: new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: 0xa8a6a0 }),
      metal: new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: 0x9ab4c8, emissive: 0x223344 }), goo: new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: 0x5adf3a, emissive: 0x1a6a10 }),
    };
  }
  all(): Entity[] { return [this.player, ...this.enemies, ...this.turrets].filter(e => e && !e.dead); }
  // bucket live enemies into a coarse XZ grid (linked lists in typed arrays) for neighbour queries
  buildGrid(): void {
    const gw = GRID_SPAN, gd = GRID_SPAN, p = this.player.pos;
    const ox = this.gridOX = Math.floor(p.x / GRID_CELL) - (GRID_SPAN >> 1), oz = this.gridOZ = Math.floor(p.z / GRID_CELL) - (GRID_SPAN >> 1);
    if (gw !== this.gridW || gd !== this.gridD) { this.gridW = gw; this.gridD = gd; this.gridHead = new Int32Array(gw * gd); }
    this.gridHead.fill(-1);
    const list = this.gridList; list.length = 0;
    let maxR = 0;
    for (const e of this.enemies) {
      if (e.dead) continue;
      const cx = Math.min(gw - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(e.pos.x / GRID_CELL) - ox)), cz = Math.min(gd - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(e.pos.z / GRID_CELL) - oz));
      const i = list.length, c = cx + cz * gw;
      list.push(e);
      if (i >= this.gridNext.length) { const n = new Int32Array(Math.max(64, this.gridNext.length * 2)); n.set(this.gridNext); this.gridNext = n; }
      this.gridNext[i] = this.gridHead[c]; this.gridHead[c] = i;
      if (e.r > maxR) maxR = e.r;
    }
    this.gridMaxR = maxR;
  }
  // remove every enemy, turret, pickup and corpse (used when loading a save)
  reset(): void {
    const s = this.game.scene;
    for (const e of this.enemies) e.removeSprite();
    for (const c of this.corpses) c.e.removeSprite();
    for (const t of this.turrets) s.remove(t.grp);
    for (const k of this.pickups) s.remove(k.m);
    this.enemies = []; this.corpses = []; this.turrets = []; this.pickups = [];
    this.walkField.ready = this.climbField.ready = false;
    this.walkField.running = this.climbField.running = false;
    this.fieldCells[0] = this.fieldCells[1] = -1;
  }

  spawnPlayer(x: number, y: number, z: number) { this.player = new Player(this.game, x, y, z, this.sheets.player); return this.player; }

  spawnEnemy(type: string, x: number, y: number, z: number): Enemy {
    let e: Enemy;
    if (type === 'zombie') e = new Zombie(this.game, x, y, z, this.sheets.zombies[(Math.random() * this.sheets.zombies.length) | 0]);
    else if (type === 'crawler') e = new Crawler(this.game, x, y, z, this.sheets.crawler);
    else if (type === 'brute') e = new Brute(this.game, x, y, z, this.sheets.brute);
    else if (type === 'bat') e = new Bat(this.game, x, y, z, this.sheets.bat);
    else if (type === 'boss') e = new BroodMother(this.game, x, y, z, this.sheets.spitter);
    else e = new Spitter(this.game, x, y, z, this.sheets.spitter);
    this.enemies.push(e);
    return e;
  }

  // A spawn spot 'minD'..'maxD' blocks (horizontally) from the player that the given kind of enemy can
  // actually use. Walkers and climbers must be able to reach the player: the spot comes from their flow
  // field (so never on top of a 200-block cliff, on a tree canopy or across a gorge), standing on dry
  // ground. Before the fields exist (game start, just loaded) it falls back to dry ground within a few
  // blocks of the player's height. Bats only need open air over generated terrain near the player's height.
  randomSpawnPoint(minD = 24, maxD = 40, type = 'zombie'): Vec3Tuple | null {
    const w = this.game.world, p = this.player.pos, water = this.game.waterSim;
    const flyer = type === 'bat';
    const field = flyer ? null : (type === 'crawler' ? this.climbField : this.walkField);
    const py = Math.floor(p.y + 0.05);
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
```
