  // generate everything within `radius` columns right now (used at start-up and after loading)
  generateAround(px: number, pz: number, radius = this.genRadius): void {
    const pcx = Math.floor(px / CHUNK), pcz = Math.floor(pz / CHUNK);
    for (const [dx, dz, d] of RINGS) if (d <= radius) this.ensureColumn(pcx + dx, pcz + dz);
  }

  /** drop a column (meshes, voxels, lanterns, damage, fire) so it regenerates from the seed */
  dropColumn(cx: number, cz: number): void {
    const ci = cx + cz * CX;
    if (!this.colState[ci]) return;
    for (let cy = 0; cy < CY; cy++) {
      const key = chunkKey(cx, cy, cz), ch = this.chunks[key];
      // out of the table first, then the slabs go back to the allocator
      this.tab.fill[key] = ABSENT; this.tab.blk[key] = 0; this.tab.dec[key] = 0;
      if (ch) { this.disposeMeshes(ch); this.core.free(ch.blocks); this.core.free(ch.decor); this.core.free(ch.water); }
      this.chunks[key] = undefined;
    }
    this.colState[ci] = 0;
    const x0 = cx * CHUNK, z0 = cz * CHUNK;
    const inCol = (i: number) => { const x = this.ix(i), z = this.iz(i); return x >= x0 && x < x0 + CHUNK && z >= z0 && z < z0 + CHUNK; };
    for (const i of this.lanterns) if (inCol(i)) this.lanterns.delete(i);
    for (const i of this.damage.keys()) if (inCol(i)) this.damage.delete(i);
    for (const i of this.burning.keys()) if (inCol(i)) this.burning.delete(i);
    if (this.game.waterSim) this.game.waterSim.dropColumn(cx, cz);
    this.capDirty = true;
  }

  /** wipe a column and generate it again from the seed (used when loading a save) */
  regenColumn(cx: number, cz: number): void {
    this.dropColumn(cx, cz);
    this.ensureColumn(cx, cz);
  }

  /** whether a column has player-made changes (so it must stay in memory) */
  columnModified(cx: number, cz: number): boolean {
    for (let cy = 0; cy < CY; cy++) { const ch = this.chunks[chunkKey(cx, cy, cz)]; if (ch && ch.modified) return true; }
    return false;
  }

  /** every loaded chunk that changed since generation (save games) */
  *modifiedChunks(): Generator<VChunk> {
    for (let ci = 0; ci < CX * CZ; ci++) {
      if (this.colState[ci] !== 2) continue;
      for (let cy = 0; cy < CY; cy++) { const ch = this.chunks[(ci % CX) + Math.floor(ci / CX) * CX + cy * CXZ]; if (ch && ch.modified) yield ch; }
    }
  }

  /** overwrite one chunk from a save (its column must already be generated); arrays are copied into slabs */
  loadChunk(cx: number, cy: number, cz: number, blocks: Uint8Array | null, fill: number, decor: Uint8Array | null, water: Uint8Array | null): void {
    const ch = this.chunk(cx, cy, cz);
    if (!ch) return;
    const x0 = cx * CHUNK, y0 = cy * CHUNK, z0 = cz * CHUNK;
    const lanternAt = (i: number) => this.idx(x0 + (i & 31), y0 + (i >> 10), z0 + ((i >> 5) & 31));
    if (ch.blocks) for (let i = ch.blocks.indexOf(B.LANTERN); i >= 0; i = ch.blocks.indexOf(B.LANTERN, i + 1)) this.lanterns.delete(lanternAt(i));
    else if (ch.fill === B.LANTERN) for (let i = 0; i < CHUNK_VOL; i++) this.lanterns.delete(lanternAt(i));
    const slab = (a: Uint8Array | null) => { if (!a) return null; const s = this.core.alloc(); s.set(a); return s; };
    const old = [ch.blocks, ch.decor, ch.water];
    ch.blocks = slab(blocks); ch.fill = fill; ch.decor = slab(decor); ch.water = slab(water);
    this.syncTable(ch);
    for (const a of old) this.core.free(a);
    ch.seq = nextMeshSeq(); // in-flight jobs may have read the freed slabs
    blocks = ch.blocks;
    if (blocks) for (let i = blocks.indexOf(B.LANTERN); i >= 0; i = blocks.indexOf(B.LANTERN, i + 1)) this.lanterns.add(lanternAt(i));
    ch.modified = true;
    for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
      const n = this.chunk(cx + dx, cy + dy, cz + dz);
      if (n) n.dirty = true;
    }
    this.capDirty = true;
  }

  // does this chunk produce any geometry? (skips open sky and rock buried on all six sides)
  private needsMesh(ch: VChunk): boolean {
    if (ch.blocks || ch.decor) return true;
    if (ch.fill === B.AIR) return false;
    for (const [dx, dy, dz] of NB6) {
      if (ch.cy + dy < 0) continue; // bedrock below the world
      const n = this.chunk(ch.cx + dx, ch.cy + dy, ch.cz + dz);
      if (!n || n.blocks || n.fill === B.AIR) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  private columnNeighboursReady(cx: number, cz: number): boolean {
    for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
      const a = cx + dx, b = cz + dz;
      if (a >= 0 && b >= 0 && a < CX && b < CZ && this.colState[a + b * CX] !== 2) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  // per frame: request nearby columns and (re)mesh visible chunks, nearest first, within a time budget
  stream(px: number, pz: number, dt: number, budgetMs = 4): void {
    const t0 = performance.now();
    const pcx = Math.floor(px / CHUNK), pcz = Math.floor(pz / CHUNK);
    let queued = 0, syncGen = 0;
    const genR = this.genRadius + 0.5, renderR = this.renderRadius;
    for (const [dx, dz, d] of RINGS) {
      if (performance.now() - t0 > budgetMs || d > genR) break;
      const cx = pcx + dx, cz = pcz + dz;
      if (cx < 0 || cz < 0 || cx >= CX || cz >= CZ) continue;
      const ci = cx + cz * CX;
      if (this.colState[ci] !== 2) {
        // the player is about to walk into it: don't wait for a worker
        if (d <= 1.5 && syncGen < 1) { this.ensureColumn(cx, cz); syncGen++; }
        else this.requestColumn(cx, cz);
        continue;
      }
      if (d > renderR || !this.columnNeighboursReady(cx, cz)) continue;
      for (let cy = 0; cy < CY; cy++) {
        const ch = this.chunks[ci + cy * CXZ]!;
        if (!ch.dirty) continue;
        if (!this.needsMesh(ch)) { ch.dirty = false; this.disposeMeshes(ch); ch.seq = nextMeshSeq(); continue; } // (a job in flight is stale)
        // edits right next to the player remesh immediately; everything else goes to the workers
        if ((ch.mesh || ch.fol) && d <= 1.5) { ch.dirty = false; this.applyMesh(this.meshing.meshNow(meshJob(this, ch))); }
        else if (queued < 24 && !this.meshing.pending.has(ch.key)) { ch.dirty = false; this.meshing.request(meshJob(this, ch)); queued++; }
      }
    }
    this.unloadT -= dt;
    if (this.unloadT <= 0) {
      this.unloadT = 0.5;
      // drop meshes that are well outside the view; they rebuild when we come back
      for (const ch of this.meshed) {
        if (Math.hypot(ch.cx - pcx, ch.cz - pcz) <= renderR + 1.5) continue;
        this.disposeMeshes(ch); ch.dirty = true;
      }
      // forget far columns the player never touched
      let dropped = 0;
      for (let ci = 0; ci < CX * CZ && dropped < 8; ci++) {
        if (this.colState[ci] !== 2) continue;
        const cx = ci % CX, cz = Math.floor(ci / CX);
        if (Math.max(Math.abs(cx - pcx), Math.abs(cz - pcz)) <= this.unloadRadius || this.columnModified(cx, cz)) continue;
        if (this.game.physics && this.game.physics.columnInUse(cx, cz)) continue;
        this.dropColumn(cx, cz); dropped++;
      }
    }
  }

  /** mesh-queue priority: distance (in chunks) from the player, Infinity once out of view */
  meshRank(key: number): number {
    const ch = this.chunks[key];
    if (!ch) return Infinity;
    const p = this.game.focus();
    const dx = ch.cx + 0.5 - p.x / CHUNK, dz = ch.cz + 0.5 - p.z / CHUNK, dy = (ch.cy + 0.5 - p.y / CHUNK) * 0.5;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    return d > this.renderRadius + 1.5 ? Infinity : Math.hypot(d, dy);
  }

  private disposeMeshes(ch: VChunk): void {
    for (const m of [ch.mesh, ch.fol]) if (m) { this.group.remove(m); m.geometry.dispose(); }
    ch.mesh = ch.fol = null;
    this.meshed.delete(ch);
  }

  // install a mesh produced by the WASM mesher (main thread or worker)
  applyMesh(d: MeshData, force = false): void {
    const ch = this.chunks[d.key];
    if (!ch) return; // column unloaded while the job was in flight
    if (d.seq < ch.seq) return; // overtaken by a newer mesh, or read voxels that were replaced since
    const p = this.game.focus();
    if (!force && Math.hypot(ch.cx - Math.floor(p.x / CHUNK), ch.cz - Math.floor(p.z / CHUNK)) > this.renderRadius + 1.5) {
      ch.dirty = true; // left the view while in flight; mesh again when it comes back
      return;
    }
    this.disposeMeshes(ch);
    ch.seq = d.seq;
    const sg = solidGeometry(d), fg = foliageGeometry(d);
    const x0 = ch.cx * CHUNK, y0 = ch.cy * CHUNK, z0 = ch.cz * CHUNK;
    if (sg) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(sg, this.material);
      m.position.set(x0, y0, z0);
      m.castShadow = m.receiveShadow = true;
      m.matrixAutoUpdate = false; m.updateMatrix();
      this.group.add(m); ch.mesh = m;
    }
    if (fg) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(fg, this.foliageMat);
      m.position.set(x0, y0, z0);
      m.castShadow = m.receiveShadow = true;
      m.customDepthMaterial = this.foliageDepth;
      m.matrixAutoUpdate = false; m.updateMatrix();
      this.group.add(m); ch.fol = m;
    }
    if (ch.mesh || ch.fol) this.meshed.add(ch);
  }

  /** mesh every dirty chunk within `radius` columns on the main thread right now (start-up, loading) */
  rebuildAround(px: number, pz: number, radius = 2): void {
    const pcx = Math.floor(px / CHUNK), pcz = Math.floor(pz / CHUNK);
    for (const [dx, dz, d] of RINGS) {
      if (d > radius) continue;
      const cx = pcx + dx, cz = pcz + dz;
      if (!this.columnReady(cx, cz) || !this.columnNeighboursReady(cx, cz)) continue;
      for (let cy = 0; cy < CY; cy++) {
        const ch = this.chunk(cx, cy, cz)!;
        if (!ch.dirty) continue;
        ch.dirty = false;
        if (this.needsMesh(ch)) this.applyMesh(this.meshing.meshNow(meshJob(this, ch)), true);
        else { this.disposeMeshes(ch); ch.seq = nextMeshSeq(); }
      }
    }
  }

  // roof-cap lookup for a window around the player (rewritten when the window moves or the layer changes)
  updateCap(cutY: number, px: number, pz: number): void {
    const layer = cutY - 1;
    const ox = Math.floor(px / 16) * 16 - CAP_SIZE / 2, oz = Math.floor(pz / 16) * 16 - CAP_SIZE / 2;
    if (layer === this.capLayer && !this.capDirty && ox === this.capOX && oz === this.capOZ) return;
    this.capLayer = layer; this.capDirty = false; this.capOX = ox; this.capOZ = oz;
    sharedUniforms.uCapOrigin.value.set(ox, oz);
    const d = this.capData;
    const colors = capColors();
    for (let z = 0, k = 0; z < CAP_SIZE; z++) for (let x = 0; x < CAP_SIZE; x++, k += 4) {
      // below the world is bedrock, above it (or outside it) is air
      const t = this.get(ox + x, layer, oz + z);
      if (t === B.AIR) { d[k + 3] = 0; continue; }
      const c = t * 4;
      d[k] = colors[c]; d[k + 1] = colors[c + 1]; d[k + 2] = colors[c + 2]; d[k + 3] = 255;
    }
    this.capTex.needsUpdate = true;
  }

  /** y of the first air block above the highest solid block in a column (analytic height if not generated) */
  surfaceY(x: number, z: number): number {
    x = Math.floor(x); z = Math.floor(z);
    if (!this.isGenerated(x, z)) return Math.floor(this.gen.heightAt(x, z)) + 1;
    const base = (x >> 5) + (z >> 5) * CX, li = (x & 31) | ((z & 31) << 5);
    for (let cy = CY - 1; cy >= 0; cy--) {
      const ch = this.chunks[base + cy * CXZ]!;
      if (!ch.blocks) { if (ch.fill !== B.AIR) return cy * CHUNK + CHUNK; continue; }
      for (let ly = 31; ly >= 0; ly--) if (ch.blocks[li | (ly << 10)] !== B.AIR) return cy * CHUNK + ly + 1;
    }
    return 0;
  }

  // ---------------- queries ----------------
  // Amanatides & Woo voxel traversal
  raycast(o: THREE.Vector3, d: THREE.Vector3, maxDist = 100, skip: RaySkip | null = null): RayResult {
    let x = Math.floor(o.x), y = Math.floor(o.y), z = Math.floor(o.z);
    const sx = Math.sign(d.x), sy = Math.sign(d.y), sz = Math.sign(d.z);
    const tdx = sx ? Math.abs(1 / d.x) : Infinity, tdy = sy ? Math.abs(1 / d.y) : Infinity, tdz = sz ? Math.abs(1 / d.z) : Infinity;
    let tmx = sx ? ((sx > 0 ? x + 1 - o.x : o.x - x) * tdx) : Infinity;
    let tmy = sy ? ((sy > 0 ? y + 1 - o.y : o.y - y) * tdy) : Infinity;
    let tmz = sz ? ((sz > 0 ? z + 1 - o.z : o.z - z) * tdz) : Infinity;
    // a ray that starts inside a block reports the face it would have entered through
    const ax = Math.abs(d.x), ay = Math.abs(d.y), az = Math.abs(d.z);
    let t = 0, nx = 0, ny = 0, nz = 0;
    if (ax >= ay && ax >= az) nx = -sx; else if (ay >= az) ny = -sy; else nz = -sz;
    const maxSteps = Math.min(4096, Math.max(512, Math.ceil(maxDist * 2)));
    for (let i = 0; i < maxSteps; i++) {
      // the bedrock floor only exists under the world, not out in the void around it
      const bt = y < 0 && (x < 0 || z < 0 || x >= this.W || z >= this.D) ? B.AIR : this.get(x, y, z);
      if (bt !== B.AIR && !(y >= this.H) && !(skip && skip(x, y, z, bt))) {
        return { hit: true, x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, t, point: new THREE.Vector3(o.x + d.x * t, o.y + d.y * t, o.z + d.z * t) };
      }
      if (tmx < tmy && tmx < tmz) { x += sx; t = tmx; tmx += tdx; nx = -sx; ny = 0; nz = 0; }
      else if (tmy < tmz) { y += sy; t = tmy; tmy += tdy; nx = 0; ny = -sy; nz = 0; }
      else { z += sz; t = tmz; tmz += tdz; nx = 0; ny = 0; nz = -sz; }
      if (t > maxDist) break;
    }
    return { hit: false, t: maxDist };
  }

  // returns remaining-hp ratio or -1 when destroyed
  damageBlock(x: number, y: number, z: number, amount: number, cause = 'hit'): number {
    const t = this.get(x, y, z);
    if (t === B.AIR || !this.inBounds(x, y, z) || y === 0) return 1; // y = 0 is the bedrock floor
    const bd = BLOCKS[t];
    if (bd.hp === Infinity) return 1;
    const i = this.idx(x, y, z);
    const dmg = (this.damage.get(i) || 0) + amount;
    (this.game.lastDamagePos ||= new THREE.Vector3()).set(x + 0.5, y + 0.5, z + 0.5);
    if (dmg >= bd.hp) {
      this.set(x, y, z, B.AIR);
      this.game.onBlockDestroyed(x, y, z, t, cause);
      return -1;
    }
    this.damage.set(i, dmg);
    this.markDirty(x, y, z);
    return 1 - dmg / bd.hp;
  }

  // Structural integrity. Every non-terrain block needs a load path to the
  // ground: stacking on a supported block is free, while each horizontal or
  // hanging link costs 1. A block whose cheapest path exceeds its material's
  // span breaks off. Returns the unsupported blocks grouped into clusters.
  // `cells` are edited cells: their neighbours are checked, and the cells themselves when solid (a block just
  // placed or written back as rubble can itself be the one hanging in mid-air)
  findFloating(cells: Iterable<readonly number[]>): FloatingCell[][] {
    const seeds: number[] = [];
    for (const c of cells) for (let n = -1; n < 6; n++) {
      const sx = c[0] + (n < 0 ? 0 : NB6[n][0]), sy = c[1] + (n < 0 ? 0 : NB6[n][1]), sz = c[2] + (n < 0 ? 0 : NB6[n][2]);
      if (!this.inBounds(sx, sy, sz)) continue;
      const t = this.get(sx, sy, sz);
      if (t !== B.AIR && !BLOCKS[t].anchored) seeds.push(this.idx(sx, sy, sz));
    }
    return this.unsupported(seeds);
  }

  unsupported(seeds: number[]): FloatingCell[][] {
    const LIMIT = 6000;
    const out: FloatingCell[][] = [];
    // Dense scratch grids over a 128 x 64 x 128 window around the seeds (local index lx | lz << 7 | ly << 14),
    // with stamps instead of clearing: visit[i] === visitStamp (seen in an earlier component),
    // inComp[i] === compStamp (member of the current component). A component that runs into the window's
    // edge is gathered again with the window centred on what was found so far (a 45-block tower cut at its
    // foot reaches far above a window centred on the cut); only one that can't fit at all is left standing.
    const S = this.sup ??= { visit: new Int32Array(1 << 20), inComp: new Int32Array(1 << 20), cost: new Uint8Array(1 << 20), type: new Uint8Array(1 << 20), comp: new Int32Array(LIMIT + 8), stamp: 0 };
    const { visit, inComp, cost, type, comp } = S;
    const buckets = this.buckets;
    let ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0, haveWindow = false, visitStamp = 0;
    const processed: number[] = []; // global idx of every block in a finished component (for window changes)
    const done = new Set<number>();
    const NB = [1, -1, 1 << 14, -(1 << 14), 1 << 7, -(1 << 7)]; // local index offsets, NB6 order
    // move the window so its corner is (x0, y0, z0); blocks seen under the old window are remembered by global index
    const recentre = (x0: number, y0: number, z0: number) => {
      if (haveWindow) for (const g of processed) done.add(g);
      ox = x0; oy = y0; oz = z0; haveWindow = true;
      visitStamp = ++S.stamp;
    };
    for (const si of seeds) {
      const sx = this.ix(si), sy = this.iy(si), sz = this.iz(si);
      if (!haveWindow || sx - ox < 8 || sx - ox >= 120 || sy - oy < 8 || sy - oy >= 56 || sz - oz < 8 || sz - oz >= 120) recentre(sx - 64, sy - 32, sz - 64);
      if (done.size && done.has(si)) continue;
      if (visit[(sx - ox) | ((sz - oz) << 7) | ((sy - oy) << 14)] === visitStamp) continue;
      const t0 = this.get(sx, sy, sz);
      if (t0 === B.AIR || BLOCKS[t0].anchored) continue;
      // 1. gather the connected component of non-anchored blocks
      let compStamp = 0, len = 0, big = false;
      for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
        const s0 = (sx - ox) | ((sz - oz) << 7) | ((sy - oy) << 14);
        compStamp = ++S.stamp; len = 0; big = false;
        comp[len++] = s0; inComp[s0] = compStamp; type[s0] = t0; cost[s0] = NO_COST;
        let edge = false;
        for (let h = 0; h < len && !big; h++) {
          const ci = comp[h];
          const lx = ci & 127, lz = (ci >> 7) & 127, ly = ci >> 14;
          for (let n = 0; n < 6; n++) {
            const nx = lx + NB6[n][0], ny = ly + NB6[n][1], nz = lz + NB6[n][2];
            const t = this.get(nx + ox, ny + oy, nz + oz);
            if (t === B.AIR || BLOCKS[t].anchored) continue;
            // members stay off the window's outer layer, so the index offsets below never wrap
            if (nx < 1 || ny < 1 || nz < 1 || nx > 126 || ny > 62 || nz > 126) { big = edge = true; break; }
            const ni = ci + NB[n];
            if (inComp[ni] === compStamp) continue;
            if (len >= LIMIT) { big = true; break; }
            inComp[ni] = compStamp; type[ni] = t; cost[ni] = NO_COST; comp[len++] = ni;
          }
        }
        if (!edge || attempt >= 3) break;
        // re-centre on the bounding box gathered so far (it always holds the seed), unless it can't fit anyway
        let x0 = 127, y0 = 63, z0 = 127, x1 = 0, y1 = 0, z1 = 0;
        for (let h = 0; h < len; h++) {
          const ci = comp[h], lx = ci & 127, lz = (ci >> 7) & 127, ly = ci >> 14;
          if (lx < x0) x0 = lx; if (lx > x1) x1 = lx; if (ly < y0) y0 = ly; if (ly > y1) y1 = ly; if (lz < z0) z0 = lz; if (lz > z1) z1 = lz;
        }
        if (x1 - x0 > 120 || y1 - y0 > 56 || z1 - z0 > 120) break;
        recentre(ox + ((x0 + x1) >> 1) - 64, oy + ((y0 + y1) >> 1) - 32, oz + ((z0 + z1) >> 1) - 64);
      }
      for (let h = 0; h < len; h++) { visit[comp[h]] = visitStamp; processed.push(this.idx((comp[h] & 127) + ox, (comp[h] >> 14) + oy, ((comp[h] >> 7) & 127) + oz)); }
      if (big) continue;
      // 2. bucketed 0-1 shortest path from blocks touching terrain
      for (const bk of buckets) bk.length = 0;
      let supported = 0;
      for (let h = 0; h < len; h++) {
        const ci = comp[h];
        const x = (ci & 127) + ox, z = ((ci >> 7) & 127) + oz, y = (ci >> 14) + oy;
        const below = this.get(x, y - 1, z);
        if (below !== B.AIR && BLOCKS[below].anchored) { cost[ci] = 0; supported++; buckets[0].push(ci); continue; }
        for (let n = 0; n < 6; n++) {
          if (NB6[n][1] < 0) continue;
          const t = this.get(x + NB6[n][0], y + NB6[n][1], z + NB6[n][2]);
          if (t !== B.AIR && BLOCKS[t].anchored) { cost[ci] = 1; supported++; buckets[1].push(ci); break; }
        }
      }
      for (let c = 0; c < buckets.length; c++) {
        const bucket = buckets[c];
        for (let k = 0; k < bucket.length; k++) {
          const ci = bucket[k];
          if (cost[ci] !== c) continue;
          for (let n = 0; n < 6; n++) {
            const ni = ci + NB[n];
            if (inComp[ni] !== compStamp) continue;
            // stacking is free and always holds: a block resting on a supported one is supported whatever its own
            // span (a lantern or crate on top of a wall 2 links from the ground used to count as over its span of
            // 1, fell, came to rest on the same wall, was written back and fell again, forever)
            const up = NB6[n][1] > 0, nc = c + (up ? 0 : 1);
            if (!up && nc > BLOCKS[type[ni]].span) continue;
            const old = cost[ni];
            if (old !== NO_COST && old <= nc) continue;
            if (old === NO_COST) supported++;
            cost[ni] = nc;
            buckets[nc].push(ni);
          }
        }
      }
      if (supported === len) continue;
      // 3. split unsupported blocks into connected falling clusters
      // (cost === NO_COST: loose and not yet taken; TAKEN: already in a cluster)
      for (let h = 0; h < len; h++) {
        const start = comp[h];
        if (cost[start] !== NO_COST) continue;
        cost[start] = TAKEN;
        const cl: FloatingCell[] = [];
        const queue = [start];
        for (let qi = 0; qi < queue.length; qi++) {
          const ci = queue[qi];
          cl.push([(ci & 127) + ox, (ci >> 14) + oy, ((ci >> 7) & 127) + oz, type[ci]]);
          for (let n = 0; n < 6; n++) {
            const ni = ci + NB[n];
            if (inComp[ni] === compStamp && cost[ni] === NO_COST) { cost[ni] = TAKEN; queue.push(ni); }
          }
        }
        out.push(cl);
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  // ---------------- fire ----------------
  ignite(x: number, y: number, z: number, force = false): boolean {
    const t = this.get(x, y, z);
    if (t === B.AIR) return false;
    const bd = BLOCKS[t];
    if (!bd.flammable) return false;
    const i = this.idx(x, y, z);
    if (this.burning.has(i) || this.burning.size > 450) return false;
    if (!force && Math.random() > bd.flammable) return false;
    this.burning.set(i, bd.burnTime * (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.6));
    this.markDirty(x, y, z);
    return true;
  }

  updateFire(dt: number): void {
    this.fireTick += dt;
    const burning = this.burning;
    if (!burning.size) { if (this.fireTick >= 0.2) this.fireTick = 0; return; }
    const g = this.game;
    // visual emission every frame
    let n = 0;
    const emitP = Math.min(1, 140 / Math.max(1, burning.size));
    for (const i of burning.keys()) {
      if (Math.random() > emitP * dt * 30) continue;
      const x = this.ix(i), z = this.iz(i), y = this.iy(i);
      g.particles.fire(x + 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.6, y + 0.9 + Math.random() * 0.3, z + 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.6);
      if (++n > 200) break;
    }
    if (this.fireTick < 0.2) return;
    const step = this.fireTick; this.fireTick = 0;
    const done: number[] = []; // block indices that finished burning
    const spread: number[] = []; // flat x, y, z triples
    for (const [i, tl] of burning) {
      const x = this.ix(i), z = this.iz(i), y = this.iy(i);
      const left = tl - step;
      if (left <= 0) { done.push(i); continue; }
      burning.set(i, left);
      for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
        if (Math.random() > 0.6) continue;
        // fire climbs: bias spreading upward
        const dx = ((Math.random() * 3) | 0) - 1, dy = ((Math.random() * 3) | 0) - 1 + (Math.random() < 0.35 ? 1 : 0), dz = ((Math.random() * 3) | 0) - 1;
        spread.push(x + dx, y + dy, z + dz);
      }
      // grass fire also lights decor-covered neighbours
      g.burnEntitiesNear(x + 0.5, y + 1, z + 0.5, step);
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < spread.length; k += 3) this.ignite(spread[k], spread[k + 1], spread[k + 2]);
    const removed: number[][] = [];
    for (const i of done) {
      const x = this.ix(i), z = this.iz(i), y = this.iy(i);
      burning.delete(i);
      const t = this.get(x, y, z);
      if (!BLOCKS[t].flammable) continue; // replaced by something that cannot burn while it was alight
      if (t === B.GRASS) { this.set(x, y, z, B.SCORCH); continue; }
      if (t === B.TNT || t === B.BARREL) { this.set(x, y, z, B.AIR); g.onBlockDestroyed(x, y, z, t, 'fire'); continue; }
      this.set(x, y, z, B.AIR);
      g.particles.smokePuff(x + 0.5, y + 0.5, z + 0.5, 3);
      removed.push([x, y, z]);
    }
    if (removed.length) g.checkSupport(removed);
  }
}
```

### src/worldgen.ts
```
// Deterministic, column-by-column world generation for a 5120 x 512 x 5120 world.
// Everything is a pure function of the seed: terrain height comes from noise, structure
// sites are chosen per 192-block region by hashing, and every builder writes through a
// writer clipped to the column being generated. A castle that spans four columns is
// therefore built identically no matter which column happens to be generated first.
// Output is plain typed arrays (one entry per 32³ chunk; uniform chunks store no array),
// Chunk arrays come from `slabs`: plain arrays by default, or views into the shared voxel
// store (see core.ts) so a worker writes a column straight into memory the main thread reads.
import { B, WATER_LEVEL, hash3, fbm, ridged } from './blocks';

export const CHUNK = 32;
const CV = CHUNK * CHUNK * CHUNK;
const REGION = 192;

export type SiteKind = 'castle' | 'ruin' | 'hut' | 'tower' | 'reservoir';
export interface Site {
  kind: SiteKind;
  x: number; z: number;
  g: number; // ground level (y of the first air block) at the site
  r: number; // footprint radius used for terrain flattening and keep-out
}

/** One generated 32-wide column of 32³ chunks, bottom (cy = 0) to top. */
export interface Column {
  cx: number; cz: number;
  blocks: (Uint8Array | null)[]; // null: every block in the chunk is fill[cy]
  fill: Uint8Array;
  decor: (Uint8Array | null)[]; // null: no decor
  water: (Uint8Array | null)[]; // water levels (FULL = 8, 0x80 = infinite source); null: dry
}

const WATER_FULL = 8, WATER_SOURCE = 0x80;
const SEA = WATER_LEVEL;
const smooth = (x: number, a: number, b: number) => { const t = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (x - a) / (b - a))); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };
const NO_SITES: Site[] = [];

/** where chunk arrays come from (a shared-memory slab allocator in the game) */
export interface SlabSource { alloc(): Uint8Array; free(a: Uint8Array): void }
const plainSlabs: SlabSource = { alloc: () => new Uint8Array(CV), free: () => {} };

export class WorldGen {
  W: number; H: number; D: number; seed: number;
  CY: number;
  /** small flat test area (see testHeight) instead of the full world */
  test: boolean;
  spawn: Site; // the starting castle in the middle of the map
  slabs: SlabSource = plainSlabs;
  private regions = new Map<number, Site[]>(); // sites per region
  private near = new Map<number, Site[]>(); // sites per region, including the 8 neighbouring regions
  // the column being generated
  private col!: Column;
  private x0 = 0; private x1 = 0; private z0 = 0; private z1 = 0;

  constructor(W: number, H: number, D: number, seed: number, test = false) {
    this.W = W; this.H = H; this.D = D; this.seed = seed; this.test = test;
    this.CY = Math.ceil(H / CHUNK);
    const sx = W >> 1, sz = D >> 1;
    this.spawn = { kind: 'castle', x: sx, z: sz, g: Math.max(SEA + 3, Math.floor(this.rawHeight(sx, sz)) + 1), r: 16 };
  }

  // ---------- terrain ----------
  /** 0 = open sea, 1 = land */
  landAt(x: number, z: number): number {
    const { W, D, seed } = this;
    const ex = Math.max(Math.abs(x / W - 0.5), Math.abs(z / D - 0.5)) * 2; // 0 at the centre, 1 at the world edge
    const ds = Math.hypot(x - (W >> 1), z - (D >> 1));
    let c = (fbm(x * 0.0011, z * 0.0011, seed + 5, 5) - 0.5) * 2.6 + 0.3;
    c += (fbm(x * 0.006, z * 0.006, seed + 21, 3) - 0.5) * 0.35; // ragged coastline
    c -= smooth(ex, 0.72, 0.97) * 1.4; // ocean all around the edge of the world
    c += (1 - smooth(ds, 140, 460)) * 0.9; // always land around the spawn castle
    return smooth(c, -0.08, 0.2);
  }

  /** mountain-belt strength 0..1 (no mountains close to spawn) */
  beltAt(x: number, z: number): number {
    const ds = Math.hypot(x - (this.W >> 1), z - (this.D >> 1));
    return smooth(fbm(x * 0.0016 + 7, z * 0.0016, this.seed + 60, 3), 0.47, 0.64) * smooth(ds, 240, 650);
  }

  // Test world: a flat 224-block square around spawn in an open sea, with a 24-block cliff
  // plateau (falls, climbing, collapses off edges) and a pond below sea level (water).
  private testHeight(x: number, z: number): number {
    const dx = x - (this.W >> 1), dz = z - (this.D >> 1);
    if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dz)) > 112) return SEA - 8;
    if (dx >= 44 && dx < 84 && dz >= 20 && dz < 60) return SEA + 26;
    // pond with stepped banks (water fills to sea level - 1), so you can wade back out
    const pr = Math.hypot(dx + 30, dz + 30);
    if (pr < 4) return SEA - 3;
    if (pr < 5.5) return SEA - 1;
    if (pr < 7) return SEA;
    if (pr < 8.5) return SEA + 1;
    return SEA + 2;
  }

  rawHeight(x: number, z: number): number {
    if (this.test) return this.testHeight(x, z);
    const seed = this.seed;
    const land = this.landAt(x, z);
    let h = SEA - 26 + land * 29; // sea floor ~22, coast ~51
    if (land > 0) {
      const plains = fbm(x * 0.03, z * 0.03, seed, 4) * 7;
      const hills = Math.pow(fbm(x * 0.008 + 50, z * 0.008 + 50, seed + 9, 4), 2.2) * 50;
      const belt = this.beltAt(x, z);
      let mtn = 0;
      if (belt > 0) mtn = (Math.pow(ridged(x * 0.0032, z * 0.0032, seed + 40, 5), 1.7) * 560 + 24) * belt;
      const lakes = smooth(fbm(x * 0.01 + 300, z * 0.01, seed + 31, 3), 0.64, 0.72) * (1 - belt);
      h += land * (plains + hills + mtn - lakes * 13);
    }
    return Math.max(2, Math.min(this.H - 26, h));
  }

  // width of the ramp from a structure's flat pad back to natural terrain (the spawn castle sits in a hill)
  private blend(s: Site): number { return s.kind === 'castle' ? 24 : 8; }

  private heightWith(x: number, z: number, sites: Site[]): number {
    let h = this.rawHeight(x, z);
    for (const s of sites) {
      if (s.kind === 'reservoir') continue;
      const d = Math.hypot(x - s.x, z - s.z);
      const k = 1 - smooth(d, s.r, s.r + this.blend(s));
      if (k > 0) h = h + (s.g - 0.8 - h) * k;
    }
    return h;
  }
  /** terrain height with flattened pads under structures */
  heightAt(x: number, z: number): number { return this.heightWith(x, z, this.sitesNear(x, z)); }

  /** surface block for a column of height h whose steepest neighbour differs by `slope` */
  topBlock(x: number, z: number, h: number, slope: number): number {
    if (h <= SEA + 1) return B.SAND;
    const n = fbm(x * 0.02, z * 0.02, this.seed + 70, 2);
    if (h > 290 + n * 60) return B.SNOW;
    if (slope >= 3 || h > 200 + n * 50) return B.STONE;
    return B.GRASS;
  }
  private slopeAt(x: number, z: number, h: number): number {
    return Math.max(Math.abs(Math.floor(this.heightAt(x + 1, z)) - h), Math.abs(Math.floor(this.heightAt(x - 1, z)) - h),
      Math.abs(Math.floor(this.heightAt(x, z + 1)) - h), Math.abs(Math.floor(this.heightAt(x, z - 1)) - h));
  }

  // ---------- structure sites ----------
  // flat land at a sensible height (structures need a level pad, not a cliff)
  private flatLand(x: number, z: number, min: number, max: number, r: number): boolean {
    const h = this.rawHeight(x, z);
    if (h <= min || h >= max) return false;
    for (const [dx, dz] of [[r, 0], [-r, 0], [0, r], [0, -r]]) if (Math.abs(this.rawHeight(x + dx, z + dz) - h) > 5) return false;
    return true;
  }

  private regionSites(rx: number, rz: number): Site[] {
    const key = rx + rz * 4096;
    let out = this.regions.get(key);
    if (out) return out;
    out = [];
    this.regions.set(key, out);
    const { seed } = this;
    const sp = this.spawn;
    if (rx < 0 || rz < 0 || rx * REGION >= this.W || rz * REGION >= this.D) return out;
    // the spawn castle belongs to the region it stands in
    if (Math.floor(sp.x / REGION) === rx && Math.floor(sp.z / REGION) === rz) out.push(sp);
    if (this.test) {
      // one of each structure at fixed spots around the castle
      const sx = sp.x, sz = sp.z;
      for (const [kind, x, z, r] of [['tower', sx + 34, sz - 12, 4], ['hut', sx - 36, sz + 8, 6], ['ruin', sx - 4, sz - 60, 13], ['reservoir', sx + 64, sz + 40, 6]] as [SiteKind, number, number, number][])
        if (Math.floor(x / REGION) === rx && Math.floor(z / REGION) === rz) out.push({ kind, x, z, g: Math.floor(this.rawHeight(x, z)) + 1, r });
      return out;
    }
    // at most one group per region: a ruined fort, a hut village, a watchtower or a dammed reservoir
    const r = hash3(rx, rz, seed + 101);
    const kind: SiteKind | null = r < 0.12 ? 'ruin' : r < 0.4 ? 'hut' : r < 0.55 ? 'tower' : r < 0.68 ? 'reservoir' : null;
    if (!kind) return out;
    const rad = kind === 'ruin' ? 13 : kind === 'hut' ? 6 : kind === 'tower' ? 4 : 6;
    const minDist = kind === 'ruin' ? 140 : kind === 'hut' ? 60 : kind === 'tower' ? 50 : 60;
    const margin = kind === 'hut' ? 44 : rad + 26;
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 6; attempt++) {
      const x = rx * REGION + margin + Math.floor(hash3(rx * 8 + attempt, rz, seed + 102) * (REGION - 2 * margin));
      const z = rz * REGION + margin + Math.floor(hash3(rx, rz * 8 + attempt, seed + 103) * (REGION - 2 * margin));
      if (Math.hypot(x - sp.x, z - sp.z) < minDist + sp.r) continue;
      if (x < 40 || z < 40 || x > this.W - 40 || z > this.D - 40) continue;
      const ok = kind === 'reservoir' ? this.flatLand(x, z, SEA + 14, SEA + 60, 5) : this.flatLand(x, z, SEA + 2, SEA + 30, rad);
      if (!ok) continue;
      const add = (k: SiteKind, sx: number, sz: number) => out!.push({ kind: k, x: sx, z: sz, g: Math.floor(this.rawHeight(sx, sz)) + 1, r: rad });
      add(kind, x, z);
      // huts come in small villages: a few more around the first one
      if (kind === 'hut') for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        const a = hash3(rx * 4 + k, rz, seed + 7) * Math.PI * 2, hx = Math.round(x + Math.cos(a) * 16), hz = Math.round(z + Math.sin(a) * 16);
        if (this.flatLand(hx, hz, SEA + 2, SEA + 30, rad) && out.every(s => Math.hypot(s.x - hx, s.z - hz) > s.r + rad + 2)) add('hut', hx, hz);
      }
      break;
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** every site whose footprint (plus its terrain blend) can reach (x, z) */
  sitesNear(x: number, z: number): Site[] {
    const rx = Math.floor(x / REGION), rz = Math.floor(z / REGION);
    const key = rx + rz * 4096;
    let s = this.near.get(key);
    if (s) return s;
    s = [];
    for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) s.push(...this.regionSites(rx + dx, rz + dz));
    if (!s.length) s = NO_SITES;
    this.near.set(key, s);
    return s;
  }

  private nearSite(x: number, z: number, pad: number): boolean {
    for (const s of this.sitesNear(x, z)) if (Math.hypot(x - s.x, z - s.z) < s.r + pad) return true;
    return false;
  }

  // ---------- clipped writes into the current column ----------
  private chunkFor(y: number): Uint8Array {
    const c = this.col, cy = y >> 5;
    let b = c.blocks[cy];
    if (!b) { b = c.blocks[cy] = this.slabs.alloc(); if (c.fill[cy]) b.fill(c.fill[cy]); }
    return b;
  }
  private put(x: number, y: number, z: number, t: number): void {
    if (x < this.x0 || x >= this.x1 || z < this.z0 || z >= this.z1 || y < 0 || y >= this.H) return;
    const c = this.col, cy = y >> 5, li = (x - this.x0) | ((z - this.z0) << 5) | ((y & 31) << 10);
    if (!c.blocks[cy] && c.fill[cy] === t) return;
    this.chunkFor(y)[li] = t;
    if (t !== B.AIR) {
      const d = c.decor[cy]; if (d) d[li] = 0;
      const wv = c.water[cy]; if (wv) wv[li] = 0;
    }
  }
  private get(x: number, y: number, z: number): number {
    if (x < this.x0 || x >= this.x1 || z < this.z0 || z >= this.z1 || y < 0 || y >= this.H) return B.AIR;
    const c = this.col, cy = y >> 5, b = c.blocks[cy];
    return b ? b[(x - this.x0) | ((z - this.z0) << 5) | ((y & 31) << 10)] : c.fill[cy];
  }
  private putWater(x: number, y: number, z: number, v = WATER_FULL): void {
    if (x < this.x0 || x >= this.x1 || z < this.z0 || z >= this.z1 || y < 1 || y >= this.H) return;
    if (this.get(x, y, z) !== B.AIR) return;
    const c = this.col, cy = y >> 5;
    (c.water[cy] ??= this.slabs.alloc())[(x - this.x0) | ((z - this.z0) << 5) | ((y & 31) << 10)] = v;
  }
  private putDecor(x: number, y: number, z: number, d: number): void {
    const c = this.col, cy = y >> 5;
    (c.decor[cy] ??= this.slabs.alloc())[(x - this.x0) | ((z - this.z0) << 5) | ((y & 31) << 10)] = d;
  }
  private box(x0: number, y0: number, z0: number, x1: number, y1: number, z1: number, t: number): void {
    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) for (let z = z0; z <= z1; z++) for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) this.put(x, y, z, t);
  }

  // ---------- column generation ----------
  generateColumn(cx: number, cz: number): Column {
    const { H, CY, seed } = this;
    const col: Column = { cx, cz, blocks: new Array(CY).fill(null), fill: new Uint8Array(CY), decor: new Array(CY).fill(null), water: new Array(CY).fill(null) };
    this.col = col;
    const x0 = cx * CHUNK, z0 = cz * CHUNK;
    this.x0 = x0; this.z0 = z0; this.x1 = Math.min(x0 + CHUNK, this.W); this.z1 = Math.min(z0 + CHUNK, this.D);
    // 1. heights, with a one-block margin for slopes
    const P = CHUNK + 2, hh = new Int16Array(P * P);
    const sites = this.sitesNear(x0 + 16, z0 + 16);
    for (let lz = 0; lz < P; lz++) for (let lx = 0; lx < P; lx++)
      hh[lx + lz * P] = Math.min(H - 26, Math.floor(this.heightWith(x0 + lx - 1, z0 + lz - 1, sites)));
    let minH = H, maxH = 0;
    for (let lz = 1; lz <= CHUNK; lz++) for (let lx = 1; lx <= CHUNK; lx++) { const h = hh[lx + lz * P]; if (h < minH) minH = h; if (h > maxH) maxH = h; }
    // 2. chunks entirely inside the rock are uniform stone; chunks above everything start as uniform air
    for (let cy = 0; cy < CY; cy++) {
      const ya = cy * CHUNK, yb = ya + CHUNK - 1;
      if (cy > 0 && yb < minH - 4) col.fill[cy] = B.STONE;
      else if (ya > maxH) col.fill[cy] = B.AIR;
      else col.blocks[cy] = this.slabs.alloc();
    }
    for (let lz = 0; lz < CHUNK; lz++) for (let lx = 0; lx < CHUNK; lx++) {
      const x = x0 + lx, z = z0 + lz, c = (lx + 1) + (lz + 1) * P;
      const h = hh[c];
      const slope = Math.max(Math.abs(hh[c + 1] - h), Math.abs(hh[c - 1] - h), Math.abs(hh[c + P] - h), Math.abs(hh[c - P] - h));
      const top = this.topBlock(x, z, h, slope);
      const under = top === B.GRASS ? B.DIRT : top === B.SNOW ? B.STONE : top;
      const colIdx = lx | (lz << 5);
      for (let cy = 0; cy < CY; cy++) {
        const b = col.blocks[cy];
        if (!b) continue;
        const ya = cy * CHUNK, yb = Math.min(h, ya + CHUNK - 1);
        for (let y = ya; y <= yb; y++) {
          let t: number;
          if (y === 0) t = B.BEDROCK;
          else if (y === h) t = top;
          else if (y > h - 3) t = under;
          else t = (y > 3 && hash3(x, y, z + seed) < 0.035) ? B.ORE : B.STONE;
          b[colIdx | ((y & 31) << 10)] = t;
        }
      }
      // ore poking out of rocky hilltops
      if (top === B.STONE && hash3(x, 7, z + seed) < 0.03) this.put(x, h, z, B.ORE);
      // the sea is an infinite water source
      for (let y = h + 1; y < SEA; y++) this.putWater(x, y, z, WATER_FULL | WATER_SOURCE);
    }
    // 3. structures overlapping this column
    for (const s of sites) {
      const reach = s.r + 6;
      if (s.x + reach < x0 || s.x - reach >= this.x1 || s.z + reach < z0 || s.z - reach >= this.z1) continue;
      if (s.kind === 'castle') this.castle(s.x, s.z, s.g, false);
      else if (s.kind === 'ruin') this.castle(s.x, s.z, s.g, true);
      else if (s.kind === 'hut') this.hut(s.x, s.z, s.g);
      else if (s.kind === 'tower') this.tower(s.x, s.z, s.g);
      else this.reservoir(s.x, s.z);
    }
    // 4. trees (a canopy can reach 3 blocks into neighbouring columns)
    for (let z = z0 - 3; z < this.z1 + 3; z++) for (let x = x0 - 3; x < this.x1 + 3; x++) {
      const y = this.treeAt(x, z);
      if (y > 0) this.tree(x, y, z);
    }
    // 5. ground decoration and scattered barrels
    const scanTop = Math.min(H - 2, maxH + 24);
    for (let z = z0; z < this.z1; z++) for (let x = x0; x < this.x1; x++) {
      let y = scanTop;
      while (y > 0 && this.get(x, y, z) === B.AIR) y--;
      const top = this.get(x, y, z), ya = y + 1;
      if (ya >= H || this.get(x, ya, z) !== B.AIR) continue;
      const wv = col.water[ya >> 5];
      if (wv && wv[(x - x0) | ((z - z0) << 5) | ((ya & 31) << 10)]) continue;
      if ((top === B.GRASS || top === B.SAND || top === B.STONE) && ya > SEA + 1 && hash3(x, z, seed + 90) < 0.0016) { this.put(x, ya, z, B.BARREL); continue; }
      if (top !== B.GRASS) continue;
      const forest = fbm(x * 0.05 + 100, z * 0.05, seed + 3, 2);
      if (hash3(x, z, seed + 78) < 0.38) {
        const r3 = hash3(x, z, seed + 79);
        let d = 1 + ((r3 * 3) | 0); // grass tufts 1..3
        if (r3 > 0.86) d = 4 + ((hash3(x, z, 5) * 3) | 0); // flowers
        else if (r3 > 0.83 && forest > 0.5) d = 7; // mushroom
        else if (r3 > 0.8) d = 8; // fern
        this.putDecor(x, ya, z, d);
      }
    }
    // 6. chunks that ended up holding a single block type go back to uniform storage
    for (let cy = 0; cy < CY; cy++) {
      const b = col.blocks[cy];
      if (!b || col.decor[cy]) continue;
      const t = b[0];
      let same = true;
      for (let i = 1; i < CV; i++) if (b[i] !== t) { same = false; break; }
      if (same) { this.slabs.free(b); col.blocks[cy] = null; col.fill[cy] = t; }
    }
    return col;
  }

  // tree base height at (x,z), or 0 for none
  treeAt(x: number, z: number): number {
    if (x < 3 || z < 3 || x >= this.W - 3 || z >= this.D - 3) return 0;
    const r = hash3(x, z, this.seed + 77);
    if (r > 0.04) return 0;
    const forest = fbm(x * 0.05 + 100, z * 0.05, this.seed + 3, 2);
    if (r >= (forest > 0.56 ? 0.035 : 0.004)) return 0;
    const h = Math.min(this.H - 26, Math.floor(this.heightAt(x, z)));
    if (h > 190 || this.topBlock(x, z, h, this.slopeAt(x, z, h)) !== B.GRASS || this.nearSite(x, z, 4)) return 0;
    return h + 1;
  }

  // ---------- builders ----------
  private tree(x: number, y: number, z: number): void {
    const h = 4 + ((hash3(x, z, 3) * 3) | 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < h; i++) this.put(x, y + i, z, B.LOG);
    const top = y + h;
    for (let dy = -2; dy <= 1; dy++) {
      const rad = dy === 1 ? 1 : (dy === -2 ? 1.6 : 2.4);
      for (let dz = -3; dz <= 3; dz++) for (let dx = -3; dx <= 3; dx++) {
        if (dx * dx + dz * dz > rad * rad + hash3(x + dx, top + dy, z + dz) * 1.2) continue;
        if (this.get(x + dx, top + dy, z + dz) === B.AIR) this.put(x + dx, top + dy, z + dz, B.LEAVES);
      }
    }
  }

  // stepped gable roof; each row overlaps the one below so it stays connected
  private gableRoof(cx: number, cz: number, y0: number, halfX: number, halfZ: number): void {
    for (let st = 0; st <= halfZ; st++) for (let dx = -halfX; dx <= halfX; dx++) {
      for (const z of [cz - halfZ + st, cz + halfZ - st]) {
        this.put(cx + dx, y0 + st, z, B.ROOF);
        if (st > 0) this.put(cx + dx, y0 + st - 1, z, B.ROOF);
      }
    }
  }

  private castle(cx: number, cz: number, g: number, ruined: boolean): void {
    const R = ruined ? 7 : 9;
    // a ruin has bites taken out of it (deterministic per block)
    // (a column only keeps a block if every block below it survived, so no bricks float)
    const keep = (x: number, y: number, z: number) => {
      if (!ruined) return true;
      for (let yy = g + 1; yy <= y; yy++) if (hash3(x, yy, z + 991) <= 0.18 + (yy - g) * 0.06) return false;
      return true;
    };
    const wall = (x: number, y: number, z: number, t: number) => { if (keep(x, y, z)) this.put(x, y, z, t); };
    this.box(cx - R, g - 1, cz - R, cx + R, g - 1, cz + R, B.COBBLE);
    this.box(cx - R, g, cz - R, cx + R, g + 10, cz + R, B.AIR);
    for (let i = -R; i <= R; i++) {
      for (const [x, z] of [[cx + i, cz - R], [cx + i, cz + R], [cx - R, cz + i], [cx + R, cz + i]]) {
        const gate = (z === cz + R && Math.abs(x - cx) <= 1) || (x === cx - R && Math.abs(z - cz) <= 1);
        for (let y = g; y < g + 5; y++) if (!gate || (y >= g + 3 && !ruined)) wall(x, y, z, B.BRICK); // ruined gates are open to the sky
        if ((i & 1) === 0 && !(gate && ruined)) wall(x, g + 5, z, B.BRICK);
      }
    }
    for (const [tx, tz] of [[cx - R, cz - R], [cx + R, cz - R], [cx - R, cz + R], [cx + R, cz + R]]) {
      for (let y = g; y < g + 9; y++) for (let dz = -2; dz <= 2; dz++) for (let dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++) {
        const edge = Math.abs(dx) === 2 || Math.abs(dz) === 2;
        if (Math.abs(dx) === 2 && Math.abs(dz) === 2) continue;
        if (edge) wall(tx + dx, y, tz + dz, B.BRICK);
        else if (y === g + 5 && !ruined) this.put(tx + dx, y, tz + dz, B.PLANKS);
        else this.put(tx + dx, y, tz + dz, B.AIR);
      }
      for (let dz = -2; dz <= 2; dz++) for (let dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++)
        if ((dx + dz) % 2 === 0 && (Math.abs(dx) === 2) !== (Math.abs(dz) === 2)) wall(tx + dx, g + 9, tz + dz, B.BRICK);
      if (!ruined) this.put(tx, g + 6, tz, B.LANTERN);
    }
    // moat ring just outside the walls, with land bridges at both gates
    for (let z = cz - R - 5; z <= cz + R + 5; z++) for (let x = cx - R - 5; x <= cx + R + 5; x++) {
      const d = Math.max(Math.abs(x - cx), Math.abs(z - cz));
      if (d < R + 3 || d > R + 4) continue;
      if ((Math.abs(x - cx) <= 1 && z > cz) || (Math.abs(z - cz) <= 1 && x < cx)) continue;
      for (let y = g - 1; y >= g - 2; y--) { this.put(x, y, z, B.AIR); this.putWater(x, y, z); }
    }
    if (ruined) {
      // overgrown courtyard with a loot stash
      for (const [dx, dz] of [[-3, -3], [3, 2], [-2, 4]]) this.put(cx + dx, g, cz + dz, B.CRATE);
      this.put(cx + 1, g, cz - 3, B.TNT); this.put(cx + 4, g, cz - 3, B.BARREL);
      return;
    }
    // central keep with a pitched roof
    const k = 3;
    for (let y = g; y < g + 6; y++) for (let dz = -k; dz <= k; dz++) for (let dx = -k; dx <= k; dx++) {
      const edge = Math.abs(dx) === k || Math.abs(dz) === k;
      const door = dz === k && dx === 0 && y < g + 2;
      const window = edge && y === g + 3 && (dx === 0 || dz === 0) && !door;
      if (edge && !door && !window) this.put(cx + dx, y, cz + dz, (y === g || y === g + 5) ? B.COBBLE : B.PLANKS);
    }
    this.box(cx - k, g + 6, cz - k, cx + k, g + 6, cz + k, B.PLANKS);
    this.gableRoof(cx, cz, g + 7, k + 1, k + 1);
    this.put(cx, g, cz, B.LANTERN);
    const stash = [[-6, -6], [-5, -6], [-6, -5], [6, 6], [5, 6], [6, -6], [-6, 6]];
    stash.forEach(([dx, dz], i) => {
      this.put(cx + dx, g, cz + dz, i % 3 === 2 ? B.TNT : B.CRATE);
      if (i % 2 === 0) this.put(cx + dx, g + 1, cz + dz, B.CRATE);
    });
    this.put(cx + 6, g, cz - 5, B.TNT);
    for (const [dx, dz] of [[-6, 2], [-6, 3], [6, 2], [2, -6], [-2, 6], [3, 6]]) this.put(cx + dx, g, cz + dz, B.BARREL);
    this.put(cx + 6, g + 1, cz - 6, B.TNT);
  }

  private hut(x: number, z: number, g: number): void {
    const s = 3;
    this.box(x - s, g - 1, z - s, x + s, g - 1, z + s, B.PLANKS);
    this.box(x - s - 1, g, z - s - 1, x + s + 1, g + 9, z + s + 1, B.AIR);
    for (let y = g; y < g + 4; y++) for (let dz = -s; dz <= s; dz++) for (let dx = -s; dx <= s; dx++) {
      const edge = Math.abs(dx) === s || Math.abs(dz) === s;
      const corner = Math.abs(dx) === s && Math.abs(dz) === s;
      const door = dz === s && dx === 0 && y < g + 2;
      const win = edge && !corner && y === g + 2 && (dx === 0 || dz === 0) && dz !== s;
      if (edge && !door && !win) this.put(x + dx, y, z + dz, corner ? B.LOG : B.PLANKS);
    }
    this.gableRoof(x, z, g + 4, s + 1, s + 1);
    this.put(x - 2, g, z - 2, B.CRATE);
    this.put(x + 2, g, z - 2, B.CRATE);
    this.put(x + 2, g + 1, z - 2, B.TNT);
    this.put(x - s - 1, g, z + 1, B.BARREL); this.put(x - s - 1, g, z + 2, B.BARREL);
    this.put(x + 2, g, z + 2, B.LANTERN);
  }

  private tower(x: number, z: number, g: number): void {
    for (let y = g; y < g + 8; y++) for (const [dx, dz] of [[-1, -1], [1, -1], [-1, 1], [1, 1]]) this.put(x + dx, y, z + dz, B.LOG);
    this.box(x - 2, g + 8, z - 2, x + 2, g + 8, z + 2, B.PLANKS);
    for (let dz = -2; dz <= 2; dz++) for (let dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++)
      if (Math.abs(dx) === 2 || Math.abs(dz) === 2) this.put(x + dx, g + 9, z + dz, B.PLANKS);
    this.put(x, g + 9, z, B.CRATE);
    this.put(x + 1, g + 9, z, B.TNT);
    this.put(x - 2, g + 10, z - 2, B.LANTERN);
    // solid staircase up the east side, with a gap in the railing at the top
    for (let i = 0; i <= 8; i++) {
      const sz = z - 6 + i;
      this.put(x + 3, g + i, sz, B.PLANKS);
      const ground = Math.floor(this.heightAt(x + 3, sz));
      for (let y = g + i - 1; y > ground; y--) this.put(x + 3, y, sz, B.COBBLE);
    }
    this.put(x + 2, g + 9, z + 2, B.AIR);
  }

  // a pond dug into a hilltop and held in by a cobblestone dam; blow the dam to flood the slope
  private reservoir(bx: number, bz: number): void {
    const by = Math.floor(this.rawHeight(bx, bz)) + 1;
    for (let dz = -5; dz <= 5; dz++) for (let dx = -5; dx <= 5; dx++) {
      const r = Math.hypot(dx, dz), x = bx + dx, z = bz + dz;
      if (r <= 3.4) {
        const depth = r < 2 ? 3 : 2;
        for (let y = by + 3; y >= by - depth; y--) this.put(x, y, z, B.AIR);
        for (let k = 0; k < depth; k++) this.putWater(x, by - 1 - k, z);
      } else if (r <= 4.6) {
        const ground = Math.floor(this.rawHeight(x, z));
        for (let y = Math.min(ground, by - 3); y <= by; y++) this.put(x, y, z, B.COBBLE);
      }
    }
  }
}
```

### src/art/characters.ts
```
// Character sprite sheets as smooth inked vector drawings (Canvas2D paths, flat colours, cel-shade
// crescents, a heavy outer contour). Same layout the entities expect: 4 walk frames across x 3 views
// down (front, back, side facing right). Every cell is drawn in a 32 x 32 unit space (the old pixel
// grid, so the feet still stand on y = 30 and hitbox-to-sprite proportions are unchanged) and rendered
// at CELL px, then baked into a mipmapped texture.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Pen, INK, canvas, get2d, inkContour, bake, capsule, ellipse, rrect, blob, poly, curve, shade, mix } from './draw';

export interface Palette {
  skin: string; skinDark: string; eye: string; hair: string;
  shirt: string; shirtLight: string; shirtDark: string; belt: string;
  pants: string; pantsDark: string; boots: string;
  cape?: string; capeDark?: string; accent?: string;
  hat?: string; hatBand?: string;
}

/** pixels per sprite cell (32 drawing units) */
export const CELL = 256;
const U = CELL / 32;
const GROUND = 30;
/** extra outer contour, in drawing units */
const OUTER = 0.42;

type Kind = 'player' | 'zombie' | 'brute';
type V2 = [number, number];

/** two-bone joint (knee/elbow) between a and b for a limb of total length L, bending toward dir */
function joint(a: V2, b: V2, L: number, dir: V2): V2 {
  const dx = b[0] - a[0], dy = b[1] - a[1], d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1e-6;
  const mx = (a[0] + b[0]) / 2, my = (a[1] + b[1]) / 2;
  if (d >= L) return [mx, my];
  const h = Math.sqrt((L / 2) ** 2 - (d / 2) ** 2);
  let px = -dy / d, py = dx / d;
  if (px * dir[0] + py * dir[1] < 0) { px = -px; py = -py; }
  return [mx + px * h, my + py * h];
}

/** rotate drawing about a pivot for the rest of a save()/restore() block */
function pivot(c: CanvasRenderingContext2D, x: number, y: number, a: number): void { c.translate(x, y); c.rotate(a); c.translate(-x, -y); }

// ---------------- body proportions per kind ----------------
interface Body {
  hipY: number; shY: number; torsoW: number; waistW: number;
  headY: number; headRX: number; headRY: number;
  legW: number; armW: number; legL: number; armL: number; hipHalf: number; handR: number;
}
const BODY: Record<Kind, Body> = {
  player: { hipY: 23.2, shY: 15.4, torsoW: 8.6, waistW: 7.6, headY: 9.7, headRX: 5.7, headRY: 5.1, legW: 2.4, armW: 1.9, legL: 6.9, armL: 6.4, hipHalf: 2.0, handR: 1.05 },
  zombie: { hipY: 23.2, shY: 15.8, torsoW: 8.4, waistW: 7.2, headY: 10.2, headRX: 5.3, headRY: 4.9, legW: 2.3, armW: 1.85, legL: 6.9, armL: 6.8, hipHalf: 2.0, handR: 1.25 },
  brute: { hipY: 22.6, shY: 13.6, torsoW: 13.6, waistW: 10.4, headY: 10.0, headRX: 4.3, headRY: 4.0, legW: 3.5, armW: 3.4, legL: 7.6, armL: 10.2, hipHalf: 2.9, handR: 2.1 },
};

// ---------------- heads ----------------
function headPath(cx: number, cy: number, rx: number, ry: number, kind: Kind): Path2D {
  // a soft rounded "bean": wider jaw for the brute, a slightly squarer skull for zombies
  if (kind === 'brute') return blob([[cx - rx, cy - ry * 0.2], [cx - rx * 0.6, cy - ry], [cx + rx * 0.6, cy - ry], [cx + rx, cy - ry * 0.2], [cx + rx * 1.1, cy + ry * 0.7], [cx, cy + ry * 1.1], [cx - rx * 1.1, cy + ry * 0.7]]);
  if (kind === 'zombie') return blob([[cx - rx, cy - ry * 0.1], [cx - rx * 0.75, cy - ry * 0.95], [cx + rx * 0.2, cy - ry * 1.05], [cx + rx * 0.95, cy - ry * 0.6], [cx + rx, cy + ry * 0.4], [cx + rx * 0.45, cy + ry], [cx - rx * 0.5, cy + ry * 0.98], [cx - rx * 0.98, cy + ry * 0.5]]);
  return ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry);
}

function playerHat(pen: Pen, cx: number, top: number, view: number, pal: Palette): void {
  const hat = pal.hat!, band = pal.hatBand!;
  const fwd = view === 2 ? 0.9 : 0;
  // back half of the brim first (front/back views see the brim from slightly above)
  const brim = ellipse(cx + fwd, top + 4.6, view === 2 ? 7.4 : 8.1, view === 2 ? 1.5 : 2.0, view === 2 ? 0.05 : 0);
  pen.shape(brim, hat, { shade: shade(hat, 0.35), sd: 1.0 });
  // crown: a soft dome with a pinched top
  const crown = blob([[cx - 4.6 + fwd * 0.4, top + 4.4], [cx - 4.2, top + 1.6], [cx - 1.6, top + 0.2], [cx + 0.2, top + 0.9], [cx + 2.0, top + 0.1], [cx + 4.4, top + 1.6], [cx + 4.8 + fwd * 0.4, top + 4.4]], 0.9);
  pen.shape(crown, hat, { sd: 1.3 });
  // band
  const b = new Path2D();
  b.moveTo(cx - 4.7 + fwd * 0.4, top + 3.0); b.quadraticCurveTo(cx, top + 3.8, cx + 4.9 + fwd * 0.4, top + 3.0);
  b.lineTo(cx + 4.9 + fwd * 0.4, top + 4.5); b.quadraticCurveTo(cx, top + 5.3, cx - 4.7 + fwd * 0.4, top + 4.5); b.closePath();
  pen.shape(b, band, { sd: 0.6, ld: 0.3 });
  if (view === 2) { // a small feather tucked in the band, swept back
    const f = blob([[cx - 3.2, top + 3.6], [cx - 5.6, top + 1.4], [cx - 6.4, top + 0.4], [cx - 4.6, top + 1.2], [cx - 2.6, top + 2.8]], 0.9);
    pen.shape(f, '#f2ead6', { shade: '#c4b8a0', sd: 0.5, lw: 0.42 });
  }
}

function drawHead(pen: Pen, kind: Kind, view: number, frame: number, pal: Palette, B: Body, cx: number, cy: number): void {
  const c = pen.ctx, rx = B.headRX, ry = B.headRY;
  const hp = headPath(cx, cy, rx, ry, kind);
  pen.shape(hp, pal.skin, { shade: pal.skinDark, sd: 1.3 });
  if (view === 0) {
    if (kind === 'player') {
      // big friendly eyes with catchlights, brows, blush and a small smile
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
        const ex = cx + s * 2.15, ey = cy + 0.9;
        pen.fill(ellipse(ex, ey, 0.95, 1.3), pal.eye);
        pen.dot(ex - 0.32, ey - 0.45, 0.38, '#ffffff');
        pen.dot(ex + 0.3, ey + 0.5, 0.16, '#ffffff');
        pen.line(curve([[ex - 1.0 * s * -1, ey - 2.2], [ex, ey - 2.55], [ex + 1.0 * s, ey - 2.1]]), 0.42, mix(pal.hair, INK, 0.3));
        c.globalAlpha = 0.45; pen.fill(ellipse(cx + s * 3.6, cy + 2.5, 1.1, 0.6), '#ff7a7a'); c.globalAlpha = 1;
      }
      pen.line(curve([[cx - 0.9, cy + 3.1], [cx, cy + 3.7], [cx + 0.9, cy + 3.1]]), 0.42, INK);
    } else if (kind === 'zombie') {
      // mismatched eyes: one wide and staring, one droopy and half lidded, glowing pupils
      const L: V2 = [cx - 2.1, cy + 0.4], R: V2 = [cx + 2.2, cy + 0.9];
      c.globalAlpha = 0.35; pen.fill(ellipse(L[0], L[1] + 0.5, 1.9, 1.9), pal.skinDark); pen.fill(ellipse(R[0], R[1] + 0.4, 1.5, 1.5), pal.skinDark); c.globalAlpha = 1;
      pen.shape(ellipse(L[0], L[1], 1.45, 1.55), '#f2efc6', { shade: '#c9c49a', sd: 0.6, lw: 0.4 });
      pen.dot(L[0] + 0.25, L[1] + 0.2, 0.62, pal.eye); pen.dot(L[0] + 0.05, L[1], 0.22, '#fff8e0');
      pen.shape(ellipse(R[0], R[1], 1.1, 1.05), '#f2efc6', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 });
      pen.dot(R[0] - 0.15, R[1] + 0.3, 0.5, pal.eye);
      const lid = new Path2D(); lid.moveTo(R[0] - 1.35, R[1] + 0.05); lid.quadraticCurveTo(R[0], R[1] - 1.7, R[0] + 1.35, R[1] - 0.25); lid.closePath();
      pen.shape(lid, pal.skinDark, { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 });
      // gaping jagged mouth
      const m = poly([[cx - 2.0, cy + 2.9], [cx + 1.8, cy + 2.6], [cx + 1.3, cy + 4.1], [cx - 0.2, cy + 4.6], [cx - 1.6, cy + 4.1]]);
      pen.shape(m, '#4a1a22', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.45 });
      pen.fill(poly([[cx - 1.4, cy + 2.95], [cx - 0.9, cy + 3.75], [cx - 0.5, cy + 2.85]]), '#efe6c8');
      pen.fill(poly([[cx + 0.4, cy + 2.8], [cx + 0.85, cy + 3.6], [cx + 1.3, cy + 2.7]]), '#efe6c8');
      // stitched scar across the brow
      pen.line(curve([[cx + 0.6, cy - 2.8], [cx + 2.6, cy - 2.2], [cx + 4.2, cy - 2.9]]), 0.3, INK);
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) pen.line(curve([[cx + 1.1 + i * 1.1, cy - 3.3], [cx + 1.3 + i * 1.1, cy - 1.9]]), 0.25, INK);
    } else {
      // brute: beady glowing eyes under a heavy brow, underbite with tusks
      pen.shape(blob([[cx - 3.9, cy - 0.6], [cx, cy - 1.9], [cx + 3.9, cy - 0.6], [cx + 3.2, cy + 0.5], [cx - 3.2, cy + 0.5]]), pal.skinDark, { shade: null, sd: 0.4, lw: 0.4 });
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) { pen.dot(cx + s * 1.7, cy + 0.95, 0.7, '#2a1a12'); pen.dot(cx + s * 1.7, cy + 0.95, 0.42, pal.eye); }
      const jaw = blob([[cx - 3.0, cy + 2.4], [cx + 3.0, cy + 2.4], [cx + 2.4, cy + 3.9], [cx - 2.4, cy + 3.9]]);
      pen.shape(jaw, '#4a1a22', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 });
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) pen.shape(poly([[cx + s * 2.3, cy + 3.7], [cx + s * 2.0, cy + 1.5], [cx + s * 1.3, cy + 3.7]]), '#f0e6c4', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.3 });
    }
  } else if (view === 2) {
    if (kind === 'player') {
      const ex = cx + 2.9, ey = cy + 0.8;
      pen.fill(ellipse(ex, ey, 0.75, 1.25), pal.eye);
      pen.dot(ex - 0.2, ey - 0.45, 0.33, '#ffffff');
      pen.line(curve([[ex - 0.9, ey - 2.3], [ex + 0.3, ey - 2.6], [ex + 1.1, ey - 2.2]]), 0.42, mix(pal.hair, INK, 0.3));
      c.globalAlpha = 0.45; pen.fill(ellipse(cx + 2.4, cy + 2.6, 1.0, 0.55), '#ff7a7a'); c.globalAlpha = 1;
      pen.fill(ellipse(cx + rx - 0.25, cy + 1.75, 0.55, 0.5), pal.skin); // nose: a small bump on the profile
      pen.line(curve([[cx + rx - 0.05, cy + 1.2], [cx + rx + 0.45, cy + 1.8], [cx + rx - 0.1, cy + 2.25]]), 0.4, INK);
      pen.line(curve([[cx + 3.0, cy + 3.3], [cx + 3.8, cy + 3.5]]), 0.38, INK);
      // ear
      pen.shape(ellipse(cx - 1.0, cy + 1.2, 0.75, 1.0), pal.skin, { shade: pal.skinDark, sd: 0.4, lw: 0.38 });
    } else if (kind === 'zombie') {
      const ex = cx + 2.5, ey = cy + 0.4;
      pen.shape(ellipse(ex, ey, 1.25, 1.35), '#f2efc6', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 });
      pen.dot(ex + 0.45, ey + 0.2, 0.55, pal.eye);
      const m = poly([[cx + 2.0, cy + 2.9], [cx + rx + 0.2, cy + 2.4], [cx + rx - 0.1, cy + 4.0], [cx + 2.6, cy + 4.3]]);
      pen.shape(m, '#4a1a22', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 });
      pen.fill(poly([[cx + 3.4, cy + 2.7], [cx + 3.8, cy + 3.5], [cx + 4.2, cy + 2.6]]), '#efe6c8');
      pen.shape(poly([[cx - 1.6, cy + 0.2], [cx - 0.6, cy - 0.2], [cx - 0.5, cy + 1.0], [cx - 0.9, cy + 1.3], [cx - 0.6, cy + 1.9], [cx - 1.5, cy + 2.0]]), pal.skinDark, { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.36 });
      pen.line(curve([[cx - 3.0, cy - 2.6], [cx - 2.0, cy - 1.4], [cx - 2.8, cy - 0.2]]), 0.3, INK);
    } else {
      pen.shape(blob([[cx + 0.6, cy - 0.9], [cx + rx + 0.6, cy - 0.5], [cx + rx + 0.3, cy + 0.4], [cx + 0.8, cy + 0.2]]), pal.skinDark, { shade: null, lw: 0.4 });
      pen.dot(cx + 2.6, cy + 1.0, 0.65, '#2a1a12'); pen.dot(cx + 2.6, cy + 1.0, 0.4, pal.eye);
      pen.shape(blob([[cx + 1.4, cy + 2.4], [cx + rx + 0.9, cy + 2.2], [cx + rx + 0.6, cy + 3.9], [cx + 1.6, cy + 3.8]]), '#4a1a22', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 });
      pen.shape(poly([[cx + rx + 0.1, cy + 3.7], [cx + rx + 0.4, cy + 1.4], [cx + rx - 0.6, cy + 3.7]]), '#f0e6c4', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.3 });
    }
  }
  // hair / hat
  if (kind === 'player') {
    const hair = pal.hair;
    if (view === 0) {
      // fringe and side locks peeking from under the hat
      pen.shape(blob([[cx - rx - 0.2, cy - 0.8], [cx - rx + 0.3, cy - 3.6], [cx + rx - 0.3, cy - 3.6], [cx + rx + 0.2, cy - 0.8], [cx + 3.2, cy - 2.2], [cx + 1.0, cy - 1.4], [cx - 1.4, cy - 2.4], [cx - 3.4, cy - 1.6]]), hair, { sd: 0.7 });
      pen.shape(blob([[cx - rx - 0.4, cy - 2.0], [cx - rx + 1.3, cy - 1.6], [cx - rx + 0.8, cy + 2.0], [cx - rx - 0.3, cy + 1.2]]), hair, { sd: 0.5 });
      pen.shape(blob([[cx + rx + 0.4, cy - 2.0], [cx + rx - 1.3, cy - 1.6], [cx + rx - 0.8, cy + 2.0], [cx + rx + 0.3, cy + 1.2]]), hair, { sd: 0.5 });
    } else if (view === 1) {
      pen.shape(blob([[cx - rx - 0.4, cy - 3.0], [cx + rx + 0.4, cy - 3.0], [cx + rx + 0.3, cy + 1.8], [cx + 2.2, cy + 3.4], [cx + 0.4, cy + 2.6], [cx - 1.6, cy + 3.5], [cx - rx - 0.3, cy + 1.8]]), hair, { sd: 1.0 });
    } else {
      pen.shape(blob([[cx - rx - 0.3, cy - 3.0], [cx + 2.6, cy - 3.6], [cx + 3.2, cy - 2.0], [cx + 0.6, cy - 2.2], [cx - 0.6, cy + 0.4], [cx - 2.4, cy + 3.0], [cx - rx - 0.4, cy + 1.6]]), hair, { sd: 0.8 });
    }
    playerHat(pen, cx, cy - ry - 2.6, view, pal);
  } else if (kind === 'zombie') {
    // a few lank tufts on a balding scalp
    const hair = pal.hair, top = cy - ry;
    const tuft = (x: number, y: number, a: number, l: number): void => { // a lank strand flopping over
      const tip: V2 = [x + Math.sin(a) * l, y - Math.cos(a) * l * 0.55];
      const s = curve([[x, y + 0.5], [x + Math.sin(a) * l * 0.4, y - l * 0.55], tip]);
      pen.line(s, 1.05, INK); pen.line(s, 0.5, hair);
    };
    if (view === 1) {
      const cap = poly([[cx - rx + 0.1, cy + 0.6], [cx - rx + 0.2, cy - 2.4], [cx - 2.4, top + 0.3], [cx + 2.2, top + 0.2], [cx + rx - 0.1, cy - 2.2], [cx + rx, cy + 0.8], [cx + 3.4, cy - 0.2], [cx + 2.6, cy + 1.6], [cx + 1.2, cy + 0.2], [cx - 0.2, cy + 1.8], [cx - 1.4, cy + 0.0], [cx - 2.8, cy + 1.4], [cx - 3.6, cy - 0.2]]);
      pen.shape(cap, hair, { sd: 0.6, lw: 0.4 });
      tuft(cx - 1.6, top + 0.6, -1.0, 2.2);
      pen.line(curve([[cx - 1.5, cy + 2.6], [cx, cy + 3.0], [cx + 1.6, cy + 2.4]]), 0.3, INK);
    } else if (view === 2) {
      pen.shape(blob([[cx - rx + 0.1, cy - 0.4], [cx - 2.8, top + 0.4], [cx + 0.6, top + 0.1], [cx - 0.4, cy - 1.6]]), hair, { sd: 0.5 });
      tuft(cx + 1.4, top + 0.5, 0.5, 2.2);
    } else {
      pen.shape(blob([[cx - rx + 0.3, cy - 1.6], [cx - 3.0, top + 0.5], [cx - 0.6, top + 0.1], [cx - 1.4, top + 1.6], [cx - 3.4, cy - 1.2]]), hair, { sd: 0.5, lw: 0.4 });
      tuft(cx + 0.4, top + 0.4, 1.1 + (frame % 2) * 0.15, 2.6); tuft(cx + 2.2, top + 0.9, 1.4, 2.0);
    }
  } else if (view !== 0) {
    pen.line(curve([[cx - 2.0, cy - ry + 1.2], [cx - 0.6, cy - ry + 2.4], [cx + 0.6, cy - ry + 1.5]]), 0.3, INK); // scar
  }
}

// ---------------- the humanoid drawing ----------------
function drawHumanoid(pen: Pen, view: number, frame: number, pal: Palette, kind: Kind): void {
  const c = pen.ctx, B = BODY[kind];
  const zom = kind !== 'player';
  const bob = (frame === 1 || frame === 3) ? (kind === 'brute' ? 0.9 : 0.7) : 0;
  const hipY = B.hipY + bob, shY = B.shY + bob, cx = 16;
  const ph = [0, 1, 0, -1][frame];
  const shirt = pal.shirt, pants = pal.pants;

  // a frayed tear in the cloth showing skin underneath (irregular, so it reads as torn, not as a patch)
  const rip = (x: number, y: number, r: number, k: number): void => {
    const pts: number[][] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      const a = (i / 7) * Math.PI * 2 + k, rr = r * (i % 2 ? 0.55 : 1) * (0.8 + 0.4 * Math.abs(Math.sin(k * 3.1 + i * 1.7)));
      pts.push([x + Math.cos(a) * rr * 1.25, y + Math.sin(a) * rr]);
    }
    pen.shape(poly(pts), pal.skin, { shade: pal.skinDark, sd: r * 0.5, light: null, lw: 0.3 });
  };
  // feet / boots
  const boot = (ax: number, ay: number, side: boolean, lift: number): void => {
    const s = kind === 'brute' ? 1.35 : 1;
    if (side) {
      const p = blob([[ax - 1.3 * s, ay - 0.9 * s], [ax + 0.6 * s, ay - 1.0 * s], [ax + 2.3 * s, ay + 0.2 * s], [ax + 2.2 * s, GROUND - lift], [ax - 1.4 * s, GROUND - lift]], 0.7);
      pen.shape(p, pal.boots, { sd: 0.6 });
    } else {
      pen.shape(blob([[ax - 1.45 * s, ay - 0.8 * s], [ax + 1.45 * s, ay - 0.8 * s], [ax + 1.7 * s, GROUND - lift - 0.2], [ax, GROUND - lift + 0.1], [ax - 1.7 * s, GROUND - lift - 0.2]], 0.8), pal.boots, { sd: 0.6 });
    }
  };
  const legFront = (sx: number, lift: number, kneeOut: number): void => {
    const hip: V2 = [cx + sx * B.hipHalf, hipY - 0.6], ank: V2 = [cx + sx * (B.hipHalf + 0.25), GROUND - 1.0 - lift];
    const knee = joint(hip, ank, B.legL - 0.4, [sx * kneeOut, -0.2]);
    pen.limb([hip, knee, ank], B.legW, pants, { shade: pal.pantsDark });
    if (zom && sx < 0) rip(knee[0] + (ank[0] - knee[0]) * 0.3, knee[1] + (ank[1] - knee[1]) * 0.3, kind === 'brute' ? 0.9 : 0.62, 1.3); // torn trouser leg
    boot(ank[0], ank[1], false, lift);
  };
  const legSide = (fx: number, lift: number, far: boolean): void => {
    const hip: V2 = [cx + (kind === 'brute' ? 0.4 : 0.2), hipY - 0.6], ank: V2 = [fx, GROUND - 1.0 - lift];
    const knee = joint(hip, ank, B.legL, [1, 0]);
    const col = far ? pal.pantsDark : pants;
    pen.limb([hip, knee, ank], B.legW, col, { shade: far ? shade(pal.pantsDark, 0.2) : pal.pantsDark });
    if (zom && !far) rip(knee[0] + (ank[0] - knee[0]) * 0.3, knee[1] + (ank[1] - knee[1]) * 0.3, kind === 'brute' ? 0.9 : 0.62, 2.1);
    boot(ank[0], ank[1], true, lift);
  };

  // torso: rounded trapezoid, shirt with a light yoke; zombies get a ragged hem and holes
  const torso = (): void => {
    const tw = B.torsoW / 2, ww = B.waistW / 2;
    const side = view === 2;
    const tl = side ? -tw * 0.62 : -tw, tr = side ? tw * 0.62 : tw;
    const bl = side ? -ww * 0.66 : -ww, br = side ? ww * 0.66 : ww;
    const hemY = hipY + (zom ? 0.9 : 0.4);
    // pants seat / hips
    pen.shape(rrect(cx + bl - 0.1, hipY - 1.9, br - bl + 0.2, 2.8, 1.1), pants, { shade: pal.pantsDark, sd: 0.8 });
    let p: Path2D;
    if (kind === 'brute' && side) {
      // side-on the brute is a hulking hump: a rounded back rising over the shoulders, a deep chest
      p = blob([[cx - 3.4, hipY - 0.2], [cx - 4.8, shY + 4.0], [cx - 3.6, shY - 0.2], [cx - 0.4, shY - 1.6], [cx + 3.4, shY + 0.2], [cx + 4.8, shY + 4.2], [cx + 4.0, hipY - 0.8], [cx + 1.2, hipY + 1.0], [cx - 1.6, hipY + 0.6]], 0.9);
    } else if (zom) {
      const pts: number[][] = [[cx + tl, shY + 0.9], [cx + tl * 0.55, shY - 0.2], [cx + tr * 0.55, shY - 0.2], [cx + tr, shY + 0.9], [cx + br + 0.3, hemY - 1.0]];
      // ragged hem, zig-zagging back across
      const n = 6;
      for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
        const t = i / n, x = cx + br + 0.3 + (bl - br - 0.6) * t;
        pts.push([x, hemY + (i % 2 ? 0.9 : -0.2) + Math.sin(i * 2.7 + frame * 0) * 0.3]);
      }
      pts.push([cx + bl - 0.3, hemY - 1.0]);
      p = poly(pts);
      // soften the shoulders by drawing a blob underneath
      pen.shape(blob([[cx + tl, shY + 1.0], [cx + tl * 0.6, shY - 0.2], [cx + tr * 0.6, shY - 0.2], [cx + tr, shY + 1.0], [cx + br, hipY - 1.0], [cx + bl, hipY - 1.0]]), shirt, { shade: pal.shirtDark, lw: 0 });
    } else {
      p = blob([[cx + tl, shY + 1.0], [cx + tl * 0.6, shY - 0.15], [cx + tr * 0.6, shY - 0.15], [cx + tr, shY + 1.0], [cx + br + 0.2, hemY - 0.6], [cx + br - 0.4, hemY], [cx + bl + 0.4, hemY], [cx + bl - 0.2, hemY - 0.6]], 0.85);
    }
    pen.shape(p, shirt, { shade: pal.shirtDark, light: pal.shirtLight, sd: 1.6, ld: 0.7 });
    c.save(); c.clip(p);
    if (!side && kind === 'player' && view === 0) {
      // satchel strap across the chest and a collar notch
      pen.limb([[cx + tl + 0.6, shY + 0.2], [cx + br - 0.4, hipY - 2.0]], 1.0, pal.belt, { lw: 0.35 });
      pen.line(curve([[cx - 1.4, shY + 0.1], [cx, shY + 1.8], [cx + 1.4, shY + 0.1]]), 0.4, pal.shirtDark);
    }
    if (zom) {
      // holes in the shirt showing skin, and tear lines
      const holes: number[][] = view === 1 ? [[cx - 1.8, shY + 3.6, 0.8], [cx + 2.2, shY + 5.6, 0.55]] : view === 2 ? [[cx + 0.4, shY + 4.4, 0.7]] : [[cx - 2.0, shY + 4.6, 0.75], [cx + 2.4, shY + 2.4, 0.5]];
      holes.forEach(([hx, hy, r], i) => rip(hx, hy, r * (kind === 'brute' ? 1.3 : 1), 0.7 + i));
      if (kind === 'brute' && view !== 1) {
        // open vest: a strip of chest down the middle, stitches across it
        const chest = blob([[cx - 1.8 + (side ? 2.4 : 0), shY + 0.4], [cx + 1.8 + (side ? 2.4 : 0), shY + 0.4], [cx + 1.2 + (side ? 2.4 : 0), hipY - 1.6], [cx - 1.2 + (side ? 2.4 : 0), hipY - 1.6]]);
        if (!side) { pen.shape(chest, pal.skin, { shade: pal.skinDark, sd: 0.7 }); for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) pen.line(curve([[cx - 1.0, shY + 2.2 + i * 1.6], [cx + 1.0, shY + 2.6 + i * 1.6]]), 0.3); }
      }
    }
    c.restore();
    // belt + buckle
    const belt = rrect(cx + bl - 0.3, hipY - 2.2, br - bl + 0.6, 1.25, 0.5);
    pen.shape(belt, pal.belt, { sd: 0.5, ld: 0.3, lw: 0.4 });
    if (view !== 1) {
      const bx = side ? cx + br - 0.9 : cx;
      pen.shape(rrect(bx - 0.75, hipY - 2.35, 1.5, 1.55, 0.35), kind === 'player' ? (pal.accent ?? '#e0b040') : '#9a9a8a', { sd: 0.35, ld: 0.25, lw: 0.35 });
    }
  };

  // ---------- capes (player) ----------
  const capeCol = pal.cape, capeDk = pal.capeDark ?? (capeCol ? shade(capeCol) : '');
  const flutter = [0, 1.0, 0.4, 1.2][frame];
  const capeBack = (): void => { // front view: edges of the cape behind the shoulders
    if (!capeCol) return;
    const p = blob([[cx - 4.6, shY], [cx + 4.6, shY], [cx + 6.1, hipY + 2.0], [cx + 3.0, hipY + 2.8], [cx - 3.0, hipY + 2.8], [cx - 6.1, hipY + 2.0]], 0.9);
    pen.shape(p, capeDk, { shade: shade(capeDk, 0.25), light: null });
  };
  const capeFull = (): void => { // back view: covers the back
    if (!capeCol) return;
    const hem = hipY + 2.6, w = flutter * 0.4;
    const p = new Path2D();
    p.moveTo(cx - 4.4, shY - 0.2);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(cx, shY - 1.0, cx + 4.4, shY - 0.2);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(cx + 6.4 + w, hipY - 2.0, cx + 6.6 + w, hem);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(cx + 4.8, hem + 1.0, cx + 3.3, hem - 0.1);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(cx + 1.6, hem + 1.0, cx, hem + 0.1);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(cx - 1.6, hem + 1.0, cx - 3.3, hem - 0.1);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(cx - 4.8, hem + 1.0, cx - 6.6 - w, hem);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(cx - 6.4 - w, hipY - 2.0, cx - 4.4, shY - 0.2);
    pen.shape(p, capeCol, { shade: capeDk, sd: 1.8, ld: 0.6 });
    // soft folds
    for (const fx of [-3.3, 0, 3.3]) pen.line(curve([[cx + fx * 0.5, shY + 2.4], [cx + fx * 0.8, hipY - 1.0], [cx + fx, hem - 0.4]]), 0.38, mix(capeDk, INK, 0.35));
    // collar
    pen.shape(blob([[cx - 4.0, shY - 0.4], [cx, shY - 1.2], [cx + 4.0, shY - 0.4], [cx + 2.8, shY + 1.4], [cx - 2.8, shY + 1.4]]), capeDk, { sd: 0.6, light: capeCol });
  };
  const capeSide = (): void => { // side view: billows out behind
    if (!capeCol) return;
    const hem = hipY + 2.3;
    const p = blob([[cx - 0.6, shY - 0.4], [cx + 1.2, shY + 0.2], [cx - 0.6, hipY - 1.0], [cx - 1.6, hem], [cx - 4.2 - flutter, hem + 0.4], [cx - 5.2 - flutter * 1.3, hipY - 1.2], [cx - 3.6 - flutter * 0.5, shY + 2.2]], 0.9);
    pen.shape(p, capeCol, { shade: capeDk, sd: 1.5 });
    pen.line(curve([[cx - 1.6, shY + 2.0], [cx - 3.0 - flutter * 0.4, hipY - 1.0], [cx - 3.2 - flutter * 0.8, hem]]), 0.36, mix(capeDk, INK, 0.35));
  };
  const capeFrontDrape = (): void => { // front view: cape over the shoulders + clasp
    if (!capeCol) return;
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
      const p = blob([[cx + s * 2.2, shY - 0.4], [cx + s * 4.8, shY - 0.1], [cx + s * 5.4, shY + 1.4], [cx + s * 4.6, shY + 3.6], [cx + s * 3.6, shY + 1.6], [cx + s * 2.0, shY + 0.8]], 0.8);
      pen.shape(p, capeCol, { shade: capeDk, sd: 0.8 });
    }
    pen.shape(ellipse(cx, shY + 0.6, 1.05, 0.95), pal.accent!, { sd: 0.5, ld: 0.3, lw: 0.4 });
  };

  // ---------- arms ----------
  const handCol = pal.skin;
  const hand = (x: number, y: number, r: number, fingers: number, dirx = 0, diry = 1): void => {
    if (fingers) { // zombie/brute: drooping fingers
      for (let i = 0; i < fingers; i++) {
        const a = (i - (fingers - 1) / 2) * 0.55, fx = x + (dirx * Math.cos(a) - diry * Math.sin(a)) * r * 1.5, fy = y + (diry * Math.cos(a) + dirx * Math.sin(a)) * r * 1.5;
        pen.shape(capsule(x, y, fx, fy, r * 0.42, r * 0.3), handCol, { shade: pal.skinDark, sd: 0.3, light: null, lw: 0.34 });
      }
    }
    pen.shape(ellipse(x, y, r, r * 0.95), handCol, { shade: pal.skinDark, sd: r * 0.5, ld: 0.3, lw: 0.42 });
  };
  const sleeveCol = kind === 'player' ? pal.shirtDark : shirt;
  const arm = (pts: V2[], far: boolean, sleeveFrac: number, fingers: number, hd: V2 = [0, 1]): void => {
    const sk = far ? pal.skinDark : pal.skin, sl = far ? shade(sleeveCol, 0.18) : sleeveCol;
    // forearm skin, then the sleeve over the upper arm (ragged cuff for the undead)
    pen.limb(pts, B.armW * (kind === 'brute' ? 0.95 : 0.9), sk, { shade: far ? shade(sk, 0.2) : pal.skinDark });
    const [a, b, e] = pts;
    const cut: V2 = sleeveFrac <= 1 ? [a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * sleeveFrac, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * sleeveFrac] : [b[0] + (e[0] - b[0]) * (sleeveFrac - 1), b[1] + (e[1] - b[1]) * (sleeveFrac - 1)];
    pen.limb(sleeveFrac <= 1 ? [a, cut] : [a, b, cut], B.armW * 1.12, sl, { shade: far ? shade(sl, 0.2) : shade(sl) });
    if (zom && sleeveFrac > 0.4) {
      const r = B.armW * 0.62;
      pen.shape(poly([[cut[0] - r, cut[1] - r * 0.5], [cut[0] + r, cut[1] - r * 0.5], [cut[0] + r * 0.6, cut[1] + r * 0.9], [cut[0] + r * 0.1, cut[1] + r * 0.3], [cut[0] - r * 0.4, cut[1] + r * 1.0]]), sl, { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.3 });
    }
    hand(e[0], e[1], B.handR * (far ? 0.95 : 1), fingers, hd[0], hd[1]);
  };
  const fingers = kind === 'player' ? 0 : 3;
  const sleeve = kind === 'player' ? 1.6 : kind === 'brute' ? 0.45 : 0.9;

  // ---------- assemble per view ----------
  if (view === 2) {
    const lean = kind === 'player' ? (bob ? 0.06 : 0.02) : kind === 'zombie' ? 0.22 + (frame % 2 ? 0.04 : 0) : 0.2 + (frame % 2 ? 0.03 : 0);
    const stride = kind === 'zombie' ? 2.4 : kind === 'brute' ? 2.6 : 3.1;
    const shoulder: V2 = [cx + 0.3, shY + 1.1];
    const armSwing = (s: number): V2[] => {
      const L = B.armL, a = s;
      const el: V2 = [shoulder[0] + Math.sin(a) * L * 0.5, shoulder[1] + Math.cos(a) * L * 0.5];
      const a2 = a + (kind === 'brute' ? 0.2 : 0.4);
      return [shoulder, el, [el[0] + Math.sin(a2) * L * 0.5, el[1] + Math.cos(a2) * L * 0.5]];
    };
    const reach = (dy: number, dx: number): V2[] => { // zombie arms held out in front
      const L = B.armL;
      // (raised against the body's forward lean so they end up roughly level, hands drooping)
      return [shoulder, [shoulder[0] + L * 0.5 + dx * 0.5, shoulder[1] - 0.5 + dy * 0.5], [shoulder[0] + L + dx, shoulder[1] - 0.8 + dy]];
    };
    const armBob = [0, 0.5, 0, -0.5][frame];
    // far arm (behind everything)
    c.save(); pivot(c, cx, hipY, lean);
    if (kind === 'zombie') arm(reach(-0.9 - armBob, -0.6), true, sleeve, fingers, [0.7, 0.7]);
    else if (kind === 'brute') arm(armSwing(0.35 + ph * 0.35 - lean), true, sleeve, fingers, [0.3, 1]);
    else arm(armSwing(ph * 0.55), true, sleeve, fingers);
    c.restore();
    // legs: far, then cape, then near
    legSide(cx - 0.7 - ph * stride, frame === 2 ? (kind === 'player' ? 1.6 : 0.9) : 0, true);
    c.save(); pivot(c, cx, hipY, lean); capeSide(); c.restore();
    legSide(cx + 0.7 + ph * stride, 0, false);
    // torso, head, near arm
    c.save(); pivot(c, cx, hipY, lean);
    torso();
    // (the brute's head juts forward and low from between its shoulders, under the hump of its back)
    const headX = cx + (kind === 'player' ? 0.6 : kind === 'zombie' ? 1.4 : 3.6), headY = B.headY + bob + (kind === 'brute' ? 3.4 : kind === 'zombie' ? 0.6 : 0);
    if (kind === 'player') {
      pen.shape(rrect(headX - 1.6, headY + B.headRY - 1.6, 2.6, 2.4, 0.8), pal.skinDark, { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 }); // neck
    }
    c.save(); pivot(c, headX, headY + B.headRY, kind === 'zombie' ? 0.18 - lean : kind === 'brute' ? -lean * 0.6 : 0);
    drawHead(pen, kind, 2, frame, pal, B, headX, headY);
    c.restore();
    if (kind === 'zombie') arm(reach(-armBob, 0.4), false, sleeve, fingers, [0.7, 0.7]);
    else if (kind === 'brute') arm(armSwing(0.35 - ph * 0.35 - lean), false, sleeve, fingers, [0.3, 1]);
    else arm(armSwing(-ph * 0.55), false, sleeve, fingers);
    c.restore();
    return;
  }

  // front / back
  const sway = kind === 'player' ? 0 : [0, 0.05, 0, -0.05][frame] * (kind === 'brute' ? 0.6 : 1);
  const liftL = frame === 1 ? 1.1 : 0, liftR = frame === 3 ? 1.1 : 0;
  const tw = B.torsoW / 2;
  const sx = tw + (kind === 'brute' ? -0.2 : 0.1);
  const hangArm = (s: number, swing: number, far: boolean): void => {
    const sh: V2 = [cx + s * sx, shY + (kind === 'brute' ? 1.6 : 1.0)];
    const hd: V2 = [cx + s * (sx + (kind === 'brute' ? 1.3 : 0.75)), sh[1] + B.armL - 0.6 + swing];
    const el = joint(sh, hd, B.armL, [s, 0]);
    arm([sh, el, hd], far, sleeve, kind === 'brute' ? 3 : 0, [s * 0.2, 1]);
  };
  if (view === 0) {
    c.save(); pivot(c, cx, GROUND, sway);
    capeBack();
    legFront(-1, liftL, 0.3); legFront(1, liftR, 0.3);
    torso();
    capeFrontDrape();
    const headY = B.headY + bob + (kind === 'brute' ? 1.2 : 0);
    if (kind === 'brute') {
      hangArm(-1, -ph * 0.6, false); hangArm(1, ph * 0.6, false);
      drawHead(pen, kind, 0, frame, pal, B, cx, headY);
    } else {
      pen.shape(rrect(cx - 1.3, headY + B.headRY - 1.4, 2.6, 2.4, 0.8), pal.skinDark, { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 }); // neck
      c.save(); pivot(c, cx, headY + B.headRY, kind === 'zombie' ? [-0.12, -0.06, 0.1, 0.04][frame] : 0);
      drawHead(pen, kind, 0, frame, pal, B, cx, headY);
      c.restore();
      if (kind === 'zombie') {
        // arms held out toward the viewer: short foreshortened upper arms, big hands in front of the belly
        const ab = [0, 0.4, 0, -0.4][frame];
        for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
          const sh: V2 = [cx + s * sx, shY + 1.0], hd: V2 = [cx + s * (sx - 0.9), shY + 5.2 + ab * s];
          arm([sh, [cx + s * (sx + 0.4), shY + 3.0 + ab * s * 0.5], hd], false, sleeve, 3, [s * 0.1, 1]);
        }
      } else { hangArm(-1, -ph * 0.7, false); hangArm(1, ph * 0.7, false); }
    }
    c.restore();
  } else {
    c.save(); pivot(c, cx, GROUND, sway);
    if (kind === 'zombie') { // arms reach away from us: stubs tucked behind the shoulders
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) arm([[cx + s * sx, shY + 1.0], [cx + s * (sx + 0.3), shY + 2.6], [cx + s * (sx - 0.4), shY + 3.6]], true, 1.6, 0);
    }
    legFront(-1, liftL, 0.3); legFront(1, liftR, 0.3);
    torso();
    if (kind === 'player' || kind === 'brute') { hangArm(-1, ph * 0.7, false); hangArm(1, -ph * 0.7, false); }
    const headY = B.headY + bob + (kind === 'brute' ? 1.2 : 0);
    if (kind !== 'brute') pen.shape(rrect(cx - 1.3, headY + B.headRY - 1.4, 2.6, 2.4, 0.8), pal.skinDark, { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 });
    drawHead(pen, kind, 1, frame, pal, B, cx, headY);
    capeFull();
    c.restore();
  }
}

// ---------------- bugs (side views; the entities only use row 2 for them) ----------------
/** a jointed insect leg: the femur rises from the body to a high knee, the shin splays down to a
