```
 *  pointed foot (low, wide stance like a beetle or spider rather than stilts) */
function bugLeg(pen: Pen, x: number, y: number, fx: number, kneeUp: number, lift: number, col: string, w: number): void {
  const foot: V2 = [fx, GROUND - 0.25 - lift];
  const knee: V2 = [x + (fx - x) * 0.55, y - kneeUp - lift * 0.4];
  pen.limb([[x, y], knee], w, col, { lw: 0.42 });
  pen.limb([knee, [fx - (fx - knee[0]) * 0.08, foot[1] - 0.5], foot], w * 0.78, col, { lw: 0.42 });
  pen.dot(knee[0], knee[1], w * 0.55, mix(col, '#ffffff', 0.18)); // glossy knee joint
}

function drawCrawler(pen: Pen, frame: number): void {
  const shell = '#5e3460', shellLt = '#a8649a', belly = '#3a2742', leg = '#4a3456';
  const bob = frame % 2 ? -0.3 : 0;
  // tripod gait: legs 0 and 2 of one side step together with leg 1 of the other
  const st = [-1, 0, 1, 0][frame];
  const attach = [13.6, 16.2, 18.8], reach = [-7.6, -2.4, 6.8];
  const by = 22.6 + bob;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { // far legs: darker, shorter reach
    const s = i % 2 ? -st : st;
    bugLeg(pen, attach[i] - 0.6, by + 2.0, attach[i] + reach[i] * 0.8 + 1.6 + s * 1.2, 1.5, frame === 1 && i === 1 ? 0.7 : 0, mix(leg, '#000000', 0.3), 0.95);
  }
  // abdomen: three overlapping shell plates over a soft belly
  pen.shape(ellipse(11.4, by + 0.3, 7.0, 3.3, -0.06), belly, { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.5 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const px = 7.8 + i * 3.1, pr = 4.4 - i * 0.6, top = by - 4.0 + i * 0.5;
    const plate = blob([[px - pr, by + 1.6], [px - pr * 0.8, top + 1.2], [px + pr * 0.1, top], [px + pr * 0.95, top + 1.4], [px + pr * 1.05, by + 1.2]], 0.9);
    pen.shape(plate, shell, { shade: shade(shell, 0.32), light: shellLt, sd: 1.2, ld: 0.6, lw: 0.45 });
  }
  pen.line(curve([[5.6, by - 1.8], [8.2, by - 3.9], [11.6, by - 4.0]]), 0.6, mix(shellLt, '#ffffff', 0.45)); // gloss
  // thorax, mandibles, head
  pen.shape(ellipse(18.2, by + 0.2, 3.1, 2.9), shell, { shade: shade(shell, 0.3), light: shellLt, sd: 1.0 });
  const hx = 22.4, hy = by + 0.4, pinch = frame % 2 ? 0.5 : 0;
  pen.shape(blob([[hx + 1.4, hy + 0.6], [hx + 4.8, hy + 1.2 - pinch], [hx + 5.0, hy + 2.4 - pinch * 0.6], [hx + 3.6, hy + 3.4], [hx + 1.2, hy + 2.6]], 0.7), '#e2c8a0', { shade: '#a8885e', sd: 0.5, lw: 0.42 });
  pen.line(curve([[hx + 3.4, hy + 1.7], [hx + 4.4, hy + 2.6]]), 0.3, INK);
  pen.shape(ellipse(hx, hy, 3.3, 3.0), mix(shell, '#000000', 0.1), { shade: shade(shell, 0.42), light: shellLt, sd: 1.0 });
  // eye cluster: big glowing reds with catchlights (the read-at-a-glance feature)
  pen.shape(ellipse(hx + 1.3, hy - 0.8, 1.15, 1.05), '#ff3a30', { shade: '#b01818', light: '#ff9a80', sd: 0.5, lw: 0.35 });
  pen.shape(ellipse(hx + 2.6, hy + 0.3, 0.7, 0.65), '#ff3a30', { shade: '#b01818', light: null, sd: 0.3, lw: 0.3 });
  pen.dot(hx + 1.0, hy - 1.1, 0.32, '#ffe8e0');
  // antennae
  pen.line(curve([[hx + 0.2, hy - 2.6], [hx + 1.6, hy - 5.8], [hx + 4.8, hy - 6.6 - pinch]]), 0.5, INK);
  pen.line(curve([[hx - 0.8, hy - 2.7], [hx - 0.4, hy - 6.2], [hx + 1.8, hy - 7.6 + pinch]]), 0.5, INK);
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { // near legs
    const s = i % 2 ? st : -st;
    bugLeg(pen, attach[i], by + 2.6, attach[i] + reach[i] + s * 1.4, 1.5, frame === 3 && i === 1 ? 0.8 : 0, leg, 1.15);
  }
}

function drawSpitter(pen: Pen, frame: number): void {
  const c = pen.ctx;
  const body = '#5a3a6c', bodyLt = '#9670ae', leg = '#463054';
  const pulse = [0, 0.05, 0.08, 0.03][frame], bob = frame % 2 ? -0.3 : 0;
  const st = [-1, 0, 1, 0][frame];
  const attach = [17.0, 19.8, 22.6], reach = [-6.6, -1.8, 6.0];
  const by = 22.4 + bob;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) bugLeg(pen, attach[i] - 0.6, by + 1.6, attach[i] + reach[i] * 0.75 - 0.6 + 1.4 + (i % 2 ? st : -st) * 1.2, 1.6, 0, mix(leg, '#000000', 0.3), 1.05);
  // the acid sac: a big translucent glowing bulb (radial gradient, bubbles, veins)
  const sx = 11.4, sy = 16.4 + bob, rx = 7.8 * (1 + pulse), ry = 7.0 * (1 + pulse);
  const sac = blob([[sx - rx, sy + 0.4], [sx - rx * 0.7, sy - ry * 0.75], [sx, sy - ry], [sx + rx * 0.75, sy - ry * 0.65], [sx + rx, sy + 0.6], [sx + rx * 0.6, sy + ry * 0.8], [sx - rx * 0.5, sy + ry * 0.85]], 1);
  const g = c.createRadialGradient(sx - 2.6, sy - 3.0, 0.5, sx, sy, rx * 1.05);
  g.addColorStop(0, '#f2ffd0'); g.addColorStop(0.3, '#a8f06a'); g.addColorStop(0.75, '#4cbf38'); g.addColorStop(1, '#2a7a2c');
  pen.fill(sac, g);
  c.save(); c.clip(sac);
  pen.line(curve([[sx - 4.6, sy + 4.6], [sx - 1.6, sy + 1.0], [sx + 1.0, sy - 3.4]]), 0.4, '#2f8a2e');
  pen.line(curve([[sx + 2.6, sy + 5.4], [sx + 2.0, sy + 1.6], [sx + 4.6, sy - 1.6]]), 0.35, '#2f8a2e');
  for (const [bx, bby, r] of [[sx - 3.2, sy + 2.6, 1.0], [sx + 2.6, sy - 1.0, 0.7], [sx + 0.8, sy + 3.8, 0.55], [sx - 0.6, sy - 0.4, 0.4]]) {
    pen.fill(ellipse(bx, bby - pulse * 6, r, r), 'rgba(230,255,190,0.55)');
    pen.line(ellipse(bx, bby - pulse * 6, r, r), 0.18, 'rgba(40,120,40,0.6)');
  }
  c.restore();
  pen.line(sac, 0.55, INK);
  pen.fill(ellipse(sx - 3.4, sy - 3.6, 2.0, 1.2, -0.5), 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)');
  pen.dot(sx - 0.8, sy - 5.0, 0.5, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)');
  // thorax: armoured purple segments with spikes
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const px = 17.4 + i * 2.8, py = by - 0.6 - i * 0.5;
    pen.shape(poly([[px - 0.8, py - 2.8], [px + 0.3, py - 5.4 - (i === 1 ? 0.6 : 0)], [px + 1.3, py - 2.7]]), shade(body, 0.2), { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.4 });
    pen.shape(ellipse(px, py, 3.0, 3.3), body, { shade: shade(body, 0.3), light: bodyLt, sd: 1.1, lw: 0.48 });
  }
  // head with a spitting nozzle and yellow eyes
  const hx = 26.0, hy = by - 2.6;
  pen.shape(rrect(hx + 1.6, hy + 0.3, 3.4, 2.4, 1.0), '#e8a03a', { sd: 0.5, lw: 0.42 });
  pen.fill(ellipse(hx + 5.0, hy + 1.5, 0.6, 0.85), '#7aff4a');
  pen.shape(ellipse(hx, hy, 3.4, 3.1), body, { shade: shade(body, 0.35), light: bodyLt, sd: 1.0 });
  pen.shape(ellipse(hx + 1.2, hy - 0.9, 1.1, 1.0), '#ffe040', { shade: '#c8a020', light: null, sd: 0.4, lw: 0.35 });
  pen.dot(hx + 1.5, hy - 0.8, 0.45, '#3a2010'); pen.dot(hx + 0.95, hy - 1.2, 0.22, '#fffbe0');
  pen.shape(ellipse(hx - 0.9, hy - 1.7, 0.6, 0.55), '#ffe040', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.3 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) bugLeg(pen, attach[i], by + 2.2, attach[i] + reach[i] + (i % 2 ? -st : st) * 1.4, 1.6, 0, leg, 1.2);
}

function drawBat(pen: Pen, frame: number): void {
  const body = '#3c2646', belly = '#6c4a72', wing = '#5a3a6e', wingDk = '#3a2448';
  const up = [-0.5, -5.2, -2.0, 3.2][frame];
  const cy = 17 + [0, 0.6, 0.2, -0.6][frame];
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
    // wing membrane: root at the shoulder, three finger bones, scalloped trailing edge
    const rx = 16 + s * 2.2, ry = cy - 1.0;
    const tipX = 16 + s * (13.4 - Math.abs(up) * 0.25), tipY = cy - 3.2 + up;
    const f1: V2 = [16 + s * 8.6, cy - 3.6 + up * 0.75], f2: V2 = [16 + s * 10.4, cy + 1.6 + up * 0.55], f3: V2 = [16 + s * 6.4, cy + 3.4 + up * 0.35];
    const p = new Path2D();
    p.moveTo(rx, ry - 0.8);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(f1[0], f1[1] - 1.4, tipX, tipY);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(16 + s * 12.0, cy + 0.4 + up * 0.6, f2[0], f2[1]);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(16 + s * 8.6, cy + 1.6 + up * 0.4, f3[0], f3[1]);
    p.quadraticCurveTo(16 + s * 4.2, cy + 2.2, rx, cy + 2.6);
    p.closePath();
    pen.shape(p, wing, { shade: wingDk, sd: 1.2, light: mix(wing, '#c090d0', 0.25), ld: 0.5 });
    for (const f of [[tipX, tipY], f2, f3]) pen.line(curve([[rx, ry], [(rx + f[0]) / 2, (ry + f[1]) / 2 - 0.6], [f[0], f[1]]]), 0.42, mix(wingDk, INK, 0.5));
  }
  // ears
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) pen.shape(poly([[16 + s * 0.8, cy - 3.0], [16 + s * 2.6, cy - 7.0], [16 + s * 3.2, cy - 2.4]]), body, { shade: shade(body, 0.3), light: '#8a5a8a', sd: 0.6 });
  // furry body + lighter belly
  pen.shape(blob([[16, cy - 4.0], [19.4, cy - 1.6], [19.2, cy + 2.6], [16, cy + 4.6], [12.8, cy + 2.6], [12.6, cy - 1.6]], 1), body, { sd: 1.2, light: '#7a5080' });
  pen.fill(ellipse(16, cy + 1.6, 1.9, 2.2), belly);
  // face: glowing eyes, tiny fangs
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) { pen.dot(16 + s * 1.3, cy - 1.4, 0.75, '#ff4a4a'); pen.dot(16 + s * 1.15, cy - 1.6, 0.25, '#ffe0d0'); }
  pen.shape(poly([[15.2, cy + 0.2], [15.6, cy + 1.3], [16.0, cy + 0.2], [16.4, cy + 1.3], [16.8, cy + 0.2]]), '#f0e8d8', { shade: null, light: null, lw: 0.3 });
  // feet
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) pen.line(curve([[16 + s * 0.9, cy + 4.2], [16 + s * 1.2, cy + 5.3]]), 0.5, INK);
}

// ---------------- sheet assembly ----------------
type CellFn = (pen: Pen, view: number, frame: number) => void;

/** draw a sheet; `sideOnly` creatures (bugs, bat) only ever show row 2, so it is drawn once and copied */
function sheet(draw: CellFn, sideOnly = false): THREE.DataTexture {
  const S = CELL, out = canvas(S * 4, S * 3), o = get2d(out);
  for (let v = sideOnly ? 2 : 0; v < 3; v++) for (let f = 0; f < 4; f++) {
    o.save();
    o.beginPath(); o.rect(f * S, v * S, S, S); o.clip(); // cells never draw into each other
    o.setTransform(U, 0, 0, U, f * S, v * S);
    draw(new Pen(o), v, f);
    o.restore();
  }
  if (sideOnly) for (let v = 0; v < 2; v++) o.drawImage(out, 0, S * 2, S * 4, S, 0, S * v, S * 4, S);
  const img = o.getImageData(0, 0, out.width, out.height);
  inkContour(img, OUTER * U);
  return bake(img, { flipY: true, alphaTest: true, levels: 7 });
}

const PLAYER_PAL: Palette = {
  skin: '#f6cfa6', skinDark: '#d89c7c', eye: '#2a2440', hair: '#6a3e26',
  shirt: '#efe6cc', shirtLight: '#fffaea', shirtDark: '#c8b896', belt: '#7a4a28',
  pants: '#465478', pantsDark: '#323c5c', boots: '#5a3624',
  cape: '#2f9a92', capeDark: '#1f706e', accent: '#f2c440',
  hat: '#9a6236', hatBand: '#cc4a3a',
};
const ZOMBIE_PALS: Palette[] = [
  { skin: '#9cc086', skinDark: '#6e9264', eye: '#ff4a3a', hair: '#3a3428', shirt: '#5e7494', shirtLight: '#7a90b0', shirtDark: '#465670', belt: '#3a2a20', pants: '#6a5640', pantsDark: '#4e3e2e', boots: '#33281f' },
  { skin: '#aec48e', skinDark: '#7f9464', eye: '#ffaa20', hair: '#7a3220', shirt: '#9a5240', shirtLight: '#b46a54', shirtDark: '#6e3a2e', belt: '#2a2a2a', pants: '#44583e', pantsDark: '#32422e', boots: '#262626' },
  { skin: '#88aa98', skinDark: '#5e7e70', eye: '#ff3aa0', hair: '#d0c8b0', shirt: '#74745e', shirtLight: '#8e8e78', shirtDark: '#545446', belt: '#4a3020', pants: '#56446a', pantsDark: '#403350', boots: '#2a2030' },
];
const BRUTE_PAL: Palette = { skin: '#94a670', skinDark: '#62744c', eye: '#ffdd30', hair: '#2a2420', shirt: '#5a4434', shirtLight: '#6e5644', shirtDark: '#3e3026', belt: '#7a6a5a', pants: '#3e3e4a', pantsDark: '#2c2c36', boots: '#22222a' };

export interface SpriteSheets {
  player: THREE.Texture; brute: THREE.Texture; bat: THREE.Texture;
  zombies: THREE.Texture[]; crawler: THREE.Texture; spitter: THREE.Texture;
}

/**
 * The character sheets. Drawing and baking one high-res sheet takes a noticeable fraction of a second,
 * so only the player's is made up front: the rest are lazy (built on first use) and are also warmed up
 * one per idle moment right after start, so in practice no spawn ever waits on a drawing.
 */
export function makeSheets(): SpriteSheets {
  const lazy = (build: () => THREE.DataTexture): (() => THREE.DataTexture) => { let t: THREE.DataTexture | null = null; return () => (t ??= build()); };
  const brute = lazy(() => sheet((p, v, f) => drawHumanoid(p, v, f, BRUTE_PAL, 'brute')));
  const bat = lazy(() => sheet((p, _v, f) => drawBat(p, f), true));
  const crawler = lazy(() => sheet((p, _v, f) => drawCrawler(p, f), true));
  const spitter = lazy(() => sheet((p, _v, f) => drawSpitter(p, f), true));
  const zombie = ZOMBIE_PALS.map(pal => lazy(() => sheet((p, v, f) => drawHumanoid(p, v, f, pal, 'zombie'))));
  const zombies: THREE.Texture[] = [];
  zombie.forEach((z, i) => Object.defineProperty(zombies, i, { get: z, enumerable: true }));
  const sheets: SpriteSheets = {
    player: sheet((p, v, f) => drawHumanoid(p, v, f, PLAYER_PAL, 'player')),
    get brute() { return brute(); }, get bat() { return bat(); },
    get crawler() { return crawler(); }, get spitter() { return spitter(); },
    zombies,
  };
  const warm = [...zombie, crawler, spitter, brute, bat];
  const idle = (fn: () => void): void => { if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) requestIdleCallback(fn, { timeout: 2000 }); else setTimeout(fn, 60); };
  const next = (): void => { const w = warm.shift(); if (w) { w(); idle(next); } };
  idle(next);
  return sheets;
}
```

### src/art/draw.ts
```
// Shared vector-art toolkit: canvases, an inked-cartoon "pen" (flat fills, cel-shade crescents,
// round-capped limbs, a thick outer contour) and the texture baker that turns a drawn canvas into a
// mipmapped DataTexture with clean alpha edges (no dark fringes, no alpha-tested features eroding
// away in the distance).
import * as THREE from 'three';

export type Ctx = CanvasRenderingContext2D;

/** warm near-black ink shared by every character, item and icon outline */
export const INK = '#2a1d29';

export function canvas(w: number, h: number): HTMLCanvasElement { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; return c; }

export function get2d(c: HTMLCanvasElement): Ctx {
  // (no willReadFrequently: that forces slow software rendering, and each drawing is read back only once)
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('2d canvas context unavailable');
  return ctx;
}

// ---------------- colour helpers ----------------
function hexRgb(h: string): [number, number, number] {
  const n = parseInt(h.slice(1), 16);
  return h.length === 4
    ? [((n >> 8) & 15) * 17, ((n >> 4) & 15) * 17, (n & 15) * 17]
    : [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255];
}
/** mix two #rrggbb colours (t = 0 -> a) */
export function mix(a: string, b: string, t: number): string {
  const A = hexRgb(a), B = hexRgb(b);
  const c = (i: number): string => Math.round(A[i] + (B[i] - A[i]) * t).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
  return '#' + c(0) + c(1) + c(2);
}
/** darker, slightly cooler/purpler tone for cel shadows (shadows tint toward violet, not grey) */
export const shade = (c: string, k = 0.28): string => mix(c, '#2c1f45', k);
/** lighter, warmer tone for rim highlights */
export const light = (c: string, k = 0.3): string => mix(c, '#fff6dc', k);

// ---------------- path helpers ----------------
/** tapered capsule from (x0,y0) radius r0 to (x1,y1) radius r1 */
export function capsule(x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number, r0: number, r1 = r0): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D(), a = Math.atan2(y1 - y0, x1 - x0), h = Math.PI / 2;
  p.arc(x1, y1, r1, a - h, a + h);
  p.arc(x0, y0, r0, a + h, a + h * 3);
  p.closePath();
  return p;
}
export function ellipse(cx: number, cy: number, rx: number, ry: number, rot = 0): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D(); p.ellipse(cx, cy, Math.max(0.01, rx), Math.max(0.01, ry), rot, 0, Math.PI * 2); return p;
}
export function rrect(x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, r: number): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D(); p.roundRect(x, y, w, h, r); return p;
}
/** closed smooth blob through points (Catmull-Rom converted to cubic beziers) */
export function blob(pts: number[][], tension = 1): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D(), n = pts.length;
  p.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const p0 = pts[(i - 1 + n) % n], p1 = pts[i], p2 = pts[(i + 1) % n], p3 = pts[(i + 2) % n];
    const k = tension / 6;
    p.bezierCurveTo(p1[0] + (p2[0] - p0[0]) * k, p1[1] + (p2[1] - p0[1]) * k, p2[0] - (p3[0] - p1[0]) * k, p2[1] - (p3[1] - p1[1]) * k, p2[0], p2[1]);
  }
  p.closePath();
  return p;
}
/** closed polygon (sharp corners: torn hems, teeth, shards) */
export function poly(pts: number[][]): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D(); p.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) p.lineTo(pts[i][0], pts[i][1]);
  p.closePath(); return p;
}
/** open smooth curve through points (for strokes) */
export function curve(pts: number[][]): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D(); p.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
  if (pts.length === 2) { p.lineTo(pts[1][0], pts[1][1]); return p; }
  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
    const mx = (pts[i][0] + pts[i + 1][0]) / 2, my = (pts[i][1] + pts[i + 1][1]) / 2;
    p.quadraticCurveTo(pts[i][0], pts[i][1], i === pts.length - 2 ? pts[i + 1][0] : mx, i === pts.length - 2 ? pts[i + 1][1] : my);
  }
  return p;
}
const shifted = (p: Path2D, dx: number, dy: number): Path2D => { const q = new Path2D(); q.addPath(p, new DOMMatrix().translate(dx, dy)); return q; };

export interface ShapeOpts {
  /** cel-shadow colour (crescent on the side away from the top-left light); null for none */
  shade?: string | null;
  /** highlight colour (thin crescent toward the light) */
  light?: string | null;
  /** shadow crescent depth (drawing units) */
  sd?: number;
  /** highlight crescent depth */
  ld?: number;
  /** ink line width (0 = no outline) */
  lw?: number;
  ink?: string;
}

/**
 * The inked-cartoon pen. Works in "drawing units" (the caller sets up a scale on the context), so the
 * same drawing code renders a 32-unit character cell at any pixel resolution.
 */
export class Pen {
  ctx: Ctx;
  lw: number; // default ink width
  constructor(ctx: Ctx, lw = 0.55) { this.ctx = ctx; this.lw = lw; }

  /** flat fill + cel shadow crescent + rim light + ink outline */
  shape(p: Path2D, fill: string, o: ShapeOpts = {}): void {
    const c = this.ctx;
    c.fillStyle = fill; c.fill(p);
    const sh = o.shade === undefined ? shade(fill) : o.shade;
    const li = o.light === undefined ? light(fill, 0.22) : o.light;
    if (sh || li) {
      c.save(); c.clip(p);
      // a crescent is the shape minus a copy of itself shifted toward the light: even-odd fill of
      // the pair, clipped to the shape, leaves exactly the far rim
      if (sh) {
        const d = o.sd ?? 1.1, q = new Path2D(); q.addPath(p); q.addPath(p, new DOMMatrix().translate(-d * 0.8, -d));
        c.fillStyle = sh; c.fill(q, 'evenodd');
      }
      if (li) {
        const d = o.ld ?? 0.55, q = new Path2D(); q.addPath(p); q.addPath(p, new DOMMatrix().translate(d * 0.8, d));
        c.fillStyle = li; c.fill(q, 'evenodd');
      }
      c.restore();
    }
    const lw = o.lw ?? this.lw;
    if (lw > 0) { c.lineWidth = lw; c.strokeStyle = o.ink ?? INK; c.lineJoin = 'round'; c.lineCap = 'round'; c.stroke(p); }
  }

  /** a bent limb as a round-capped polyline: ink underlay, shade, then a lit core offset to the light */
  limb(pts: number[][], w: number, col: string, o: { lw?: number; shade?: string | null; ink?: string } = {}): void {
    const c = this.ctx, p = curve(pts), lw = o.lw ?? this.lw;
    c.lineJoin = 'round'; c.lineCap = 'round';
    if (lw > 0) { c.strokeStyle = o.ink ?? INK; c.lineWidth = w + lw * 2; c.stroke(p); }
    const sh = o.shade === undefined ? shade(col) : o.shade;
    c.strokeStyle = sh ?? col; c.lineWidth = w; c.stroke(p);
    if (sh) { c.strokeStyle = col; c.lineWidth = w * 0.62; c.stroke(shifted(p, -w * 0.14, -w * 0.1)); }
  }

  /** a plain stroke (seams, creases, stitches, cracks) */
  line(p: Path2D, w: number, col = INK, dash?: number[]): void {
    const c = this.ctx;
    c.lineJoin = 'round'; c.lineCap = 'round'; c.lineWidth = w; c.strokeStyle = col;
    if (dash) c.setLineDash(dash);
    c.stroke(p);
    if (dash) c.setLineDash([]);
  }
  fill(p: Path2D, col: string | CanvasGradient): void { this.ctx.fillStyle = col; this.ctx.fill(p); }
  dot(x: number, y: number, r: number, col: string): void { this.fill(ellipse(x, y, r, r), col); }
}

/**
 * Thicken the outer silhouette of a drawing (on a transparent background) by `r` px of ink, composited
 * underneath it. A heavier outer contour than the inner lines is what makes cartoon figures pop against
 * busy backgrounds, and the extra width keeps the alpha-tested silhouette solid in the smaller mips.
 * Exact Euclidean distance to the opaque area, but only out to r + 1 px: a row pass finds the horizontal
 * distance to the nearest opaque texel, then a column pass takes min(dx² + dy²) over the few rows in
 * reach. Both passes walk memory in order, so a whole sheet takes a few ms.
 */
export function inkContour(img: ImageData, r: number, ink = INK): void {
  const { width: w, height: h, data } = img, R = Math.ceil(r + 1), FAR = R + 1;
  const hd = new Uint8Array(w * h);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    const o = y * w;
    let d = FAR;
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) { d = data[(o + x) * 4 + 3] >= 128 ? 0 : Math.min(FAR, d + 1); hd[o + x] = d; }
    d = FAR;
    for (let x = w - 1; x >= 0; x--) { d = hd[o + x] === 0 ? 0 : Math.min(FAR, d + 1); if (d < hd[o + x]) hd[o + x] = d; }
  }
  const n0 = parseInt(ink.slice(1), 16), ir = (n0 >> 16) & 255, ig = (n0 >> 8) & 255, ib = n0 & 255;
  const best = new Float32Array(w);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    best.fill(1e9);
    for (let dy = -R; dy <= R; dy++) {
      const yy = y + dy;
      if (yy < 0 || yy >= h) continue;
      const o = yy * w, dy2 = dy * dy;
      for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) { const dx = hd[o + x]; if (dx < FAR) { const d2 = dx * dx + dy2; if (d2 < best[x]) best[x] = d2; } }
    }
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      if (best[x] >= 1e9) continue;
      const ai = Math.min(1, r + 0.5 - Math.sqrt(best[x]));
      if (ai <= 0) continue;
      const k = (y * w + x) * 4, ad = data[k + 3] / 255;
      if (ad >= 1) continue;
      const ao = ad + ai * (1 - ad), wi = ai * (1 - ad);
      data[k] = (data[k] * ad + ir * wi) / ao; data[k + 1] = (data[k + 1] * ad + ig * wi) / ao; data[k + 2] = (data[k + 2] * ad + ib * wi) / ao;
      data[k + 3] = ao * 255;
    }
  }
}

// ---------------- texture baking ----------------
interface Level { data: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>; width: number; height: number; }

/** fill fully transparent texels with the average colour of opaque neighbours (a few rings outward), so
 *  linear filtering across the silhouette blends toward the edge colour instead of toward black. Works on
 *  a frontier list, so the cost is proportional to the outline length, not the image area. */
const N8X = [-1, 0, 1, -1, 1, -1, 0, 1], N8Y = [-1, -1, -1, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1];
function bleed(d: Uint8Array, w: number, h: number, passes: number): void {
  const n = w * h, state = new Uint8Array(n); // 0 unknown, 1 known, 2 queued in the frontier
  let front = new Int32Array(n), fl = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (d[i * 4 + 3] > 0) state[i] = 1;
  const enqueueAround = (i: number, list: Int32Array, len: number): number => {
    const x = i % w, y = (i / w) | 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
      const xx = x + N8X[k], yy = y + N8Y[k];
      if (xx < 0 || yy < 0 || xx >= w || yy >= h) continue;
      const j = yy * w + xx;
      if (state[j] === 0) { state[j] = 2; list[len++] = j; }
    }
    return len;
  };
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (state[i] === 1) {
    const x = i % w, y = (i / w) | 0;
    // only opaque texels on the edge seed the frontier
    if ((x > 0 && state[i - 1] === 0) || (x < w - 1 && state[i + 1] === 0) || (y > 0 && state[i - w] === 0) || (y < h - 1 && state[i + w] === 0)
      || (x > 0 && y > 0 && state[i - w - 1] === 0) || (x < w - 1 && y < h - 1 && state[i + w + 1] === 0) || (x > 0 && y < h - 1 && state[i + w - 1] === 0) || (x < w - 1 && y > 0 && state[i - w + 1] === 0)) fl = enqueueAround(i, front, fl);
  }
  let next = new Int32Array(n);
  for (let p = 0; p < passes && fl > 0; p++) {
    for (let f = 0; f < fl; f++) {
      const i = front[f], x = i % w, y = (i / w) | 0;
      let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0, c = 0;
      for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
        const xx = x + N8X[k], yy = y + N8Y[k];
        if (xx < 0 || yy < 0 || xx >= w || yy >= h) continue;
        const j = yy * w + xx;
        if (state[j] !== 1) continue;
        r += d[j * 4]; g += d[j * 4 + 1]; b += d[j * 4 + 2]; c++;
      }
      if (c) { d[i * 4] = r / c; d[i * 4 + 1] = g / c; d[i * 4 + 2] = b / c; }
    }
    let nl = 0;
    for (let f = 0; f < fl; f++) state[front[f]] = 1; // the whole ring becomes known at once
    if (p + 1 < passes) for (let f = 0; f < fl; f++) nl = enqueueAround(front[f], next, nl);
    const t = front; front = next; next = t; fl = nl;
  }
}

/** 2x box downsample; colour averaged with alpha weights (premultiplied) so transparent texels never
 *  darken the edge, then alpha rescaled so the share of texels passing alphaTest (0.5) stays the same as
 *  in the full-size image ("alpha-tested coverage preservation": thin blades and limbs don't melt away) */
function downsample(src: Level, coverage: number): Level {
  const w = Math.max(1, src.width >> 1), h = Math.max(1, src.height >> 1), s = src.data;
  const out = new Uint8Array(w * h * 4), alpha = new Float32Array(w * h);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
    let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0, a = 0, rr = 0, gg = 0, bb = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
      const sx = Math.min(src.width - 1, x * 2 + (k & 1)), sy = Math.min(src.height - 1, y * 2 + (k >> 1));
      const j = (sy * src.width + sx) * 4, al = s[j + 3] / 255;
      r += s[j] * al; g += s[j + 1] * al; b += s[j + 2] * al; a += al;
      rr += s[j]; gg += s[j + 1]; bb += s[j + 2];
    }
    const i = (y * w + x) * 4;
    if (a > 1e-3) { out[i] = r / a; out[i + 1] = g / a; out[i + 2] = b / a; }
    else { out[i] = rr / 4; out[i + 1] = gg / 4; out[i + 2] = bb / 4; }
    alpha[y * w + x] = a / 4;
  }
  let scale = 1;
  if (coverage > 0) {
    // the alpha level a* that exactly `coverage` of the texels reach (from a histogram), then scale so
    // a* maps to the 0.5 test threshold
    const B = 1024, hist = new Uint32Array(B);
    for (let i = 0; i < alpha.length; i++) hist[Math.min(B - 1, (alpha[i] * B) | 0)]++;
    let need = coverage * alpha.length, k = B - 1;
    for (; k > 0; k--) { need -= hist[k]; if (need <= 0) break; }
    scale = Math.min(4, Math.max(0.5, 0.5 / Math.max(1 / B, k / B)));
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) out[i * 4 + 3] = Math.min(255, Math.round(alpha[i] * scale * 255));
  return { data: out, width: w, height: h };
}

export interface BakeOpts {
  /** flip rows so canvas top is v = 1 (what a CanvasTexture with flipY does) */
  flipY?: boolean;
  /** colour texture (sRGB) vs raw data (detail multipliers) */
  srgb?: boolean;
  /** number of mip levels to build (the chain stops early: a short chain caps how blurry the texture
   *  gets, which is also how atlas gutters are kept wider than the filter footprint) */
  levels?: number;
  /** preserve alpha-tested coverage across mips */
  alphaTest?: boolean;
  /** transparent-texel colour bleed rings per level */
  bleed?: number;
  anisotropy?: number;
}

/**
 * Bake a drawn canvas into a mipmapped DataTexture (manual mips: three uploads them as-is with
 * texStorage). Linear filtering + trilinear mips give smooth, alias-free vector art at every zoom.
 */
export function bake(src: HTMLCanvasElement | ImageData, o: BakeOpts = {}): THREE.DataTexture {
  const w = src.width, h = src.height;
  const img = src instanceof ImageData ? src : get2d(src).getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
  let lvl: Level = { data: new Uint8Array(img.data.buffer), width: w, height: h };
  const bl = o.bleed ?? 2;
  if (bl) bleed(lvl.data, w, h, bl);
  let coverage = 0;
  if (o.alphaTest) { let n = 0; for (let i = 3; i < lvl.data.length; i += 4) if (lvl.data[i] >= 128) n++; coverage = n / (w * h); }
  const maxLevels = o.levels ?? Math.floor(Math.log2(Math.max(w, h))) + 1;
  const levels: Level[] = [lvl];
  while (levels.length < maxLevels && (lvl.width > 1 || lvl.height > 1)) {
    lvl = downsample(lvl, coverage);
    if (bl) bleed(lvl.data, lvl.width, lvl.height, 2);
    levels.push(lvl);
  }
  // keep a canvas of the source drawing (before the in-place row flip) for debugging / previews
  let preview = src as HTMLCanvasElement;
  if (src instanceof ImageData) { preview = canvas(w, h); get2d(preview).putImageData(img, 0, 0); }
  if (o.flipY) for (const L of levels) {
    const row = L.width * 4, tmp = new Uint8Array(row);
    for (let y = 0; y < L.height >> 1; y++) {
      const a = y * row, b = (L.height - 1 - y) * row;
      tmp.set(L.data.subarray(a, a + row)); L.data.copyWithin(a, b, b + row); L.data.set(tmp, b);
    }
  }
  const tex = new THREE.DataTexture(levels[0].data, w, h, THREE.RGBAFormat, THREE.UnsignedByteType);
  tex.mipmaps = levels as unknown as THREE.DataTexture['mipmaps'];
  tex.generateMipmaps = false;
  tex.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
  tex.minFilter = levels.length > 1 ? THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter : THREE.LinearFilter;
  tex.anisotropy = o.anisotropy ?? 4;
  if (o.srgb !== false) tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.userData.canvas = preview; // the source drawing, for debugging / previews
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}
```

### src/art/foliage.ts
```
// Foliage atlas: 8 crossed-card decor tiles (3 grass tufts, 3 flowers, a mushroom, a fern) as smooth
// vector drawings. The mesher maps tile t to u in [t/8, (t+1)/8], v 0 (ground) .. 1 (top), so the atlas
// is one row of square tiles; every tile keeps a transparent margin at its sides so trilinear sampling
// never pulls colour from the neighbouring tile.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { Pen, canvas, get2d, bake, ellipse, blob, curve, mix, shade } from './draw';

const T = 128;          // px per tile
const K = T / 16;       // drawn in a 16-unit space (ground at y = 16)
const LEAF_INK = '#3e6a32'; // a deep green rather than black: ground cover should not out-shout the characters

/** a tapered, curving grass blade from a base point to a tip, filled with a base-to-tip gradient */
function blade(pen: Pen, x: number, h: number, lean: number, w: number, dark: string, lit: string): void {
  const c = pen.ctx, tipX = x + lean, tipY = 16 - h;
  const p = new Path2D();
  p.moveTo(x - w / 2, 16.4);
  p.quadraticCurveTo(x - w * 0.4 + lean * 0.25, 16 - h * 0.55, tipX, tipY);
  p.quadraticCurveTo(x + w * 0.4 + lean * 0.45, 16 - h * 0.5, x + w / 2, 16.4);
  p.closePath();
  const g = c.createLinearGradient(0, 16, 0, tipY);
  g.addColorStop(0, dark); g.addColorStop(1, lit);
  c.fillStyle = g; c.fill(p);
  c.lineWidth = 0.24; c.strokeStyle = LEAF_INK; c.lineJoin = 'round'; c.stroke(p);
  // a lit midrib on the sunny side
  pen.line(curve([[x - w * 0.1, 15.6], [x + lean * 0.3 - w * 0.12, 16 - h * 0.5], [tipX - lean * 0.08, tipY + h * 0.12]]), w * 0.18, mix(lit, '#fffbd8', 0.35));
}

function tuft(pen: Pen, n: number, seed: number, hMin: number, hMax: number): void {
  let s = seed;
  const r = (): number => ((s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647);
  // (bright: the cards sit in their own and their neighbours' shadow, and in SSAO, so they render a good
  // deal darker than drawn; these keep tufts reading as light strokes over the ground's grass colour)
  const greens: [string, string][] = [['#6aac48', '#c8f088'], ['#76b852', '#d6f89a'], ['#62a440', '#bce67c'], ['#7cc058', '#e2fca8']];
  // back blades darker, front blades brighter: drawn back to front
  const blades: { x: number; h: number; l: number; w: number; k: number }[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const x = 3.2 + r() * 9.6;
    blades.push({ x, h: hMin + r() * (hMax - hMin), l: (x - 8) * 0.45 + (r() - 0.5) * 3, w: 1.5 + r() * 0.8, k: i });
  }
  blades.sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
  for (const b of blades) {
    const [d, l] = greens[b.k % 4], f = b.k / n;
    blade(pen, b.x, b.h, b.l, b.w, mix(d, '#2a4a20', 0.18 * (1 - f)), l);
  }
}

function flower(pen: Pen, petal: string, centre: string, seed: number): void {
  // leaves and stems
  blade(pen, 5.0, 5.5, -2.2, 1.6, '#4a8a34', '#9ad064');
  blade(pen, 11.2, 4.8, 2.2, 1.6, '#4a8a34', '#9ad064');
  const heads: [number, number, number][] = [[8.2, 3.6, 1.0], [4.0, 8.2, 0.7], [12.4, 9.0, 0.66]];
  for (const [hx, hy] of heads) { // inked stems
    const stem = curve([[8 + (hx - 8) * 0.2, 16.2], [hx + (8 - hx) * 0.2, (hy + 16) / 2], [hx, hy + 0.6]]);
    pen.line(stem, 0.62, LEAF_INK); pen.line(stem, 0.36, '#5a9a3a');
  }
  heads.forEach(([hx, hy, s], i) => {
    const n = 5, R = 1.55 * s, rot = seed + i * 0.7;
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      const a = rot + (k / n) * Math.PI * 2, px = hx + Math.cos(a) * R, py = hy + Math.sin(a) * R * 0.85;
      pen.shape(ellipse(px, py, 1.25 * s, 0.95 * s, a), petal, { shade: shade(petal, 0.22), light: mix(petal, '#ffffff', 0.45), sd: 0.45 * s, ld: 0.3 * s, lw: 0.26, ink: mix(petal, '#2a1420', 0.7) });
    }
    pen.shape(ellipse(hx, hy, 0.95 * s, 0.85 * s), centre, { shade: shade(centre, 0.35), sd: 0.4, lw: 0.24, ink: '#5a3a10' });
  });
}

function mushroom(pen: Pen): void {
  // a small one behind, a big toadstool in front
  pen.shape(blob([[11.4, 16.3], [11.5, 12.6], [12.6, 12.5], [12.7, 16.3]], 0.6), '#efe4d0', { sd: 0.4, lw: 0.3 });
  pen.shape(blob([[9.8, 12.9], [12.0, 10.6], [14.4, 12.9], [12.1, 13.5]], 0.9), '#c8904a', { sd: 0.5, lw: 0.3 });
  const stem = blob([[6.6, 16.4], [6.9, 10.0], [8.9, 9.8], [9.5, 16.4]], 0.6);
  pen.shape(stem, '#f2e8d4', { shade: '#cbb898', sd: 0.7, lw: 0.34 });
  pen.fill(ellipse(8.0, 11.0, 2.2, 0.6), 'rgba(80,40,30,0.35)'); // shadow under the cap
  const cap = blob([[2.6, 10.4], [4.0, 6.2], [8.0, 4.4], [12.2, 6.2], [13.4, 10.4], [8.0, 11.4]], 0.95);
  pen.shape(cap, '#d8402e', { shade: '#9a2a2a', light: '#ff7a5a', sd: 1.1, ld: 0.5, lw: 0.36 });
  for (const [x, y, r] of [[5.4, 7.6, 0.8], [8.6, 6.0, 0.95], [11.2, 8.4, 0.7], [7.2, 9.4, 0.5], [10.0, 9.8, 0.45]]) pen.shape(ellipse(x, y, r, r * 0.85), '#fff6ea', { shade: '#e0cdbc', light: null, sd: 0.25, lw: 0.2 });
}

function fern(pen: Pen): void {
  const fronds: [number, number, number][] = [[8.0, 13.2, 0.1], [7.6, 10.4, -1.1], [8.4, 10.8, 1.1]];
  fronds.sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
  for (const [bx, len, bend] of fronds) {
    // the rachis curves outward and droops; leaflets taper toward the tip, alternating sides
    const pts: [number, number][] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= 10; i++) {
      const t = i / 10;
      pts.push([bx + bend * len * 0.55 * t * t + Math.sign(bend) * t * 0.6, 16.2 - len * t + (Math.abs(bend) > 0.5 ? t * t * len * 0.28 : 0)]);
    }
    for (let i = 1; i < 10; i++) {
      const [x, y] = pts[i], t = i / 10, L = 2.6 * (1 - t * 0.75), [nx, ny] = pts[i + 1];
      const dx = nx - x, dy = ny - y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1, ax = dx / d, ay = dy / d;
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
        const ex = x + (-ay * s) * L + ax * L * 0.45, ey = y + (ax * s) * L + ay * L * 0.45;
        const leaf = blob([[x, y], [(x + ex) / 2 - ay * s * 0.1 + ax * 0.5, (y + ey) / 2 + ax * s * 0.1 + ay * 0.5], [ex, ey], [(x + ex) / 2 - ax * 0.3, (y + ey) / 2 - ay * 0.3]], 0.9);
        pen.shape(leaf, s > 0 ? '#5a9c3c' : '#4a8a32', { shade: '#2e6224', light: '#9ed266', sd: 0.4, ld: 0.2, lw: 0.24, ink: LEAF_INK });
      }
    }
    pen.line(curve(pts), 0.42, LEAF_INK);
    pen.line(curve(pts), 0.22, '#7ab84a');
  }
}

export function makeFoliageAtlas(): THREE.DataTexture {
  const c = canvas(T * 8, T), ctx = get2d(c);
  const tile = (t: number, draw: (pen: Pen) => void): void => {
    ctx.save(); ctx.setTransform(K, 0, 0, K, t * T, 0);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0.35, -1, 15.3, 18); ctx.clip(); // side margins: no bleed between tiles
    draw(new Pen(ctx, 0.3));
    ctx.restore();
  };
  tile(0, p => tuft(p, 7, 11, 4.0, 8.0));
  tile(1, p => tuft(p, 9, 23, 5.0, 10.5));
  tile(2, p => tuft(p, 11, 37, 6.0, 13.0));
  tile(3, p => flower(p, '#ff5a6e', '#ffe08a', 0.3));
  tile(4, p => flower(p, '#ffd23a', '#c86a20', 1.1));
  tile(5, p => flower(p, '#7ab8ff', '#fff4c0', 0.7));
  tile(6, mushroom);
  tile(7, fern);
  return bake(c, { flipY: true, alphaTest: true, levels: 6 });
}
```

```
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {

  },
  pick: (p) => {
    p.shape(capsule(3.4, 14.0, 10.4, 4.4, 0.9, 0.9), '#9a6236', { light: '#c88a54', sd: 0.5 });
    p.shape(blob([[1.4, 6.4], [5.0, 2.6], [9.6, 1.4], [14.4, 3.2], [15.0, 6.6], [12.6, 4.6], [9.4, 3.8], [4.4, 5.4]], 0.8), '#9aa4ac', { light: '#e0e8ee', sd: 0.8 });
  },
};

const iconCache: Record<string, string> = {};
export function icon(name: string): string {
  if (iconCache[name]) return iconCache[name];
  const c = canvas(ICON, ICON), ctx = get2d(c);
  ctx.setTransform(K * 0.9, 0, 0, K * 0.9, ICON * 0.05, ICON * 0.05); // a little margin for the outer contour
  (ICONS[name] || ICONS.stone)(new Pen(ctx, 0.5));
  const img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, ICON, ICON);
  inkContour(img, 0.45 * K);
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  return (iconCache[name] = c.toDataURL());
}
```

### src/art/tiles.ts
```
// Voxel detail atlas: one smooth, painterly, seamlessly tiling greyscale tile per material. The world
// shader multiplies a face's colour by (0.55 + v * 0.9), so 128 is neutral. Tiles are painted in a float
// field with wrap-around brush dabs, periodic noise and periodic Voronoi cells, so every operation tiles.
//
// Atlas layout: 4 x 4 cells of CELL px; each holds a TILE px tile surrounded by a GUTTER of wrapped
// texels (the tile's own opposite edges), so bilinear filtering across a block edge reads the right
// neighbour. The mip chain stops at level MAX_LOD, where the gutter is still a full texel wide, so no
// level ever blends one material into the next.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { canvas, get2d, bake } from './draw';

export const TILE = { GRASS: 0, DIRT: 1, STONE: 2, SAND: 3, BARK: 4, RINGS: 5, LEAVES: 6, PLANKS: 7, BRICK: 8, ORE: 9, CRATE: 10, TNT: 11, COBBLE: 12, ROOF: 13, METAL: 14, PLAIN: 15 } as const;

/** texels per block face */
export const DETAIL_TILE = 96;
/** wrapped border around each tile */
export const DETAIL_GUTTER = 16;
/** cell pitch in the atlas (tile + 2 gutters) */
export const DETAIL_CELL = DETAIL_TILE + DETAIL_GUTTER * 2;
/** atlas side (4 cells) */
export const DETAIL_SIZE = DETAIL_CELL * 4;
/** last mip level built (gutter 16 >> 4 = 1 texel there) */
const MAX_LOD = 4;

const T = DETAIL_TILE;
type Field = Float32Array;

// ---------------- painting primitives (all wrap around the tile) ----------------
let seed = 7;
const rnd = (): number => ((seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647);
const field = (v = 128): Field => new Float32Array(T * T).fill(v);
const wrap = (i: number): number => ((i % T) + T) % T;
const smooth = (t: number): number => t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
const sstep = (a: number, b: number, x: number): number => smooth(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (x - a) / (b - a))));
/** shortest wrapped offset between two coordinates */
const wd = (a: number, b: number): number => { let d = a - b; d -= Math.round(d / T) * T; return d; };

/** periodic value noise: an nx x ny lattice of random values spanning the tile, quintic-interpolated */
function lattice(nx: number, ny: number): (x: number, y: number) => number {
  const g = new Float32Array(nx * ny);
  for (let i = 0; i < g.length; i++) g[i] = rnd() * 2 - 1;
  return (x, y) => {
    const fx = (x / T) * nx, fy = (y / T) * ny;
    const ix = Math.floor(fx), iy = Math.floor(fy), tx = fx - ix, ty = fy - iy;
    const q = (t: number): number => t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
    const at = (i: number, j: number): number => g[(((j % ny) + ny) % ny) * nx + (((i % nx) + nx) % nx)];
    const a = at(ix, iy), b = at(ix + 1, iy), c = at(ix, iy + 1), d = at(ix + 1, iy + 1);
    const u = q(tx), v = q(ty);
    return (a + (b - a) * u) * (1 - v) + (c + (d - c) * u) * v;
  };
}
/** fractal sum of periodic noise octaves (lattice doubles each octave so it stays periodic) */
function fbm(nx: number, ny: number, oct: number): (x: number, y: number) => number {
  const ls = Array.from({ length: oct }, (_, o) => lattice(nx << o, ny << o));
  return (x, y) => { let s = 0, a = 1, n = 0; for (const l of ls) { s += l(x, y) * a; n += a; a *= 0.5; } return s / n; };
}
function addNoise(f: Field, n: (x: number, y: number) => number, amp: number): void {
  for (let y = 0; y < T; y++) for (let x = 0; x < T; x++) f[y * T + x] += n(x, y) * amp;
}
/**
 * Paint a soft elliptical brush dab: inside the ellipse the field is blended toward `val(lx, ly)`
 * (lx, ly in -1..1 along the dab's own axes), with a feathered edge. Strokes overlap like paint.
 */
function dab(f: Field, cx: number, cy: number, rx: number, ry: number, ang: number, val: (lx: number, ly: number, cur: number) => number, soft = 0.35, alpha = 1): void {
  const R = Math.ceil(Math.max(rx, ry)) + 1, ca = Math.cos(ang), sa = Math.sin(ang);
  for (let dy = -R; dy <= R; dy++) for (let dx = -R; dx <= R; dx++) {
    const px = Math.round(cx) + dx, py = Math.round(cy) + dy;
    const ox = px - cx, oy = py - cy;
    const lx = (ox * ca + oy * sa) / rx, ly = (-ox * sa + oy * ca) / ry;
    const r = Math.hypot(lx, ly);
    if (r >= 1) continue;
    const a = alpha * sstep(1, 1 - soft, r);
    const k = wrap(py) * T + wrap(px);
    f[k] += (val(lx, ly, f[k]) - f[k]) * a;
  }
}
/** periodic Voronoi: for each texel the nearest / second-nearest site distances and the nearest site */
function voronoi(n: number, jitter = 0.8): { d1: Float32Array; d2: Float32Array; id: Int16Array; sx: number[]; sy: number[] } {
  const g = Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(n)), sx: number[] = [], sy: number[] = [];
  for (let j = 0; j < g; j++) for (let i = 0; i < g; i++) {
    sx.push(((i + 0.5 + (rnd() - 0.5) * jitter) / g) * T); sy.push(((j + 0.5 + (rnd() - 0.5) * jitter) / g) * T);
  }
  const d1 = new Float32Array(T * T), d2 = new Float32Array(T * T), id = new Int16Array(T * T);
  for (let y = 0; y < T; y++) for (let x = 0; x < T; x++) {
    let a = 1e9, b = 1e9, ai = 0;
    for (let s = 0; s < sx.length; s++) {
      const d = Math.hypot(wd(x, sx[s]), wd(y, sy[s]));
      if (d < a) { b = a; a = d; ai = s; } else if (d < b) b = d;
    }
    d1[y * T + x] = a; d2[y * T + x] = b; id[y * T + x] = ai;
  }
  return { d1, d2, id, sx, sy };
}
function normalize(f: Field, mean = 128): void {
  let s = 0; for (let i = 0; i < f.length; i++) s += f[i];
  const d = mean - s / f.length; for (let i = 0; i < f.length; i++) f[i] += d;
}
/** a small 3x3 blur pass (wrapping), to melt hard edges into a painted look */
function soften(f: Field, k = 1): void {
  for (let it = 0; it < k; it++) {
    const s = new Float32Array(f);
    for (let y = 0; y < T; y++) for (let x = 0; x < T; x++) {
      let a = 0;
      for (let j = -1; j <= 1; j++) for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) a += s[wrap(y + j) * T + wrap(x + i)] * (i === 0 && j === 0 ? 4 : (i === 0 || j === 0) ? 2 : 1);
      f[y * T + x] = a / 16;
    }
  }
}
const each = (f: Field, fn: (x: number, y: number, v: number) => number): void => { for (let y = 0; y < T; y++) for (let x = 0; x < T; x++) f[y * T + x] = fn(x, y, f[y * T + x]); };

/** dome-shaded pebble: lit top-left, shadowed bottom-right, with a soft cast shadow */
function pebble(f: Field, cx: number, cy: number, r: number, base: number, amp: number): void {
  dab(f, cx + r * 0.25, cy + r * 0.3, r * 1.05, r * 0.95, 0, (_x, _y, cur) => cur - amp * 0.9, 0.6, 0.7);
  dab(f, cx, cy, r, r * 0.85, rnd() * Math.PI, (lx, ly) => base + (-lx - ly) * amp * 0.5 + (1 - lx * lx - ly * ly) * amp * 0.3, 0.3);
}

// ---------------- the materials ----------------
function grass(): Field {
  const f = field(); addNoise(f, fbm(3, 3, 3), 18);
  // short painterly blade strokes, lights over darks, loosely combed in one direction
  for (let i = 0; i < 380; i++) {
    const amp = rnd() < 0.55 ? 26 + rnd() * 10 : -28, a = 0.5 + (rnd() - 0.5) * 1.2;
    dab(f, rnd() * T, rnd() * T, 1.0 + rnd() * 0.6, 3 + rnd() * 3.5, a, (_x, ly, cur) => cur + amp * (1 - ly * 0.4), 0.5, 0.8);
  }
  normalize(f); return f;
}
function dirt(): Field {
  const f = field(); addNoise(f, fbm(4, 4, 4), 26);
  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) dab(f, rnd() * T, rnd() * T, 1 + rnd() * 2, 0.8 + rnd() * 1.2, rnd() * 3, (_x, _y, cur) => cur - 14, 0.5, 0.8);
  for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) pebble(f, rnd() * T, rnd() * T, 2.2 + rnd() * 3.2, 128 + (rnd() - 0.5) * 20, 26);
  normalize(f); return f;
}
function stone(): Field {
  const f = field(), v = voronoi(5, 0.95), n = fbm(4, 4, 4), m = fbm(3, 3, 2), cell = v.sx.map(() => (rnd() - 0.5) * 22);
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const k = y * T + x, e = v.d2[k] - v.d1[k];
    // rough rock faces with a broken crack network: a noise mask fades parts of the cracks out, so it
    // reads as weathered stone rather than a flagstone grid repeating on every block
    const crack = (1 - sstep(0, 2.4, e)) * sstep(-0.15, 0.25, m(x, y));
    return 130 + cell[v.id[k]] * 0.7 + n(x, y) * 20 - 44 * crack + 8 * sstep(2.4, 5, e) * (1 - sstep(5, 9, e));
  });
  for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) dab(f, rnd() * T, rnd() * T, 2 + rnd() * 5, 1 + rnd() * 2, rnd() * 3, (_x, _y, cur) => cur + (rnd() < 0.5 ? 9 : -9), 0.8, 0.6);
  soften(f); normalize(f); return f;
}
function sand(): Field {
  const f = field(), w = lattice(2, 2);
  addNoise(f, fbm(6, 6, 3), 9);
  // wind ripples: whole numbers of waves across the tile so they wrap
  each(f, (x, y, v) => v + Math.sin(((y / T) * 4 + w(x, y) * 0.25 + Math.sin((x / T) * Math.PI * 2) * 0.12) * Math.PI * 2) * 8);
  for (let i = 0; i < 70; i++) dab(f, rnd() * T, rnd() * T, 0.7 + rnd() * 0.6, 0.7 + rnd() * 0.6, 0, (_x, _y, cur) => cur + (rnd() < 0.5 ? 18 : -16), 0.5, 0.8);
  normalize(f); return f;
}
function bark(): Field {
  const f = field(), streak = fbm(10, 2, 3), wob = lattice(4, 3);
  // vertical fibres and deep wandering furrows
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const u = x / T + wob(x, y) * 0.05;
    const fur = Math.abs(Math.sin(u * Math.PI * 5));
    return 132 + streak(x, y) * 26 - 54 * (1 - sstep(0.0, 0.32, fur)) + 10 * sstep(0.6, 1, fur);
  });
  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) dab(f, rnd() * T, rnd() * T, 0.8 + rnd(), 4 + rnd() * 6, (rnd() - 0.5) * 0.15, (_x, _y, cur) => cur + (rnd() < 0.5 ? 12 : -14), 0.6, 0.8);
  soften(f); return f;
}
function rings(): Field {
  const f = field(), n = fbm(3, 3, 3);
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const r = Math.hypot(x - T / 2 + 0.5, y - T / 2 + 0.5) + n(x, y) * 3.5;
    const R = T * 0.47;
    if (r > R) return 92 + n(x * 3 % T, y) * 14; // bark rim
    return 138 + Math.sin(r * 0.62) * 13 * (0.6 + 0.4 * r / R) - (r > R - 3 ? 26 : 0) - 10 * (1 - sstep(0, 4, r));
  });
  // a couple of radial drying cracks
  for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
    const a = rnd() * Math.PI * 2;
    for (let s = 6; s < T * 0.4; s += 0.8) dab(f, T / 2 + Math.cos(a + s * 0.01) * s, T / 2 + Math.sin(a + s * 0.01) * s, 0.9, 0.9, 0, (_x, _y, cur) => cur - 20, 0.5, 0.6);
  }
  soften(f); return f;
}
function leaves(): Field {
  const f = field(84); addNoise(f, fbm(3, 3, 3), 14);
  // clumps of overlapping pointed leaves over deep shadowy gaps: each leaf drops a soft shadow down and
  // to the right, then is painted lit on its upper half with a darker midrib; back to front, so later
  // leaves overlap earlier ones like brush strokes
  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
    const base = 138 + (rnd() - 0.5) * 40, a = rnd() * Math.PI, x = rnd() * T, y = rnd() * T;
    const rx = 4.5 + rnd() * 3.5, ry = 2.2 + rnd() * 1.4;
    dab(f, x + 1.6, y + 2.0, rx * 1.05, ry * 1.2, a, (_x, _y, cur) => cur - 26, 0.7, 0.6);
    dab(f, x, y, rx, ry, a, (lx, ly) => base - ly * 24 - lx * lx * 14 + (Math.abs(ly) < 0.1 ? -14 : 0), 0.25);
  }
  normalize(f); return f;
}
function planks(): Field {
  const f = field(), grain = fbm(2, 12, 3), wav = lattice(3, 4);
  const BH = T / 4;
  const joint = [0.62, 0.15, 0.85, 0.4].map(j => j * T), tone = [0, 8, -6, 4];
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const b = Math.floor(y / BH), ly = y - b * BH;
    let v = 130 + tone[b] + grain(x, y) * 12 + Math.sin((ly / BH) * Math.PI * 3 + wav(x, y) * 3 + x * 0.02) * 6;
    v += 12 * (1 - sstep(1.5, 4, ly)) - 16 * sstep(BH - 5, BH - 1.5, ly); // bevelled board edges
    v -= 52 * (1 - sstep(0, 1.6, Math.min(ly, BH - ly))); // seam
    const jd = Math.abs(wd(x, joint[b]));
    v -= 46 * (1 - sstep(0.5, 1.8, jd)); v += 10 * sstep(1.8, 3, jd) * (1 - sstep(3, 5, jd)) * (wd(x, joint[b]) > 0 ? 1 : -1);
    return v;
  });
  for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) for (const s of [-1, 1]) { // nails either side of each end joint
    const nx = joint[b] + s * 4.5, ny = b * BH + BH / 2 + (s > 0 ? -4 : 4);
    dab(f, nx + 0.6, ny + 0.6, 2.0, 2.0, 0, (_x, _y, cur) => cur - 30, 0.5);
    dab(f, nx, ny, 1.5, 1.5, 0, (lx, ly) => 150 - (lx + ly) * 30, 0.3);
  }
  // a knot or two
  for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) { const kx = rnd() * T, ky = (Math.floor(rnd() * 4) + 0.5) * BH; dab(f, kx, ky, 4.5, 2.6, 0, (lx, ly) => 100 + Math.sin(Math.hypot(lx, ly) * 9) * 12, 0.5); }
  soften(f); return f;
}
function bricks(): Field {
  const f = field(), n = fbm(4, 4, 3), CH = T / 4, BW = T / 2, M = 2.2;
  const tone = Array.from({ length: 16 }, () => (rnd() - 0.5) * 22);
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const row = Math.floor(y / CH), off = row % 2 ? BW / 2 : 0;
    const bx = wrap(x + off), col = Math.floor(bx / BW), lx = bx - col * BW, ly = y - row * CH;
    // signed distance to the rounded brick face (negative inside)
    const hx = BW / 2 - M, hy = CH / 2 - M, rr = 2.5;
    const qx = Math.abs(lx - BW / 2) - hx + rr, qy = Math.abs(ly - CH / 2) - hy + rr;
    const sd = Math.hypot(Math.max(qx, 0), Math.max(qy, 0)) + Math.min(Math.max(qx, qy), 0) - rr;
    const face = 122 + tone[(row * 2 + col) % 16] + n(x, y) * 12 + 12 * (1 - sstep(0, 4, ly - M)) - 14 * sstep(CH - M - 4, CH - M, ly) + 6 * (1 - sstep(0, 4, lx - M));
    const mortar = 170 + n(y, x) * 10 - 22 * (1 - sstep(0, 2, -sd + 2)); // mortar darkens into the joint
    return face + (mortar - face) * sstep(-0.8, 0.8, sd);
  });
  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) dab(f, rnd() * T, rnd() * T, 1 + rnd() * 2.5, 0.8 + rnd(), rnd() * 3, (_x, _y, cur) => cur + (rnd() < 0.5 ? 10 : -12), 0.7, 0.7);
  return f;
}
function ore(st: Field): Field {
  const f = new Float32Array(st);
  // glinting crystal clusters: faceted shards, bright faces with a dark rim
  for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
    const cx = rnd() * T, cy = rnd() * T;
    dab(f, cx + 1.5, cy + 2, 10, 8, 0, (_x, _y, cur) => cur - 30, 0.8);          // the pocket in the rock
    dab(f, cx, cy, 9, 8, 0, (_x, _y, cur) => cur + 22, 0.9, 0.6);                // soft glow around it
    for (let s = 0; s < 5; s++) {                                                 // crystals, radiating out
      const a = -Math.PI / 2 + (s - 2) * 0.5 + (rnd() - 0.5) * 0.3, l = 4.2 + rnd() * 2.4;
      dab(f, cx + Math.cos(a) * l * 0.6, cy + Math.sin(a) * l * 0.6, l, 1.6 + rnd() * 0.6, a, (lx, ly) => (ly < 0 ? 248 : 200) - Math.abs(lx) * 30, 0.2);
    }
  }
  return f;
}
function crate(): Field {
  const f = field(), grain = fbm(2, 10, 3), F = 12;
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const inner = x >= F && x < T - F && y >= F && y < T - F;
    const g = grain(inner ? x : y, inner ? y : x) * 10;
    let v: number;
    if (!inner) { // frame boards, bevelled
      const e = Math.min(x, y, T - 1 - x, T - 1 - y);
      v = 152 + g + 10 * (1 - sstep(0, 3, e)) - 12 * sstep(F - 4, F, e);
    } else { // horizontal slats behind the brace
      const ly = (y - F) % 18;
      v = 118 + g - 40 * (1 - sstep(0, 1.6, Math.min(ly, 18 - ly))) - 18 * (1 - sstep(0, 6, Math.min(x - F, y - F)));
      const db = Math.abs((x - F) - (T - F - 1 - y)) / Math.SQRT2; // diagonal brace
      if (db < 7) v = 150 + g - 18 * sstep(4.5, 7, db) * (x - F > T - F - 1 - y ? 1 : -0.4);
    }
    return v;
  });
  for (const [nx, ny] of [[6, 6], [T - 6, 6], [6, T - 6], [T - 6, T - 6]]) {
    dab(f, nx + 0.8, ny + 0.8, 2.6, 2.6, 0, (_x, _y, cur) => cur - 34, 0.5);
    dab(f, nx, ny, 2.0, 2.0, 0, (lx, ly) => 175 - (lx + ly) * 35, 0.3);
  }
  soften(f); return f;
}
function tnt(): Field {
  const f = field(), n = fbm(3, 6, 3), SW = T / 4;
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const lx = (x % SW) / SW; // dynamite sticks: cylinders with a highlight and dark seams
    let v = 120 + Math.cos((lx - 0.38) * Math.PI) * 26 - 50 * (1 - sstep(0, 0.07, Math.min(lx, 1 - lx))) + n(x, y) * 6;
    if (y > T * 0.36 && y < T * 0.64) { // paper band
      const by = (y - T * 0.36) / (T * 0.28);
      v = 205 + n(x, y) * 8 - 46 * (1 - sstep(0, 0.08, Math.min(by, 1 - by)));
    }
    return v;
  });
  // stencilled lettering hint inside the band
  const L = (pts: number[][]): void => {
    for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
      const [x0, y0] = pts[i], [x1, y1] = pts[i + 1], d = Math.hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0);
      for (let s = 0; s <= d; s += 0.7) dab(f, x0 + (x1 - x0) * s / d, y0 + (y1 - y0) * s / d, 1.9, 1.9, 0, () => 60, 0.5);
    }
  };
  const m = T / 2, yt = T * 0.43, yb = T * 0.57;
  L([[m - 34, yt], [m - 20, yt]]); L([[m - 27, yt], [m - 27, yb]]);
  L([[m - 8, yb], [m - 8, yt], [m + 8, yb], [m + 8, yt]]);
  L([[m + 20, yt], [m + 34, yt]]); L([[m + 27, yt], [m + 27, yb]]);
  return f;
}
function cobble(): Field {
  const f = field(), v = voronoi(9, 0.85), n = fbm(4, 4, 3), tone = v.sx.map(() => (rnd() - 0.5) * 24);
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const k = y * T + x, s = v.id[k], e = v.d2[k] - v.d1[k];
    const dx = wd(x, v.sx[s]), dy = wd(y, v.sy[s]), R = T / 3;
    // rounded stones: dome shading toward the top-left, dark mortar gaps
    const dome = (-dx - dy) / R * 16 + (1 - Math.min(1, Math.hypot(dx, dy) / R)) * 10;
    return 128 + tone[s] + dome + n(x, y) * 10 - 58 * (1 - sstep(0.5, 4.5, e));
  });
  soften(f); normalize(f); return f;
}
function roof(): Field {
  const f = field(), n = fbm(3, 6, 3), RH = T / 4, SW = T / 4;
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const row = Math.floor(y / RH), ly = y - row * RH, off = row % 2 ? SW / 2 : 0;
    const lx = wrap(x + off) % SW, cxo = lx - SW / 2;
    // shingles get lighter toward their exposed lower edge, which is rounded and casts a shadow below
    const edge = RH - 3 - (cxo * cxo) / (SW * 1.6);
    let v = 112 + (ly / RH) * 34 + n(x, y) * 10;
    v -= 48 * (1 - sstep(0, 1.4, Math.min(lx, SW - lx)));       // gaps between shingles
    if (ly > edge) v = 86 - (ly - edge) * 4 + n(x, y) * 6;      // shadow of the row's rounded edge
    v -= 26 * (1 - sstep(0, 4, ly));                              // tucked under the row above
    return v;
  });
  soften(f); return f;
}
function metal(): Field {
  const f = field(), brush = fbm(1, 24, 3);
  each(f, (x, y) => {
    const e = Math.min(x, y, T - 1 - x, T - 1 - y);
    let v = 136 - y * 0.12 + brush(x, y) * 8;
    // bevelled panel edge: lit along the top and left, shadowed along the bottom and right
    v += (1 - sstep(0, 5, e)) * (e === x || e === y ? 34 : -40);
    return v;
  });
  for (const [rx, ry] of [[10, 10], [T - 10, 10], [10, T - 10], [T - 10, T - 10], [T / 2, 10], [T / 2, T - 10]]) {
    dab(f, rx + 1, ry + 1.2, 3.6, 3.6, 0, (_x, _y, cur) => cur - 30, 0.5);
    dab(f, rx, ry, 3.0, 3.0, 0, (lx, ly) => 180 - (lx + ly) * 40, 0.25);
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { // scratches
    const x0 = rnd() * T, y0 = rnd() * T, a = rnd() * Math.PI, l = 6 + rnd() * 14;
    for (let s = 0; s < l; s += 0.6) dab(f, x0 + Math.cos(a) * s, y0 + Math.sin(a) * s, 0.6, 0.6, 0, (_x, _y, cur) => cur + 22, 0.4, 0.6);
  }
  return f;
}
function plain(): Field { const f = field(); addNoise(f, fbm(3, 3, 3), 8); addNoise(f, fbm(12, 12, 2), 4); normalize(f); return f; }

export function makeDetailAtlas(): THREE.DataTexture {
  seed = 7;
  const tiles: Field[] = [];
  tiles[TILE.GRASS] = grass(); tiles[TILE.DIRT] = dirt(); tiles[TILE.STONE] = stone(); tiles[TILE.SAND] = sand();
  tiles[TILE.BARK] = bark(); tiles[TILE.RINGS] = rings(); tiles[TILE.LEAVES] = leaves(); tiles[TILE.PLANKS] = planks();
  tiles[TILE.BRICK] = bricks(); tiles[TILE.ORE] = ore(tiles[TILE.STONE]); tiles[TILE.CRATE] = crate(); tiles[TILE.TNT] = tnt();
  tiles[TILE.COBBLE] = cobble(); tiles[TILE.ROOF] = roof(); tiles[TILE.METAL] = metal(); tiles[TILE.PLAIN] = plain();
  // per-material contrast around neutral: a block face is only ~40 px on screen at the default zoom,
  // so the big shapes (courses, boards, shingles) need to be bold to survive; fine dabs need less
  const CONTRAST = [1.25, 1.3, 1.2, 1.2, 1.2, 1.2, 1.0, 1.35, 1.6, 1.1, 1.2, 1.0, 1.15, 1.35, 1.1, 1.0];
  tiles.forEach((f, t) => { for (let i = 0; i < f.length; i++) f[i] = 128 + (f[i] - 128) * CONTRAST[t]; });
  const S = DETAIL_SIZE, c = canvas(S, S), ctx = get2d(c), img = ctx.createImageData(S, S), d = img.data;
  for (let t = 0; t < 16; t++) {
    const ox = (t % 4) * DETAIL_CELL, oy = Math.floor(t / 4) * DETAIL_CELL, f = tiles[t];
    // the tile plus its wrapped gutter
    for (let y = 0; y < DETAIL_CELL; y++) for (let x = 0; x < DETAIL_CELL; x++) {
      const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, f[wrap(y - DETAIL_GUTTER) * T + wrap(x - DETAIL_GUTTER)]));
      const k = ((oy + y) * S + ox + x) * 4;
      d[k] = d[k + 1] = d[k + 2] = v; d[k + 3] = 255;
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  // raw data (not sRGB), canvas row 0 at v = 0 like before; the short chain caps the blur at MAX_LOD
  const tex = bake(c, { srgb: false, bleed: 0, levels: MAX_LOD + 1, anisotropy: 8 });
  return tex;
}
```

```
const EXPECTED_RESPONSE_TYPES = new Set(['basic', 'cors', 'default']);

async function __wbg_load(module, imports) {
    if (typeof Response === 'function' && module instanceof Response) {
        if (typeof WebAssembly.instantiateStreaming === 'function') {
            try {
                return await WebAssembly.instantiateStreaming(module, imports);

            } catch (e) {
                const validResponse = module.ok && EXPECTED_RESPONSE_TYPES.has(module.type);

                if (validResponse && module.headers.get('Content-Type') !== 'application/wasm') {
                    console.warn("`WebAssembly.instantiateStreaming` failed because your server does not serve Wasm with `application/wasm` MIME type. Falling back to `WebAssembly.instantiate` which is slower. Original error:\n", e);

                } else {
                    throw e;
                }
            }
        }

        const bytes = await module.arrayBuffer();
        return await WebAssembly.instantiate(bytes, imports);

    } else {
        const instance = await WebAssembly.instantiate(module, imports);

        if (instance instanceof WebAssembly.Instance) {
            return { instance, module };

        } else {
            return instance;
        }
    }
}

function __wbg_get_imports() {
    const imports = {};
    imports.wbg = {};
    imports.wbg.__wbg_wbindgenthrow_451ec1a8469d7eb6 = function(arg0, arg1) {
        throw new Error(getStringFromWasm0(arg0, arg1));
    };
    imports.wbg.__wbindgen_init_externref_table = function() {
        const table = wasm.__wbindgen_export_1;
        const offset = table.grow(4);
        table.set(0, undefined);
        table.set(offset + 0, undefined);
        table.set(offset + 1, null);
        table.set(offset + 2, true);
        table.set(offset + 3, false);
        ;
    };

    return imports;
}

function __wbg_init_memory(imports, memory) {
    imports.wbg.memory = memory || new WebAssembly.Memory({initial:18,maximum:32768,shared:true});
}

function __wbg_finalize_init(instance, module, thread_stack_size) {
    wasm = instance.exports;
    __wbg_init.__wbindgen_wasm_module = module;
    cachedFloat32ArrayMemory0 = null;
    cachedUint8ArrayMemory0 = null;

    if (typeof thread_stack_size !== 'undefined' && (typeof thread_stack_size !== 'number' || thread_stack_size === 0 || thread_stack_size % 65536 !== 0)) { throw 'invalid stack size' }
    wasm.__wbindgen_start(thread_stack_size);
    return wasm;
}

function initSync(module, memory) {
    if (wasm !== undefined) return wasm;

    let thread_stack_size
    if (typeof module !== 'undefined') {
        if (Object.getPrototypeOf(module) === Object.prototype) {
            ({module, memory, thread_stack_size} = module)
        } else {
            console.warn('using deprecated parameters for `initSync()`; pass a single object instead')
        }
    }

    const imports = __wbg_get_imports();

    __wbg_init_memory(imports, memory);

    if (!(module instanceof WebAssembly.Module)) {
        module = new WebAssembly.Module(module);
    }

    const instance = new WebAssembly.Instance(module, imports);

    return __wbg_finalize_init(instance, module, thread_stack_size);
}

async function __wbg_init(module_or_path, memory) {
    if (wasm !== undefined) return wasm;

    let thread_stack_size
    if (typeof module_or_path !== 'undefined') {
        if (Object.getPrototypeOf(module_or_path) === Object.prototype) {
            ({module_or_path, memory, thread_stack_size} = module_or_path)
        } else {
            console.warn('using deprecated parameters for the initialization function; pass a single object instead')
        }
    }

    if (typeof module_or_path === 'undefined') {
        module_or_path = new URL('voxel_core_bg.wasm', import.meta.url);
    }
    const imports = __wbg_get_imports();

    if (typeof module_or_path === 'string' || (typeof Request === 'function' && module_or_path instanceof Request) || (typeof URL === 'function' && module_or_path instanceof URL)) {
        module_or_path = fetch(module_or_path);
    }

    __wbg_init_memory(imports, memory);

    const { instance, module } = await __wbg_load(await module_or_path, imports);

    return __wbg_finalize_init(instance, module, thread_stack_size);
}

export { initSync };
export default __wbg_init;
```
